Also stamps fresh device presses and accumulates look-mouse deltas.
- **Capture a new binding** (action, `bindings.cpp:577`): Suspend gameplay input while assigning, clearing or restoring a device slot. Cancel on focus loss or timeout.
- **Can HUD layout editing run?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:2899`): Suppress its default F7 action when photo mode or the surface map is open. Require allowed input to start editing.
  - in EditTick: HudEdit pressed (not on the local map or photo mode)?: Called by vehicleflight each frame. On the F7 map or in photo mode the key is drained for routeedit. Edit on: Page Up/Down pick, arrows move (repeat 40 ms after 350 ms), +/- size 5%, Delete reset, Home/End spacing; off saves hoverui.ini with a notice.
  - in EditTick: ShowHints (J) toggles every hint and panel: Flips show_all, saves hoverui.ini, notice 'Contextual hints shown' / 'Hints: only what you need now'.
- **Adjust or save HUD layout** (action, `hoverui.cpp:2933`): Process selection, offset and scale controls. Toggling layout editing off saves hoverui.ini.
  - in EditTick: HudEdit pressed (not on the local map or photo mode)?: Called by vehicleflight each frame. On the F7 map or in photo mode the key is drained for routeedit. Edit on: Page Up/Down pick, arrows move (repeat 40 ms after 350 ms), +/- size 5%, Delete reset, Home/End spacing; off saves hoverui.ini with a notice.
  - in EditTick: ShowHints (J) toggles every hint and panel: Flips show_all, saves hoverui.ini, notice 'Contextual hints shown' / 'Hints: only what you need now'.

Arrows: sample → allowed (Each binding tick); sample → capture (Remap requested); capture → quiet (Capture owns input); allowed → quiet (No); allowed → gestures (Yes); gestures → publish (Started and triggered); gestures → claim (Available bindings); resolve → editgate (Inspect native result); editgate → block (Editor blocks); editgate → preserve (Editor allows); editgate → native (Ordinary button event); claim → native (Block request); native → block (FVO owns press); native → preserve (Unclaimed or pre-blocked); publish → hud (HUD edit command); hud → layout (Eligible); hud → quiet (Suppressed)

## Scanner destinations, HUD and vehicle camera  `#scanner-hud-camera`  (Interface and authoring)

The scanner combines native selection and ray evidence and captures aim when a command starts. The HUD and camera hooks consume published presentation state.

- **Player and scanner state** (state, `scantarget.cpp:505`): In a live gameplay update, service the existing route and chain. Read the player's aim while the scanner is open.
  - in Tick: Scanner marker and T-key gestures: Scanner open: ray distance and T sets the map marker, chaining the own vehicle (summon / follow / Auto to marker). targetinput: tap selects, double tap clears, hold arms a ram; targetsession keeps the marker identity. · log `input.key`
- **Fresh physical hit?** (decision, `scantarget.cpp:211`): Use the game camera ray or fall back to body heading. Check hit freshness and finite evidence.
- **Use the physical surface** (action, `scantarget.cpp:235`): Record position and hit form. A named native selection can take priority during normal navigation.
- **Use map context or estimate** (action, `scantarget.cpp:240`): When eligible, estimate along the ray or use the native named selection and potential map point. Placement look uses physical surface evidence only.
- **Capture aim at command start** (action, `scantarget.cpp:525`): Record point, actor, focus form and heading on Started. Replay the saved aim on Triggered.
  - in Tick: Scanner marker and T-key gestures: Scanner open: ray distance and T sets the map marker, chaining the own vehicle (summon / follow / Auto to marker). targetinput: tap selects, double tap clears, hold arms a ram; targetsession keeps the marker identity. · log `input.key`
- **Which command fired?** (decision, `scantarget.cpp:538`): Scanner selection requires the open scanner. Dispatch guard, focus and recording by context. Driving-only commands toggle Auto or Cruise.
  - in Tick: Scanner marker and T-key gestures: Scanner open: ray distance and T sets the map marker, chaining the own vehicle (summon / follow / Auto to marker). targetinput: tap selects, double tap clears, hold arms a ram; targetsession keeps the marker identity. · log `input.key`
- **Start or extend a trip?** (decision, `scantarget.cpp:453`): An invalid point reports no target. A continuing trip queues the point. Otherwise set the marker, and request travel if the marker was placed.
- **Service active and queued destinations** (action, `scantarget.cpp:380`): Drop stale itinerary on external marker replacement. Process skip, retry or clear. Automatically advance near a matching active marker and its height, provided travel is reachable.
- **Handle guard, focus or recording** (action, `scantarget.cpp:476`): Guard may focus an eligible actor or vehicle, set a guard point on foot or replace a driven trip. Recording commands use the saved aim, with exact valid aim required for a summon point.
- **Toggle Auto or Cruise** (action, `scantarget.cpp:561`): Toggle according to the driving command. Starting Cruise clears the scanner itinerary.
  - in Tick: Scanner marker and T-key gestures: Scanner open: ray distance and T sets the map marker, chaining the own vehicle (summon / follow / Auto to marker). targetinput: tap selects, double tap clears, hold arms a ram; targetsession keeps the marker identity. · log `input.key`
- **Is the presentation movie usable?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:946`): HUD and scanner use separate movie state. Rebuild stale objects after a movie change and check available state and visibility.
  - in Work: HUD movie Advance hook runs FVO HUD work: Vtable patches: HUDMenu update, MovieImpl Advance and destructor (without it no native buttons: a stale menu would crash the next load), MonocleMenu message. All Scaleform work runs inside the movie's own Advance. An exception disables the HUD until the next load.
  - in Work: HUD frame drawn: End of the HUD movie's Advance work.
- **Render contextual information** (action, `hoverui.cpp:2404`): Render contextual hints and route text. The scanner captures the native destination and displays target text when its screen location is available.
  - in MonoWorkBody: Scanner (MonocleMenu) open in the last 300 ms?: Shows the call/follow buttons, status box (SUMMONING / FOLLOWING), Target text and the scanner copy of the route panel; captures the selected map marker's name and distance (scannercontext::RangeMeters) for 200 ms.
- **Is physical attitude available?** (decision, `vehiclecamera.cpp:91`): Require a vehicle sample stamped within 500 ms, physical camera enabled, native vehicle view and finite pitch and roll.
  - in ReadAttitude: Fresh (<= 500 ms), physical with you at the wheel, vehicle view?: Also read by hoverui's attitude instrument.
- **Apply filtered vehicle bank** (action, `vehiclecamera.cpp:64`): Run the native camera conversion first. Apply filtered roll per view and reset filters when the sample generation changes. The HUD can read the same live attitude.
  - in Apply: Out: camera done: 
- **Keep native camera output** (state, `vehiclecamera.cpp:73`): Clear the filter if the physical attitude or hooks are unavailable. The HUD may show an attitude sample while editing its layout.
  - in Apply: Out: camera done: 

Arrows: tick → aim (Scanner open); tick → chain (Existing route); tick → snapshot (Action starts, with or without scanner target); tick → hud (Publish target hints); aim → exact (Fresh hit); aim → estimate (No fresh hit); exact → estimate (Named target takes priority); exact → snapshot (Use surface); estimate → snapshot (Use available selection); snapshot → dispatch (Trigger arrives); dispatch → route (Scanner tap); dispatch → guard (Hold, focus or authoring); dispatch → cruise (Driving Auto or Cruise); route → chain (New marker or queued stop); chain → hud (Publish route status); hud → draw (Live movie); camera → bank (Available); camera → base (Unavailable); bank → draw (HUD reads the same attitude sample)

## UI widgets, settings and installer choices  `#ui-widgets-settings`  (Interface and authoring)

The DLL builds its HUD and route editor inside Starfield’s Scaleform movies. The FOMOD configures install-time choices through INI files, and the in-game settings pages update the same gameplay settings.

- **Game movie advances** (state, `hoverui.cpp:2585`): Dispatch HUD, scanner, settings and route editor work to the matching movie. Movie ownership is tracked separately, and the destructor clears references tied to the destroyed movie.
- **Can the native HUD widget be extended?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:647`): Require an object root and the native RightMeters / HUDVehicle widget. Lazily allocate native BasicButtons inside the vehicle widget.
- **Refresh active HUD controls** (action, `hoverui.cpp:679`): Render vehicle prompts and gear. Gate displays by use of controller or keyboard, hover state, contextual hints, first rides and layout editing.
- **Refresh contextual cards and hints** (action, `hoverui.cpp:2029`): Read the latest HUD state for rover ownership, occupied vehicles, Auto, health and plan. Build from native menu objects and current binding presentation.
  - in RefreshExtras: Status card on? fill heading, health, trip, escort lines: 8 card lines (foot and car layouts); the plan ledger line fills the last empty line in test builds. Empty card when features StatusCard is off (always on now) unless editing. Also the vehicle bar under the enemy bar.
- **Can the vehicle health bar build?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:2331`): Try the game enemy-meter template at intervals. Update the bar on success. After repeated failure retain the status-card health line.
- **Lay out visible content** (action, `hoverui.cpp:2483`): Arrange eligible elements and declutter overlapping mod text around existing HUD clips. Layout editing shows the real saved positions.
- **Build route and photo overlay sprites** (action, `routeedit.cpp:2025`): Create a named layer and native AS3 sprites for routes, volumes, panel, pointer and text. Map visuals (diagnostic core 53D2FDBC, installed, in-game verification pending): the shown route draws its road footprint without the Lane volumes switch (routeedit.cpp DrawRouteFacts / DrawRoadTiles; routes without road tiles show lane corridor x lanes), floor tinted by travel (hover blue, drive green, both violet), lane direction arrows and L1/L2 numbers, solids-allowed hatch, per-tile tags (travel, want speed, solids, box, join, divider, stop/yield/signal), line end (stop bar, arrive ring), corner radius beside points, a summary line and legend; dock-selected tiles draw their real field box (docklayout.inl DockDrawTileBox, tilebox.h geometry). The amber edge display limits its inward offset through tight bends. The tile panel exposes the edge widths, Readme identifies the current screen, and Help explains fields by screen. Source: roadvisual.h, trafficroute.h:39, docklayout.inl:792 and notes/MAP_VISUALS_AUDIT_20261119.md. Keep the pointer topmost and fall back to the lines layer if the flow sprite is unavailable.
- **Which settings page is ready?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:1361`): Wait for visible native menu panels and data. Merge FVO options into Gameplay Options. Controls rows use the native Controls list when its hooks are installed.
  - in SettingsWorkBody: Gameplay Options: FVO checkboxes and control rows: Runs on the settings movie thread while SettingsPanel is visible: our feature checkboxes (features::InMenu) and one control row per action/device appended when generations change; selecting a control starts BeginCapture. Hiding the panel cancels the capture.
- **Merge FVO gameplay options** (action, `hoverui.cpp:1411`): Populate feature checkboxes and the attached addon's Reload Traffic link. Preserve live game and other-mod rows. If native options have not arrived, postpone replacing the list.
- **Native Controls page or link fallback?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:1283`): Use the native Controls list when hooks are installed and the panel is visible. When the fallback link hook is available, offer binding rows through Gameplay Options.
- **Does FVO own the setting event?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:1487`): Owned checkbox or link events set features, reload routes or start capture. Other events pass to native handlers. Settings reset delegates to native after appropriate FVO resets.
- **Write gameplay settings** (action, `features.cpp:124`): Persist supported toggles in settings.ini and update their in-memory generation for UI refresh. Native FVO Controls validates and saves bindings by device and slot.
- **Check installer profile on first reload** (state, `features.cpp:72`): The FOMOD writes mode and selected feature fragments under Data/SFSE/Plugins/FlyingVehicleOverhaul.features. Core reads them when first loading gameplay settings.
- **Is the installer profile enabled, valid and changed?** (decision, `featureinstaller.h:25`): Keep mode or a missing profile leaves preferences in place. Check complete enabled choices and compare them with the last applied profile.
  - in Apply: Installer fragment present and changed? import once: First Reload only: Data\SFSE\Plugins\FlyingVehicleOverhaul.features\mode.ini enabled=1; per-feature <section>.<key>.ini; only choices that differ from the .fomod-applied ledger are written; settings.ini backed up once to .before-fomod. A missing page file means no choice.
  - in Apply: Installer choices (Data\SFSE\Plugins\...features) changed?: Checked once per session. mode.ini [installer] enabled=1: per-key fragment files are imported into settings.ini only when the chosen value changed (ledger); a page not shown installs nothing. Failure keeps existing settings.
- **Import changed installer choices** (action, `featureinstaller.h:51`): Back up existing settings on first application, stage and replace settings and the applied-choice ledger. Only changed installer choices are written.
  - in Apply: Installer fragment present and changed? import once: First Reload only: Data\SFSE\Plugins\FlyingVehicleOverhaul.features\mode.ini enabled=1; per-feature <section>.<key>.ini; only choices that differ from the .fomod-applied ledger are written; settings.ini backed up once to .before-fomod. A missing page file means no choice.
  - in Apply: Installer choices (Data\SFSE\Plugins\...features) changed?: Checked once per session. mode.ini [installer] enabled=1: per-key fragment files are imported into settings.ini only when the chosen value changed (ledger); a page not shown installs nothing. Failure keeps existing settings.

Arrows: movie → widget (Vehicle HUD); movie → extras (Live HUD context); movie → draw (Photo or surface-map host); movie → pages (Main or pause settings); widget → refresh (Native widget available); widget → extras (Base widget unavailable, contextual retry independent); refresh → layout (Refresh controls); extras → bar (Health presentation); extras → layout (Display contextual elements); bar → layout (Bar or card line); pages → merge (Gameplay options); pages → controls (Controls list); merge → events (Feature or link activated); controls → events (Fallback link activated); controls → prefs (Native binding applied); events → prefs (FVO feature changed); events → pages (Native event or refresh another frame); installer → validate (Initial feature reload); validate → import (Enabled, valid and changed); validate → prefs (Keep existing settings); import → pages (Read settings when options open)

## Diagnostics, crash reporting and freeze capture  `#diagnostics-watchdog`  (Settings and diagnostics)

The core initializes a buffered log and crash writer. Diagnostic builds also sample resources, watch frame heartbeats, report recovery and capture qualifying stalls or long hangs.

- **Initialize crash recording** (state, `crash.cpp:791`): Initialize a process dump worker when DbgHelp, events and the writer are available. Install the top-level exception filter for both build flavors.
  - in Install: Install crash logger, dump thread and freeze watch: Always: SetUnhandledExceptionFilter, dbghelp dump thread, atexit clean-exit record. Test builds only (#ifndef FVO_RELEASE): previous-run report, run-state.txt, FreezeWatch thread, vectored first-chance handler. [diagnostics] hangDumpSeconds 45 (6..600). · log `OBS system=run event=previous_run`
- **Diagnostic build?** (decision, `crash.cpp:813`): Diagnostic builds arm the freeze watcher and first-chance exception handler. Published builds retain important logging and unhandled crash capture.
- **Which exception was observed?** (decision, `crash.cpp:295`): Skip expected or nonfatal first-chance events. Diagnostic mode can capture the first qualifying allocator access violation and report bounded first-chance observations.
  - in Handler: Fatal exception: CRASH lines, registers, crumbs, last-crash.dmp: Vectored handler (test builds) and the unhandled filter (all builds). Faults flagged by SetExpectedFaults are ignored. Prints module+offset, registers, breadcrumbs, register objects matched to recent FVO actions, unwound stack.
- **Record unhandled crash** (action, `crash.cpp:495`): Set run state to crashed, reset first-chance reporting limits and report the final exception with context and prior filter chain.
- **Ask the dedicated dump writer** (action, `crash.cpp:260`): Copy exception context into stable storage, signal the worker and wait for completion. Serialize DbgHelp use and report a busy, missing or failed writer.
- **Continue native exception handling** (state, `crash.cpp:483`): First-chance reporting returns continue-search. The unhandled path hands back to the prior filter when present.
- **Publish update heartbeat** (state, `crash.cpp:784`): Record time and thread ID from the core frame path. Track recovery when updates resume after a gap.
  - in Beat: crash::Beat: heartbeat for the freeze watch: Every non-nested Frame, paused or not, so a menu is not read as a hang. A gap of 3 s or more is recorded as a recovered stall. · log `OBS system=freeze.stall`
- **Does this hitch qualify for a stack dump?** (decision, `stallcapture.h:9`): Require a heartbeat at least three seconds old, gameplay, foreground, available quota and a retry window. Up to three successes per process and at most one per heartbeat.
  - in Check: Stall: gap >=3 s, foreground, gameplay, <3 captures?: Gates: no_heartbeat, below_threshold, background, not_gameplay, quota (3), already_captured, retry_backoff (1 s), attempts_exhausted (3). Watch line every 5 s or on a gate change. · log `OBS system=freeze.stall`
- **Capture hitch context** (action, `crash.cpp:735`): Write a small stack and module dump. Track attempts, log lock ownership where available and retain a bounded dated copy on success.
- **Did the heartbeat exceed the hang threshold?** (decision, `crash.cpp:753`): Long hangs have a separate check and may include loading or background gaps. Default 45 seconds, setting bounded to 6–600 seconds. Allow up to three spaced attempts, stopping after success.
- **Capture all-thread hang dump** (action, `crash.cpp:753`): Collect lock and action diagnostics on the first attempt. Save last-hang.dmp and a dated latest copy if the write succeeds.
  - in FreezeWatch: 6 s without update: log the beat thread's stack candidates: Suspends the last update thread, copies its stack, prints game and FVO return addresses; 12 lines per run, WriteUnlocked (never waits for the log lock).
- **Update run status and read recovery** (action, `crash.cpp:548`): Persist run state and heartbeat age. The next diagnostic startup reports clean, crash or killed/frozen. Never infer a particular cause from a leftover running marker.
- **Preserve and buffer diagnostic output** (action, `log.cpp:41`): Rotate the previous log or use a PID-specific fallback. Routine entries carry sequence, tick, PID, TID and epoch. Important and exception entries can bypass the regular lock.
  - in Init: Open FlyingVehicleOverhaul.log (old one kept as .prev.log): Moves the previous log to <Name>.prev.log. If the old file is still held by a running game, this run writes <Name>.<pid>.log instead of truncating the live one (2026-11-07 fix). · log `OBS system=log event=own_file`
- **Collect diagnostic resource sample?** (decision, `resources.cpp:31`): If verbose and due, sample memory, process I/O and GPU usage and budgets. Disable further GPU queries after a slow post-initialization diagnostic sample.
  - in Tick: Reports: scheduler 10 s, probe cost, resources, pacing: Scheduler counts every 10 s; vehicle_probe::ReportUpdateCost (hookcost samples); resources::Tick RAM/IO/GPU only with verbose log, every 2 s; framepacing window every 10 s.
- **Write diagnostic telemetry** (action, `telemetry.cpp:84`): A diagnostic writer queues time- and thread-stamped JSONL observations. Published builds do not open that file.
  - in Init: Telemetry clock + jsonl writer thread (test builds): Starts the clock; in FVO_RELEASE returns before opening a file. Test builds write Logs\SeamlessPlanets\vehicle-telemetry-<time>.jsonl from a background thread.

Arrows: log → boot (Core initializes log before crash recorder); boot → build (Determine instrumentation); boot → fatal (Unhandled path armed); build → fault (First-chance diagnostic hook); build → beat (Diagnostic watcher armed); build → telemetry (Diagnostic telemetry); fault → dump (First qualifying allocator fault); fault → yield (Ordinary first-chance event); fatal → dump (Unhandled report reaches capture); fatal → statefile (Crash state); dump → yield (Return handler result); beat → stall (Watch heartbeat and foreground); stall → stack (Eligible); stall → hang (Check separate long-hang policy); stack → hang (Check long-hang policy); stack → log (Write hitch record); hang → allthreads (Eligible); hang → statefile (Periodic run-state refresh); allthreads → log (Write hang result); log → metrics (Verbose resource probe)

## Core save state and settings  `#core-save-settings`  (Settings and diagnostics)

The core hooks native saves and loads. Flight persistence can restore the player’s previous manual hover through the regular start path. Persisted features and legacy tuning files use separate readers.

- **Native SaveGame detour** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:133`): Handle core hover first. With the addon attached, also invoke the traffic snapshot preparation. Then invoke native save and finish the traffic save on scope exit.
  - in SaveGameDetour: SaveGameDetour: record per-save state around the native save: flightsave::OnSave(save name) always; BeforeTrafficSave before the original; AfterTrafficSave from a scope guard after it.
- **Is this the player's own manual hover?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7162`): Require the player driven vehicle, active hover, no autopilot, no passenger ride and the current hover vehicle matching the driver.
- **Can the save name be resolved?** (decision, `flightsave.cpp:42`): Derive a consistent filesystem key from the native save name. If empty or storage unavailable, skip the hover sidecar operation.
  - in OnSave: Save or load event (game hooks): SaveGame/LoadGame hooks in main_vehicle.cpp (area A), installed only with the Traffic addon.
  - in OnSave: Save: hovering by hand? Write the record, else delete an old one: FlightStates\<key>.txt next to FVO settings: 'FVO saved hover v1', vehicle, z, gear. Key = save name lower-case without .sfs.
- **Write current hover sidecar** (action, `flightsave.cpp:57`): Write a versioned FlightStates record with vehicle ID, height and gear. The saved format omits velocity and attitude.
  - in OnSave: Save or load event (game hooks): SaveGame/LoadGame hooks in main_vehicle.cpp (area A), installed only with the Traffic addon.
  - in OnSave: Save: hovering by hand? Write the record, else delete an old one: FlightStates\<key>.txt next to FVO settings: 'FVO saved hover v1', vehicle, z, gear. Key = save name lower-case without .sfs.
- **Delete obsolete hover entry** (action, `flightsave.cpp:51`): When saving at the same name with manual hover off, remove any previous hover record for that slot.
  - in OnSave: Save or load event (game hooks): SaveGame/LoadGame hooks in main_vehicle.cpp (area A), installed only with the Traffic addon.
  - in OnSave: Save: hovering by hand? Write the record, else delete an old one: FlightStates\<key>.txt next to FVO settings: 'FVO saved hover v1', vehicle, z, gear. Key = save name lower-case without .sfs.
- **Native LoadGame detour** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:100`): Close lifecycle work for the old world, begin traffic transition, read the core hover request and gate mutations until the engine load returns.
  - in LoadGameDetour: LoadGameDetour: close requests, drop old world, run the load: Runs on the load's background thread. Before the original: lifecycle::Close, trafficprofile::BeginLoad, trafficarrival::Reset, flightsave::OnLoad(save name), vehiclediag::NoteBeforeLoad, g_dropAll = true. After: lifecycle::Open, g_loadDone. 2026-10-28 crashes: nothing that changes the world runs mid-load.
- **Does the save have a valid hover record?** (decision, `flightsave.cpp:65`): Reset the previous request, then load and parse the named sidecar. An absent or invalid record produces no request.
  - in OnLoad: Save or load event (game hooks): SaveGame/LoadGame hooks in main_vehicle.cpp (area A), installed only with the Traffic addon.
  - in OnLoad: Load: read this save's record as a pending request: Forgets the old world's hover; unreadable or missing record = load as before.
- **Can the player restore hover now?** (decision, `flightsavelogic.h:57`): When current hover is off, wait for seating up to 15 seconds from the first request check. Require the same vehicle and loaded height within 5 metres.
  - in Decide: Same vehicle at its wheel within 5 m height, within 15 s?: Wait / Restore / Expired / OtherVehicle / Moved; answered once.
- **Resume normal manual hover** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:7659`): Restore gear and invoke vehicleflight::Start through the usual manual hover path.
  - in PlayerTick: Hover off: does a loaded save ask for the hover back?: Asked every frame while hover is off until answered. Restore: g.gear = saved gear, then Start(driven) through the same on path as the hover key.
  - in PlayerTick: Hover key pressed while driving (500 ms guard)?: The only manual hover on/off. A second toggle within 500 ms is ignored (L3 double edges). On: Start(vehicle) (lift-off). Off: Stop(key label).
  - in PlayerTick: Hover off: manual wheels, the gear caps ground speed: BindBody, groundOn=true, groundFrac = {1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1}[gear] of a top speed learned in gear 4 with W held (no boost). The game's own controls drive; the physics hook still applies the gear's grip and corner forces.
- **Continue through normal vehicle state** (state, `flightsave.cpp:83`): Wait while eligible seating may return. Finish the pending request on expiry, another vehicle or a moved vehicle, with the recorded reason.
  - in TakeRestore: Out: restore answered (PlayerTick starts the hover): 
- **Resolve Documents storage** (action, `fvopaths.h:12`): Use the Windows Documents known folder. When needed, migrate the legacy SeamlessVehicle folder and ini name. A failed folder move can retain the older location.
- **Which feature value takes effect?** (decision, `features.cpp:111`): Unavailable features are off. Always-on features remain enabled, and Physical AI follows Physical VTOL. Other features read changed settings or fall back to compiled defaults.
  - in On: settings.ini feature switches (reloaded every 5 s): Defaults: NPC vehicles on, traffic on, route markers on, wall pass on, radar on, flight assist on; free roll, physical VTOL, physical AI, ground planner, verbose log off. Set() saves at once. Read from any thread via cached atomics.
- **Load legacy flight tuning** (action, `flighttune.cpp:108`): Core setup calls Load, which reads bounded flight.ini values from Documents/My Games/Starfield/SFSE/Logs/SeamlessPlanets. Apply and Tick have no fvo-main callers, so runtime use of these overrides was not shown.
- **Keep camera tune entry points indexed** (state, `camtune.cpp:106`): The module defines camera.ini under the legacy Documents/My Games/Starfield/SFSE/Logs/SeamlessPlanets path. In the inspected current source only EyeOffset has an external caller, shipmodel.cpp:480.

Arrows: sample → save (Publish current state before saves); save → file (Handle hover sidecar); file → write (Usable key and hover recorded); file → clear (Usable key and hover off); file → skip (Missing key or configuration path); load → read (Check named record); read → restore (Valid record); read → skip (Absent or invalid); restore → start (Same seat and height); restore → skip (Wait, expired, wrong or moved); path → file (Documents location); path → read (Documents location); path → features (settings.ini)

## Native guards for map models, dust and actor disable  `#native-patch-guards`  (Settings and diagnostics)

The core validates hook entry points and preserves the native path for ordinary requests. Map-model capping, dust creation filtering and premature actor disable each have their own gate.

- **Install standalone core guards** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:477`): Ask mapguard and disableguard to validate their entry points as part of core setup.
  - in InstallFeatureSystems: InstallFeatureSystems: every feature hook, once: world::SetServices (vehicle list callbacks), then hoverui, routeedit, mapguard, disableguard, inputgate, mappoints, loadedarea, npcvehicles, profiles, vehicleaim, vehiclediag, vehiclehits enemy bar, flighttune::Load. Each Install checks its own bytes and logs on mismatch; none can stop the service.
- **Does the native entry match?** (decision, `hook.cpp:88`): Require exact expected bytes for the prologue or the expected target for an inline call. The local-map task uses an expected vtable entry.
  - in Install: hook::Install / RedirectCall: patch only if bytes match: Install: compares the expected prologue, copies it into a 64-byte trampoline, writes a 14-byte absolute jmp and NOPs the rest. RedirectCall: rewrites one E8 call through a near relay page after checking the old destination. A mismatch changes nothing and logs.
  - in Install: Detour returns to the game: Every detour calls the original through its trampoline (except the refused disable and the dust create).
- **Install the matched adapter** (action, `hook.cpp:99`): Preserve the original call through a trampoline or direct original pointer. Allocate relay and arrange memory permissions where required.
  - in Install: hook::Install / RedirectCall: patch only if bytes match: Install: compares the expected prologue, copies it into a 64-byte trampoline, writes a 14-byte absolute jmp and NOPs the rest. RedirectCall: rewrites one E8 call through a near relay page after checking the old destination. A mismatch changes nothing and logs.
  - in Install: Detour returns to the game: Every detour calls the original through its trampoline (except the refused disable and the dust create).
- **Leave a failed hook unavailable** (state, `mapguard.cpp:418`): If map-task validation fails, leave map guard unavailable. Its create-reference hook can fail independently after the model guard installed.
  - in Install: Wheel dust being created into a persistent cell?: Hook on create-reference FUN_1405A0520: REV-8 wheel-dust bases going into the persistent cell get the invalid handle (as a failed placement). Dust in ordinary cells is kept.
- **Does the map build exceed 60,000 models?** (decision, `mapguard.cpp:102`): Count the model records and publish the observed size. Above the cap, limit the original build call to the first 60,000.
  - in RunDetour: Local map model build over 60000 references?: Vtable slot patch on the build task (0x144C54E38 slot 2, FUN_14142FAE0). Over kMaxModels 60000 the list is shortened for the build and restored after (the node's child count is 16-bit; 65535 wrapped and crashed). · log `OBS system=map.trace`
- **Call original map builder** (action, `mapguard.cpp:112`): For large builds temporarily shorten the range, call the native function and restore the original end. Keep the surplus records owned by their original vector.
  - in RunDetour: Local map model build over 60000 references?: Vtable slot patch on the build task (0x144C54E38 slot 2, FUN_14142FAE0). Over kMaxModels 60000 the list is shortened for the build and restored after (the node's child count is 16-bit; 65535 wrapped and crashed). · log `OBS system=map.trace`
- **Does wheel dust qualify for creation?** (decision, `mapguard.cpp:312`): Classify known dust references. Reject out-of-range creation, and reject dust headed for the persistent cell even near the player. Unclassified refs and other eligible dust continue.
- **Return an invalid creation handle** (state, `mapguard.cpp:307`): Skip native creation when filtered. The disabled move-to-player-cell path does not run in this build.
- **Is the actor ready to disable?** (decision, `disableguard.cpp:35`): For a non-null actor, reject a missing setup pointer or a guarded read failure before invoking native Disable.
- **Skip premature actor disable** (state, `disableguard.cpp:39`): Return without running the original routine. Log only a bounded number of skipped requests.
- **Use the native operation** (action, `mapguard.cpp:329`): Allowed creation and actor disable requests delegate to the appropriate original.
- **Report persistent-cell state** (action, `mapguard.cpp:443`): Suspend the main-frame guard during loading. Read valid dust distance settings and report periodically or when map load is large. The old saved-dust cleanup has no active call here.
  - in Tick: mapguard::Tick: persistent-cell report, dust settings: Any non-Skip work. Skipped while loading. Resolves dust bases, reads [world] dustNear (120 m), reports the persistent cell every 60 s or after a build over 20000.

Arrows: install → expected (Validate each hook); expected → installed (Match and patch permitted); expected → failed (Mismatch or setup failure); installed → cap (Run local-map task); installed → dust (Create-reference callback); installed → actor (Queued actor disable); installed → report (Periodic main-frame work); cap → model (Native range or capped range); dust → deny (Filtered dust); dust → native (Allowed); actor → hold (Unready); actor → native (Ready or null as provided by native caller); cap → report (Record unusually large model build)

## A1 DLL load (SFSEPlugin_Load)  `#flow-a-s1`  (Detailed flow · A Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics)

Area A is the core DLL's spine. At DLL load, SFSEPlugin_Load opens the log, installs the crash logger, checks the runtime (1.16.244), publishes the native vehicle listener, and hooks the game's load routine and the loading-screen step. At PostDataLoad it starts the standalone service: it hooks the ship-system update (the pulse) and the vehicle physics step, the save routine and every feature hook, then adds one permanent SFSE main-thread task (Frame). Each Frame decides whether the world is safe (no save loading or settling, planet manager at state 2), drops everything after a load started, and admits a Full update, Housekeeping or Skip from the native-pulse scheduler. A Full update runs every system in a fixed order (world, hits, map and navigation, context, NPCs, flight, registry and traffic). Engine mutations go through the lifecycle request table into the game's own service queue, or the loading-screen cover. A separate freeze-watch thread (test builds only) records stalls, hangs and crashes. Next: SFSE messages and service start. Oddities found when mapping: 1) Two load paths decide the same thing: the LoadGame hook (the one that fires) and the SFSE PreLoadGame/PostLoadGame handler, which also calls Reset() directly on the SFSE thread in standalone, while the hook only sets g_dropAll so Reset runs on the main thread. 2) Three resets of the world cover one event: g_dropAll on the next frame, planet state 0/1, and PreLoadGame. Reset can run twice per load. 3) In Frame, g_worldReady is set inside the g_standalone block and then set again just after it for all modes (duplicate logic). 4) WithCore / CoreApi / CoreMessage / api_client forwarding are legacy: the SeamlessPlanets core is gone, so the standalone path is the only live one. core_api.h comments still describe SeamlessPlanets as owner of every hook. 5) Dead or idle: trace::Init is never called in this DLL, so trace.cpp writes nothing (logger::Write still calls trace::Raw, which does nothing). telemetry::Frame is never called. game::VerifySignatures and game::ReadLimits have no callers. Most of prefetch.h is a declaration-only header; api_client.cpp implements it and sends it to vehicle_probe. src/build_repro.log is a stray compiler error log (missing oncoming_repro.cpp), not code. 6) Version mismatch: SFSEPlugin_Version is 122 and the Important line says 'FVO 1.2.1 loaded'. 7) Hook ownership is spread out: area A installs load, save, reduced-step, disable-guard, map-guard (vtable slot patch + create-reference) and map-trace hooks. vehicle_probe (pulse), vehicleflight, vehiclediag, npcvehicles, vehiclehits, vehicleaim, vehiclecamera, loadedarea, inputgate, controlspage, mappoints, hoverui and routeedit install their own. The full list of hooked game functions: grep hook::Install / hook::RedirectCall. 8) Stage edges that do not keep one-in/one-out: the s3 detours and the s7 lifecycle are entered from many callers and engine events, not in sequence. The freeze watch (s8) runs on its own thread, linked only by the heartbeat. Data nodes are read across stages. 9) Not mapped here: handoff.h is a traffic seat/physics hand-off phase model and wheel mailbox (api_traffic.cpp, vehicleflight.cpp), not engine-level, so it is left to area D. overrides.h and selfreport.h are pure policy contracts used by D/C; they are only listed here as data (A.contracts). 10) Release builds (FVO_RELEASE) compile out Write/Observe, the freeze watcher, the vectored handler, run-state, telemetry and the map trace. Only the unhandled-exception crash report and Important lines stay.

- **SFSEPlugin_Load: DLL loaded by SFSE** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:565` · log `OBS system=runtime event=trace-start`): Runs once on the loader thread. Order: logger::Init, trace-start line, telemetry::Init, crash::Install, runtime check, SFSE interfaces, listener boot, load hook, cover hook. Logs '1.2.1 loaded' although SFSEPlugin_Version says 122.
- **Open FlyingVehicleOverhaul.log (old one kept as .prev.log)** (action, `log.cpp:41` · log `OBS system=log event=own_file`): Moves the previous log to <Name>.prev.log. If the old file is still held by a running game, this run writes <Name>.<pid>.log instead of truncating the live one (2026-11-07 fix).
- **Telemetry clock + jsonl writer thread (test builds)** (action, `telemetry.cpp:84`): Starts the clock; in FVO_RELEASE returns before opening a file. Test builds write Logs\SeamlessPlanets\vehicle-telemetry-<time>.jsonl from a background thread.
- **Install crash logger, dump thread and freeze watch** (action, `crash.cpp:791` · log `OBS system=run event=previous_run`): Always: SetUnhandledExceptionFilter, dbghelp dump thread, atexit clean-exit record. Test builds only (#ifndef FVO_RELEASE): previous-run report, run-state.txt, FreezeWatch thread, vectored first-chance handler. [diagnostics] hangDumpSeconds 45 (6..600).
- **Is the game runtime 1.16.244?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:565`): sfse->runtimeVersion must equal addr::kRuntimeVersion (0x01100F40). Any other build: the plugin returns false.
- **Messaging + task interfaces and SFSE listener registered?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:565`): QueryInterface(kInterface_Messaging/Task) and RegisterListener("SFSE", SfseMessage). All fallible SFSE registration finishes before any early detour.
- **Native vehicle listener published before data load?** (decision, `listenerboot.h:18`): Via vehiclediag::PrepareForDataLoad: checks the assignment bytes at +0x13169DB and 4 vtables, then publishes the static object pointer at +0x61EEAD8 (NULL-only swap) so persistent vehicle records can call the pending-list callbacks early; then installs the pending-list hooks. Invalid: load returns false.
- **FVO stays off (load refused or service not started)** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:565`): Reached on a wrong runtime, missing SFSE interfaces, a refused listener boot, or a missing pulse/physics hook at service start. Each case logs one line.
- **Hook the game's load routine FUN_141815620** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:119`): SFSE never sent PreLoadGame/PostLoadGame in practice, so the load routine itself is wrapped (15-byte prologue check). Not installed: FVO does not pause during loads.
- **Hook the loading-screen reduced step FUN_1418891E0** (action, `lifecycle.cpp:121`): While Main+0x44C (loading screen) is set the main loop takes this reduced step, which never drains the service queue; the detour runs traffic's queued commands after it. Not installed: traffic commands wait for the screen to close.
- **Load returns true; wait for SFSE messages** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:565`): Stage exit. Also logs the autopilot and boarding revision strings.

Arrows: sfseLoad → logInit; logInit → telemetryInit; telemetryInit → crashInstall; crashInstall → runtimeOk; runtimeOk → disabled (other runtime: return false); runtimeOk → sfseIfaces (1.16.244); sfseIfaces → disabled (missing: return false); sfseIfaces → listenerBoot (registered); listenerBoot → disabled (signatures differ: return false); listenerBoot → loadHookInstall (published or already present); loadHookInstall → coverInstall; coverInstall → bootPort

## A2 SFSE messages and service start  `#flow-a-s2`  (Detailed flow · A Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics)

Stage 2 of 9 in A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics. Next: Engine hooks owned by area A (event driven).

- **Which SFSE message arrived?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:529`): PostLoad: listen for the core. PostDataLoad: start standalone if no core started. PreLoadGame / PostLoadGame: load timestamps (rarely sent).
- **PostLoad: listen for the SeamlessPlanets core API** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:529`): RegisterListener(seamless::kCorePluginName = "SeamlessPlanets", CoreMessage). The Seamless core is no longer installed, so in practice no API message comes.
- **Core API message valid (type SPAP, version 1, full size)?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:517`): Invalid: g_started = true and FVO disables itself so no second update hook is ever installed. Valid: WithCore.
- **WithCore: run under the core's frame, save and reset callbacks** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:481`): SetApi, vehicleflight::Install, InstallFeatureSystems, then registers BeforeTrafficSave/AfterTrafficSave, Frame, CorePlayerUpdate (the pulse) and Reset with the core. Legacy path; the core DLL was removed.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: CorePlayerUpdate -> shipmodel::OnPlayerUpdate
- **PostDataLoad: start the standalone service** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:499`): The normal path. Needs the task interface, then vehicle_probe::Install (ship-system update hook 142169100: ground probes and the native update pulse) and vehicleflight::Install (vehicle step hook).
- **Ship-system update hook installed (pulse + probes)?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:499`): vehicle_probe::Install hooks addr::kShipSystemUpdate (16-byte prologue). Its detour posts updateschedule::Pulse each native update and services ground/ray/sweep probes inside the game's update. Missing: 'standalone update unavailable; disabled'.
- **Vehicle physics step hook installed?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:499`): vehicleflight::Install hooks addr::kVehicleStep 1423CB260 (plus VTOL level/damp/clamp and boost hooks). Missing: FVO disabled. Owned by area B.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicleflight::Install: vehicle step 1423CB260, VTOL level/damp/clamp, boost hooks
- **Hook SaveGame FUN_141814220 (core owns it in every install)** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:144`): 2026-11-17: the core owns the save hook (saved hover always, traffic snapshot only with the addon). Sets trafficprofile::SaveReady(true/false); without it saved hover is off and traffic stays paused.
- **InstallFeatureSystems: every feature hook, once** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:475`): world::SetServices (vehicle list callbacks), then hoverui, routeedit, mapguard, disableguard, inputgate, mappoints, loadedarea, npcvehicles, profiles, vehicleaim, vehiclediag, vehiclehits enemy bar, flighttune::Load. Each Install checks its own bytes and logs on mismatch; none can stop the service.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicleaim, vehiclediag Install (build/pending/motion hooks), flighttune::Load
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: npcvehicles::Install (seat, finder, sit, dismount, input hooks), loadedarea, profiles
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: routeedit::Install, mappoints::Install (globe publish hook)
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: hoverui::Install (settings menu), inputgate::Install (input resolve redirect)
- **AddTaskPermanent(FrameTask): Frame on every main-thread task** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:499`): g_standalone = true. FrameTask::Run calls Frame() on the game's main thread every SFSE task pass. Native-pulse scheduling, 60 Hz fallback, 240 Hz admission ceiling, no catch-up queue.
- **Service running: hooks armed, Frame task registered** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:499`): Stage exit. From here control enters area A per frame (A.frame) and per engine event (the detours in stage s3).
- **SFSE PreLoadGame: mark load, reset requests (and world)** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:529`): g_loadBegan, trafficprofile::BeginLoad, lifecycle::Reset, and in standalone a full Reset() on the SFSE thread. Second path beside the load-routine hook; the hook is what actually fires.
- **SFSE PostLoadGame: mark load done** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:529`): g_loadDone = now; the 3 s settle in Loading() starts from here or from the hook's return.

Arrows: sfseMsg → coreListen (PostLoad); sfseMsg → withoutCore (PostDataLoad and no core started); sfseMsg → preLoadMsg (PreLoadGame); sfseMsg → postLoadMsg (PostLoadGame); coreListen → coreApiOk (core API message (legacy core)); coreApiOk → withCore (valid v1); withCore → physHookOk; withoutCore → pulseHookOk; pulseHookOk → physHookOk (pulse hook in); physHookOk → saveHookInstall (standalone); physHookOk → featureSystems (with core (core owns the save hook)); saveHookInstall → featureSystems (SaveReady true or false); featureSystems → addTask (standalone); featureSystems → servicePort (with core: core calls Frame); addTask → servicePort

## A3 Engine hooks owned by area A (event driven)  `#flow-a-s3`  (Detailed flow · A Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics)

Stage 3 of 9 in A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics. Next: Frame gate (Frame, main thread).

- **hook::Install / RedirectCall: patch only if bytes match** (action, `hook.cpp:88`): Install: compares the expected prologue, copies it into a 64-byte trampoline, writes a 14-byte absolute jmp and NOPs the rest. RedirectCall: rewrites one E8 call through a near relay page after checking the old destination. A mismatch changes nothing and logs.
- **LoadGameDetour: close requests, drop old world, run the load** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:100`): Runs on the load's background thread. Before the original: lifecycle::Close, trafficprofile::BeginLoad, trafficarrival::Reset, flightsave::OnLoad(save name), vehiclediag::NoteBeforeLoad, g_dropAll = true. After: lifecycle::Open, g_loadDone. 2026-10-28 crashes: nothing that changes the world runs mid-load.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: flightsave::OnLoad(save name): restore saved hover per save
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficprofile::BeginLoad, trafficarrival::Reset
- **SaveGameDetour: record per-save state around the native save** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:133`): flightsave::OnSave(save name) always; BeforeTrafficSave before the original; AfterTrafficSave from a scope guard after it.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: flightsave::OnSave(save name)
- **Is the FVO Traffic addon attached?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:131`): trafficpool::AddonAttached(). Yes: trafficprofile::BeforeSave takes the traffic snapshot for this save. Also gates trafficprofile::Allow each frame and the cover-closing call.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficprofile::BeforeSave/AfterSave traffic snapshot
- **ReducedStepDetour: loading-screen step ran; name main thread** (action, `lifecycle.cpp:110`): Calls the native step first. The first caller's thread becomes coverThread; a call from another thread is logged once and runs nothing.
- **Service lock free? run cover commands for up to 3 ms** (decision, `lifecycle_cover.h:20`): RunCover: never waits; busy lock = next step. Runs CoverList FIFO under the engine's own service lock (145F43DC8), kCoverBudgetMs 3.0, inside __try so a fault still releases the lock.
- **Actor queued for disable before it was set up (+0x358 null)?** (decision, `disableguard.cpp:49`): Detour on FUN_141958630 (actor vtable +0x7E8). Null +0x358: the disable is skipped and logged (first 50) instead of crashing at +0x1958672 (2026-10-29 traffic-driver crashes).
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: protects traffic-pool drivers queued for disable early
- **Local map model build over 60000 references?** (decision, `mapguard.cpp:102` · log `OBS system=map.trace`): Vtable slot patch on the build task (0x144C54E38 slot 2, FUN_14142FAE0). Over kMaxModels 60000 the list is shortened for the build and restored after (the node's child count is 16-bit; 65535 wrapped and crashed).
- **Test builds: observe map builders and procgen calls** (action, `mapguard.cpp:85` · log `OBS system=map.trace`): [diagnostics] mapTrace=1 (default): 5 builder hooks + 2 procgen call-site redirects, observation only. Procgen time goes to mapguard::g_procgenAt, which pauses rays for 3 s (probeguard).
- **Wheel dust being created into a persistent cell?** (decision, `mapguard.cpp:418`): Hook on create-reference FUN_1405A0520: REV-8 wheel-dust bases going into the persistent cell get the invalid handle (as a failed placement). Dust in ordinary cells is kept.
- **Detour returns to the game** (state, `hook.cpp:88`): Stage exit. Every detour calls the original through its trampoline (except the refused disable and the dust create).

Arrows: hookInstall → hookPort (patched or unchanged); mapModels → mapTrace (Install: test builds, mapTrace=1); mapModels → hookPort (>60000: build first 60000, restore; else original); dustCreate → hookPort (dust to persistent: invalid handle; else original); mapTrace → hookPort (observation only); disableGuard → hookPort (+0x358 null: skipped; else original disable); loadDetour → hookPort (original load returned); saveDetour → addonAttached (before the native save); addonAttached → hookPort (yes: traffic snapshot taken; no: hover state only); reducedStep → coverRun (main thread and cover list not empty); reducedStep → hookPort (other thread or empty list); coverRun → hookPort (lock busy: try next step)

## A4 Frame gate (Frame, main thread)  `#flow-a-s4`  (Detailed flow · A Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics)

Stage 4 of 9 in A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics. Next: Full update order (Update) and housekeeping.

- **Frame(): main-thread task entry each SFSE task pass** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): trafficprofile::Report first (outside the re-entry guard), then the guard, heartbeat, load drop, world state, scheduler.
- **Already inside Frame (engine pumped tasks recursively)?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): g_inCallback atomic flag: a nested call returns at once (skips, never waits).
- **crash::Beat: heartbeat for the freeze watch** (action, `crash.cpp:784` · log `OBS system=freeze.stall`): Every non-nested Frame, paused or not, so a menu is not read as a hang. A gap of 3 s or more is recorded as a recovered stall.
- **A load started since the last frame (g_dropAll), standalone?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): 2026-11-15 hotfix: drop everything FVO holds from the old world on the very next frame instead of waiting for planet state 0/1.
- **Reset(): every module drops what it holds from the old world** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:263` · log `OBS system=runtime event=reset`): Order: traffic disallowed, lifecycle::Reset FIRST (no queued callback refills), secretunpause, scheduler/pacing, runtimecells, bindings, vehicleflight, shipvehicle, shipmodel, vehicle_probe, realvehicles, vehiclediag, ringroad, routeprobes, trafficpool, pilotquest, npcvehicles, passenger, scantarget, traffic, context, viewdist, vehiclekeep, profiles, world, vehiclehits, navseed, cellbake, navmap, navfield, environment.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicleflight::LoadReset, vehiclediag::Reset, vehicle_probe::Reset
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficpool, pilotquest, npcvehicles, passenger, traffic, context, profiles, vehiclekeep, vehiclehits Reset
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: ringroad, routeprobes Reset
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: bindings, shipvehicle, shipmodel, environment Reset
- **Planet manager state: 0/1 world replaced, 2 world ready?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): game::ReadPlanetState (vtable-checked). Standalone: world ready and state 0/1 -> Reset. State 3/4 (terrain re-centre every ~250 m) never resets. g_worldReady = state 2.
- **Scheduler: Full, Housekeeping or Skip this poll?** (decision, `updateschedule.h:29` · log `OBS system=runtime`): Full once per new native pulse serial (ship-system update), at most 240/s; if no pulse for 100 ms, a 60 Hz clock fallback. Otherwise Housekeeping at 60 Hz, else Skip. Missed pulses are coalesced, never replayed.
- **Save loading or settling (began, not done + 3 s)?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:157` · log `OBS system=runtime`): kLoadSettleMs 3000 after the load returns; kLoadTimeoutMs 180000 if it never returns. Sets trafficpool::WorldActionsAllowed(!loading && worldReady).
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficpool::WorldActionsAllowed, trafficprofile::Allow
- **Arrival window: loading screen up, no save read, world ready?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:194` · log `OBS system=cover.measure`): Screen flag +0x5E60AFA (BeginLoadingScreen writes 1, EndLoadingScreen 0). Logs open/ready/seeded/close, measures pause and menus 4x/s while up and 3 s after (FaderMenu -> trafficarrival::fade). Sets trafficarrival::Window and crash::SetGameplay(worldReady && !screen && !readingSave).
- **Screen closed: tell the traffic addon (CoverClosingV1)** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:194` · log `OBS system=cover.closing`): Records trafficarrival::closedAt, logs arrival placement counts, then calls the optional export FVO_Traffic_CoverClosingV1(3) while the cover still holds. An older addon without it is left alone.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: FVO_Traffic_CoverClosingV1(3), trafficarrival closedAt/fade/Window
- **Behind the screen during a save settle: vehicle list + traffic only** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): When arrival && loading && work != Skip: world::Publish, navseed::Tick (nav map seeded first), trafficpool::Tick. Flight, AI and everything else wait.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: world::Publish + navseed::Tick behind the loading screen
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficpool::Tick places arrival traffic behind the screen
- **World safe for the full simulation (!loading && worldReady)?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): 2026-11-15 hotfix: no full simulation at the main menu, during a save load or its settle. Door/fast-travel/landing screens without a save load keep the full update. Transitions log and leave a crumb.
- **Held: 1 s load trace; re-attach kept vehicles after load** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): Once a second: loading, world ready, screen, manager state, TES interior cell, hook counts. After the load routine returned and world ready: vehiclediag::AfterLoadTick gives kept vehicles a chassis before the world runs.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehiclediag::AfterLoadTick re-attaches kept vehicles' chassis
- **Run Full update, Housekeeping, or nothing?** (decision, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): Full is downgraded to Housekeeping when the world is not safe. Skip runs nothing more except the pacing measurement.
- **Gate decided: admitted work begins** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:347`): Stage exit. 

Arrows: frame → reentry; reentry → beat (first entry); beat → dropAll; dropAll → reset (yes: drop old world); dropAll → planetState (no); reset → planetState (worldReady = false); planetState → reset (standalone, was ready, state 0/1); planetState → schedule (worldReady = (state 2)); schedule → loading (Full / Housekeeping / Skip); loading → arrival (loading flag); arrival → coverClosing (screen just closed); arrival → arrivalTick (window && loading && not Skip); arrival → worldSafe (otherwise); coverClosing → worldSafe; arrivalTick → worldSafe; worldSafe → heldTrace (not safe); worldSafe → workKind (safe); heldTrace → workKind (Full downgraded to Housekeeping); workKind → gatePort (Full or Housekeeping)

## A5 Full update order (Update) and housekeeping  `#flow-a-s5`  (Detailed flow · A Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics)

Stage 5 of 9 in A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics. Next: After-update services and reports.

- **Frame setup: scene scan cache, features, bindings, frame marks** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): modscene::NewFrame (one shared loaded-ref scan per frame), features::Tick, bindings::Tick, vehicleflight::BeginFrame and npcvehicles::BeginFrame (decision owners must renew their commands this frame).

  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: call: bindings::Tick; features read by hoverui settings menu

- **Feature switches: reload settings.ini if changed (5 s)** (action, `features.cpp:144`): Reload compares write time and size. On() reads cached atomics on any thread; AlwaysOn ids answer true, PhysicalAi follows PhysicalVtol, retired Placement/PlacementMode answer false.
- **Installer fragment present and changed? import once** (decision, `featureinstaller.h:25`): First Reload only: Data\SFSE\Plugins\FlyingVehicleOverhaul.features\mode.ini enabled=1; per-feature <section>.<key>.ini; only choices that differ from the .fomod-applied ledger are written; settings.ini backed up once to .before-fomod. A missing page file means no choice.
- **1 identity+world: world::Publish (the vehicle list)** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): Cost bucket 0. Every later step reads this frame's vehicle list.

- **3 map+navigation: bake, survey, navmesh, seed, field, surface, navmap** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): In order: cellbake, routesurvey, navprobe, navmesh (each told Loading()), navseed, navfield::Service, environment, navmap. Each part timed; the slowest is named in a spike line.

  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: call: environment::Tick (surface checks)
- **4 context+profiles: profiles::Tick, context::Tick** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): Cost bucket 3.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: call: profiles::Tick, context::Tick

  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: call: npcvehicles::Tick, passenger::Tick
- **6 flight: vehicleflight::Tick, vehiclediag::Tick, loadedarea::Tick** (action, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): Cost bucket 5. The physics itself runs later in the vehicle-step hook on the physics thread; this is the main-thread planning side.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: call: vehicleflight::Tick, vehiclediag::Tick
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: call: loadedarea::Tick (wall pass)

  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: call: realvehicles, trafficpool, traffic, vehiclekeep, scantarget ticks

  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: call: shipvehicle::Tick

## A7 Engine request service (lifecycle) and engine adapters  `#flow-a-s7`  (Detailed flow · A Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics)

Stage 7 of 9 in A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics. Next: Diagnostics: crash, stall, hang and logs.

- **lifecycle::Poll / Post: keyed request to run engine work** (action, `lifecycle.cpp:150` · log `OBS system=lifecycle.poll`): Requests table (lifecycle_requests.h): one ticket per key, 64 limit, epoch generation so results from an old world read as 0. Poll returns the result on a later frame; Post is fire-and-forget. No synchronous waits.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: API: traffic/pool births and moves go through lifecycle Poll/Post
- **Screen down: cover leftovers go to the native queue first** (action, `lifecycle.cpp:37`): Called on every Poll/Post/Pending; logs commands run behind the screen, steps, longest, handed and busy.
- **Can the lifecycle queue accept this request?** (decision, `lifecycle_requests.h:63` · log `OBS system=lifecycle.post`): Polling waits when closed or full. A post says false on closure, a full table, unavailable work or a rejected submission, so the owner can retry. Accepted or already pending posts return true. Uncollected completions can be evicted after 10 s when full.
- **lifecycle::Keep: owning handle with form-id check** (action, `lifecycle.cpp:134`): Resolves the ref's handle through the game's handle table; a different pointer or form id gives an empty handle; released with the game's RefRelease.
- **Loading screen up and cover thread known?** (decision, `lifecycle.cpp:55`): Needs the native service arena verified (Compatible: 15-byte check at 1422CDF60). Screen up: keep the ticket in the CoverList. Otherwise hand off any cover leftovers, then the native queue.
- **Hold in CoverList for the loading-screen step** (state, `lifecycle_cover.h:43`): FIFO; run by A.coverRun on the main thread under the service lock.
- **Enqueue a Command into the game's BSService queue** (action, `lifecycle_transport.h:24`): Pinned 1.16.244 arena ABI: allocator 1422CDF60 reserves a Command {vtable, Invoke, ticket}; the engine runs it in its service drain.
- **Command runs: inside the service (owner thread)?** (decision, `lifecycle.cpp:55`): Wrapped work calls crash::Action, then refuses (result 0) if not InService (threading flag clear or owner == this thread). Request::Run skips canceled or old-epoch work.
- **engineworker::Post: one long-lived thread for off-main jobs** (action, `engineworker.h:40` · log `OBS system=engineworker`): The game numbers each thread that takes its reader lock (max 64, never given back); one thread = one number for the session. Used by navmesh and navprobe; jobs run in order.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: runs navmesh bake and navprobe jobs off the main thread
- **Result ready for the caller's next poll** (state, `lifecycle.cpp:159`): Stage exit. 

Arrows: lcPoll → lcHandOff (every Poll/Post/Pending first); lcHandOff → lcNative (screen down, leftovers); lcHandOff → lcClosed; lcClosed → lcPort (closed or full: nothing yet / post dropped; or done: result); lcClosed → lcKeep (new ticket: factory builds work (owning handles)); lcKeep → lcSubmit (work ready); lcSubmit → lcCover (screen up, cover thread known); lcSubmit → lcNative (otherwise (arena verified)); lcSubmit → lcPort (arena unsupported: failed result); lcNative → lcInService (game's service drain); lcInService → lcPort (ran (or refused: 0)); engineWorker → lcPort (job done on the worker thread)

## A8 Diagnostics: crash, stall, hang and logs  `#flow-a-s8`  (Detailed flow · A Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics)

Stage 8 of 9 in A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics. Next: Stores and contracts other parts read.

- **FreezeWatch thread: once a second (test builds)** (action, `crash.cpp:709` · log `OBS system=freeze.memory`): Reads the heartbeat, gameplay flag and foreground window; writes run-state.txt every 5 s and process memory every 10 s.
- **Stall: gap >=3 s, foreground, gameplay, <3 captures?** (decision, `stallcapture.h:9` · log `OBS system=freeze.stall`): Gates: no_heartbeat, below_threshold, background, not_gameplay, quota (3), already_captured, retry_backoff (1 s), attempts_exhausted (3). Watch line every 5 s or on a gate change.
- **Write last-stall-N.dmp, scan locks, keep a dated copy** (action, `crash.cpp:227` · log `OBS system=freeze.locks`): Small dump of the hitch in play; on success LogLocks (spinners, waiters, allocator OOM retry) and KeepCopy.
- **No update for hangDumpSeconds (45 s)?** (decision, `crash.cpp:709`): Up to 3 attempts, 10 s apart, once per run.
- **All-thread last-hang.dmp + locks + last FVO actions** (action, `crash.cpp:181` · log `OBS system=freeze.alloc`): First attempt logs LogLocks and LogActions; the dump is copied by KeepCopy.
- **Keep dated dump copies: 3 hang, 6 stall** (action, `dumpkeep.h:19` · log `OBS system=freeze.keep`): PF-2: hang-/stall-<date>-<time>-fg|bg.dmp, oldest deleted past kKeepHang 3 / kKeepStall 6, so a later false alarm cannot overwrite evidence.
- **6 s without update: log the beat thread's stack candidates** (action, `crash.cpp:709`): Suspends the last update thread, copies its stack, prints game and FVO return addresses; 12 lines per run, WriteUnlocked (never waits for the log lock).
- **Fatal exception: CRASH lines, registers, crumbs, last-crash.dmp** (action, `crash.cpp:295`): Vectored handler (test builds) and the unhandled filter (all builds). Faults flagged by SetExpectedFaults are ignored. Prints module+offset, registers, breadcrumbs, register objects matched to recent FVO actions, unwound stack.
- **First fault in the game allocator range? last-allocator.dmp** (decision, `crash.cpp:281`): First access violation in Starfield+22D2000..22D37FF writes last-allocator.dmp, once per process.
- **Recording done; watcher sleeps 1 s** (state, `crash.cpp:709`): Stage exit. 

Arrows: freezeWatch → stallGate; stallGate → stallDump (capture); stallGate → hangGate (any other gate); stallDump → dumpKeep (written); stallDump → hangGate; hangGate → hangDump (yes, attempts < 3); hangGate → stack6s (no); hangDump → dumpKeep (written); hangDump → stack6s; dumpKeep → stack6s; stack6s → diagPort (6 s+ and under 12 lines: stack logged); crashHandler → allocatorDump (fault in allocator range); crashHandler → diagPort (expected or non-fatal: ignored; fatal: report + last-crash.dmp); allocatorDump → diagPort (first one: dump written)

Always on: CombatAi, HoverOrDrive, VehicleDamage, HealthBars, StatusCard, Witnesses, NativeGround.

- **FVO addon C interface (exports for FVO_Traffic)** (state, `fvo_addon_api.h:23`): Data store read by other steps. Plain-C types: trip goals and step, traffic provider, pool spots, route and cruise planners, world shift, local time, RefPos, tick registration. Exports live in api_traffic.cpp (area D).
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: C interface implemented in api_traffic.cpp, used by FVO_Traffic
- **One vehicle limit (256) and probe/ray slot layout** (state, `fvolimits.h:9`): Data store read by other steps. kVehicles 256 sizes the vehicle list, pilot slots, probe/ray/sweep slots (prefetch.h enums) and traffic pools.
- **Is play time running? (cover, menu, photo bits)** (state, `playclock.h:13`): Data store read by other steps. kCovered (trafficarrival::Covered), kMenu (!inputgate::GameplayAllowed), kPhoto (routeedit::PhotoOpen). Every clock that must not count paused time reads this.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: reads inputgate::GameplayAllowed

Pure data contracts used by C and D.

Arrows: logData → observeGate (Observe lines pass the gate)

## B1 Frame and the player's own vehicle (PlayerTick)  `#flow-b-s1`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Each frame vehicleflight::Tick runs PlayerTick (the player's own vehicle: keys, gear, exit, summon/cover, Auto/Cruise handover, the hover toggle and the saved-hover restore) and then PilotsTick (NPC pilots), and both feed Fly(), which turns a goal or the player's keys into targets for one of four modes: manual wheels (hover off, the game's controls, gear caps ground speed), drive mode (autopilot on the game's wheels through the drive mailbox, optionally the rail or tyre mode), the stock hover model, or the physical VTOL chassis. On the physics thread StepDetour (the hooked native vehicle step, every vehicle) finds which pilot slot owns the body, applies the gear's tyre grip and turning weight BEFORE the game's wheel step, then measures rear slip and applies the four corner forces AFTER it, and finally StepModel writes the hover/rail/guard/ground-cap velocities or StepPhysical's impulses. The gear (1-4, Alt tap for the player, auto gear for Cruise/AI) sets top speed, thrust, tyre friction, rear grip, turning weight and the corner-force budget. The camera only reads the vehicle (yaw gap, pitch aim) except in physical VTOL, where a hooked camera rotation banks with the chassis. A save made while the player hovers by hand records vehicle, height and gear; loading it turns the hover back on through the normal Start path. Next: Mode switches: Start, Stop, Auto, Cruise, drive on/off. Oddities found when mapping: Order in the physics hook (StepDetour): before the game's wheel step = CheckCollision, PreparePhysical, ApplyGrip (tyre friction + turning weight x gear) and ApplyTraction; after it = WheelSlip, MeasureRear, ApplyCorners, then StepModel's velocity/spin writes (hover, rail, guard, ground cap) or StepPhysical's impulses. So the stock hover and the rail overwrite what the game just computed, while grip is set up front and corner forces correct afterwards. Oddities: (1) Two systems still steer the same car on a rail lane: the rail velocity writer and the tyre-mode pedals switch per frame on settings flags and reverse direction (railTyres / railTyresReverse); g_driveRail is a constexpr true, so the 'off' branch is dead. (2) The gear means different things per mode: in manual driving it scales ground speed to 0.25/0.5/0.75/1.0 of a top speed learned only in gear 4 (kGroundShare), while wheelgrip calls gear 3 'the game's own car' for grip; so gear 3 manual still caps speed at 75 % of learned top. Hover gears use kGearTop 30/60/100/150; the autopilot forces apGear=4 and uses kAiGearTop, but auto gear writes g.gear 1-3, which only changes grip/turning weight/corner budget and steering cap, not the AI top speed. (3) Two hand-back paths when the autopilot ends in drive mode: HO-1 Stop (autoOnWheels) and LeaveDrive (hover on, lift-off) for drive mode entered later by the plan; whether an Auto started in hover ends on wheels depends on the start, not on the current mode. (4) Dead or unwired files in this list: flight.h declares namespace flight but no flight.cpp exists and nothing calls it (vehicleflight.cpp only includes it); flightmodel.cpp/flighttune.cpp are compiled but only flighttune::Load runs at startup, the fm model is never stepped; camtune is used only by shipmodel.cpp (ship, not this area); native_movement_* are compiled but nothing outside them calls them (main_vehicle.cpp comment says the probe was switched off); travelmode::Decide is never called (only its Default enum and kDamaged are used, by the energy plan's permit); railturn::Pursue and hoverfollow::Follow run inside AutodriveDecide (area C); liftpath.h is used only by the traffic addon (area E), travellogic.h by traffic.cpp (D), vehiclevel.h by vehicleworld.cpp (C), bodyowner.h by loadedarea.cpp (A), physicssetup.h by vehiclediag.cpp (A, launch guard / wreck glide), native_personalities.h by npcvehicles.cpp (D), camera_visibility.h by vehiclekeep/environment/inputgate (D/F); cameraahead.h is used here only for the summon air-spot check. (5) Traction control (GK-1) is wired but off by default (all slipN 0), and its input is the WheelInfo +0xB4 slip that autogear.h calls unproven. (6) Stage s6 has several exits (guard, rail, ground cap, physical, hover, unowned) that all return to the game; s1 has many early returns (dead, no vehicle, hover toggle off) rather than one way out. (7) The camera hooks and hoverui only act in physical VTOL; in the stock hover the camera is purely read (yaw gap drives the nose).

- **Frame: snapshot vehicles, env, player tick, pilots tick, rebind** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:9554`): Called from the frame task after npcvehicles/passenger. Order: ReportStepCost, world::Vehicles snapshot, EnvTick, TrailTick + PlayerAirTick, PlayerTick, PilotsTick, BindBody for every slot, WatchFlip of the player's empty rover.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: called from the frame task in main_vehicle.cpp; LoadReset on load; vehicleflight::Install at boot
- **Is the player dead?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:3818`): Actor vtable +0x868. Yes: Stop("you died"), Auto, hold, follow and guard off, HUD cleared, nothing else this frame.
- **Which vehicle does the player drive?** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:3432`): DrivenBy(player) each frame; g_driverVehicle is read by the physics hook and the boost hook; g_manualDriverId is 0 while riding as a passenger. Boost request = boost held or throttle lock in hover, never while Auto.
- **Publish what a save made now would record** (action, `flightsave.cpp:37`): Only the player's own manual hover at the wheel (active, not autopilot, not passenger): vehicle id, z, gear; otherwise vehicle 0.
- **Gear key tapped? Cycle gear 1-2-3-4-1** (decision, `altcontrol.h:29`): bindings Gear trigger -> g.gear = NextGear. The notice shows the gear's top speed and grip/rear/turning weight from wheelgrip::ProfileOf; gear 3 is the game's own handling. The same g.gear is used on wheels and in hover and carries over into Start. Hovering, the notice also names the gear's flight row (turn response, drift damping, turn grip) and vehicle.gear logs event=flight_shift reason=player_gear_key.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: input: bindings actions (Gear, HoverToggle, Boost, Accelerate, Brake, Turn, Lift, ThrottleLock, FlightAssist, DriverExit)
- **Flight assist key toggles the FlightAssist feature** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): features::Set(FlightAssist). Read by Fly into Shared::assist (stock roll levelling, vtolassist coordinated turns).
- **Is the driven vehicle wrecked?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): Yes: Auto off, Stop("wrecked: getting you out"), the game's own exit first (npcvehicles::BeginPlayerExit), a forced ExitPlayer after 2.5 s, retried every 500 ms.
- **Exit key pressed while hovering?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): Hover blocks the game's exit test, so: Auto off, Stop("exit: setting down first"), then after 0.4 s and under 0.8 m/s (or 4 s) the game's exit runs.
- **Got out with Auto on? Vehicle holds its spot** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): playerAuto off; if hovering, holdId/holdX/Y/Z = the last driven vehicle and autopilot stays on (a hold goal).
- **Auto on and a drive key, gear or brake pressed?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): Accelerate/Brake/TurnLeft/TurnRight amount > 0 or the gear key ends Auto (playerAuto, autopilot off). Boarding with a pending scanner trip instead starts Auto (Start on its wheels). Ending Auto also ends Cruise later in the frame.
- **On foot: summon / cover / crew keys** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607` · log `summon.air`): keygesture tap/hold (1 s). Summon far = AskMove next to the player, near = Start(rover) + autopilot; cover/follow launches the rover after kFollowRelaunch; a follower can be handed the vehicle; profiles run on the empty vehicle. Every path ends in Start/Stop and a Goal.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: call: npcvehicles crew orders, CrewBoard, Recall, FreeFollower, ExitPlayer
- **Build this frame's Goal (hold, cruise lap, profile, marker, summon)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): Hold point; Cruise: ask the addon's cruise planner (every 5 s if none) and step the lap's FvoTripClaim; a profile's vcontract goal; the taxi marker with an optional traffic route trip and Orbit; remote profile; summon/follow point (under the player when airborne). PilotNav carries the lane/route nav params.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: call: PilotNav / RouteNav nav params, AtDestination, triplogic
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: API: the Traffic addon's cruise planner and route planner (FvoTripClaim step)
- **Hover off: does a loaded save ask for the hover back?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): Asked every frame while hover is off until answered. Restore: g.gear = saved gear, then Start(driven) through the same on path as the hover key.
- **No vehicle to control this frame?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): Not driving and no autopilot vehicle: Stop("left the vehicle") if hovering, groundOn off, return.
- **Hover key pressed while driving (500 ms guard)?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): The only manual hover on/off. A second toggle within 500 ms is ignored (L3 double edges). On: Start(vehicle) (lift-off). Off: Stop(key label).
- **Hover off: manual wheels, the gear caps ground speed** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): BindBody, groundOn=true, groundFrac = {1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1}[gear] of a top speed learned in gear 4 with W held (no boost). The game's own controls drive; the physics hook still applies the gear's grip and corner forces.
- **A descend-before-stop pending for the player's vehicle?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:7607`): Yes: SettleTick instead of Fly.
- **NPC pilots: same Fly per slot at 20/10/5 Hz tiers** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:8727`): 16 nearest within 150 m at 20 Hz, to 300 m 10 Hz, rest 5 Hz; unbound bodies 3 Hz. Settle pending -> SettleTick. NPC side is area D.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: NPC pilots' jobs (PilotFly, PilotCommand, PilotStop) come from npcvehicles / traffic
- **Out: Fly(idx, vehicle, goal)** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Stage exit. Hover is active (g.active); the goal and focus go to Fly.
