Arrows: tick → dead (player present); dead → driven (alive); driven → flightsavePub; flightsavePub → gearKey; gearKey → assistKey; assistKey → wreck; wreck → exitKey (not wrecked); exitKey → gotOutAuto; gotOutAuto → autoTakeover (driving); autoTakeover → summonCover; summonCover → goal; goal → restore; restore → noVehicle (no restore); noVehicle → hoverToggle (a vehicle); hoverToggle → manualDrive (hover off); hoverToggle → settleCheck (hover on); settleCheck → p1 (no); tick → pilotsTick (after PlayerTick); pilotsTick → p1 (each NPC pilot due this frame)

## B2 Mode switches: Start, Stop, Auto, Cruise, drive on/off  `#flow-b-s2`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Stage 2 of 8 in B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save. Next: Fly: per-pilot decisions on the main thread.

- **Cruise key (double T): Auto with no destination** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:9267`): Needs the traffic addon's cruise planner (FVO_RegisterCruisePlanner). Starts Auto if needed, cruise=true; a second press ends it via ToggleAuto.
- **Auto key (T tap): Auto on/off** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:9241`): Called by scantarget (area F). Off: playerAuto/autopilot off. On: if not hovering Start(0, driven, onWheels=true) (CW-1), autopilot+playerAuto, taxi and cruise state cleared.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: call: scantarget.cpp routes the Auto / Cruise keys and scanner trips
- **Scanner trip: Auto to the marker (now or on boarding)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:9219`): Driving: Start on its wheels, Auto on, Cruise ended. On foot: summon now, g_goOnBoard arms Auto for when you board (15 min). Cancel holds at the current spot.
- **Start: reset pilot state, bind body, hover on** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:3835`): State reset keeping key and gear; liftoff=true; Shared reset (spin 0, gEst clamped 3..20, physical state cleared), BindBody, Shared::active=true. The body keeps its speed and attitude.
- **Started on its wheels (Auto/Cruise/scanner)?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:3835`): Yes: no lift-off; drive=true, autoOnWheels=true, adOut.mode=Drive, Shared::active=false (hover model not writing). The energy plan may switch to hover later where the route allows.
- **LeaveDrive: drive mode off, hover on (lift-off)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4341`): drive=false, g_driveOut cleared, Shared::active=true, spin and hold reset, liftoff=true.
- **Stop: hover off, drive mailbox cleared, autopilot ended** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:3763`): Settle first; then physOn off, camera reset (player), Shared active/physical/groundOn/landing off, g_driveOut cleared, boost flags cleared; an autopilot (not a wreck) leaves a release guard (kGuardMs) that zeroes sideways speed. 'collision' blocks relaunch 4 s.
- **Autopilot stop while more than 3.5 m up? Descend first** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:8676`): Never for the player's manual flight or urgent reasons (landed, lost, wreck, died, board, flipped...). Unknown height: descend anyway. Time limit 20 s + 1 s per 5 m.
- **Settle: scripted sink with brakes, then Stop** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:8698`): Command kVertical|kBrake|kThrottle|kScripted, sink 2..12 m/s, hold goal over the spot via Fly; Stop with settleForce when down or out of time.
- **Modes: wheels / drive mode / rail / hover / physical VTOL** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:703`): manual wheels = !g.active (groundOn); drive mode = g.drive (hover inactive, g_driveOut publishes steer/pedals); rail = Shared::railOn (+railTyres); hover = Shared::active; physical = Shared::physical && !physicalFault (else stock hover).
- **Out: mode set; Fly runs next frame** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:3763`): Stage exit. Mode switches only set state; the next Fly/physics step acts on it.

Arrows: autoKey → start (on, not hovering: onWheels=true); cruiseKey → autoKey (Auto not on yet / second press ends); scanTrip → start (driving: onWheels=true); start → startWheels; startWheels → modes (yes: drive mode, no lift-off / no: hover, lift-off); stop → settle (first); settle → settleTick (autopilot > 3.5 m up: descend first); settle → modes (not held: hover off, guard armed); settleTick → stop (down or timed out (settleForce)); leaveDrive → modes (hover on, lift-off); modes → p2

## B3 Fly: per-pilot decisions on the main thread  `#flow-b-s3`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Stage 3 of 8 in B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save. Next: Drive actuation on the game's wheels.

- **Fly: landing site, warnings, physics trace** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385` · log `flight.physics`): Reads scripted/manual commands (g_cmds), landing on arrival (environment::CheckSite Safe -> goal.z), nav height floor, VehicleWarning, PersonAhead; too many hits stops the autopilot.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: data: environment::CheckSite / BoardingSite for landing
- **A contact since last frame? (floor / bump / hard hit)** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): From CheckCollision. Vehicle contact: plain collision. Autopilot: cramped score and nav-map note, floor hold if pushed up. Manual: lift-off grace, push-up floor hold, bump or hard hit; the flight never ends on a hit.
- **Drive mode, autopilot ended, it started on wheels?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385` · log `vehicle.handover`): Yes: Stop("autopilot ended: back on its wheels"): the car goes back to the player's wheels, no hover.
- **Drive mode with no autopilot? Leave drive (hover on)** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Never keep overriding the controls: LeaveDrive("autopilot ended").
- **Autopilot: energy plan decides heading, speed, wheels/hover** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `plan.authority`): Stage exit. Area C owns it; it writes g.adOut (bearing, speed, mode Drive/Hover, reverse, brake, climb, why). Fly only reads adOut.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: call: AutodriveDecide (energy plan) writes g.adOut; railturn, hoverfollow, tracking cap and auto gear live inside it
- **Auto gear (Cruise/AI on wheels): gear from rear slip and skid** (decision, `autogear.h:30` · log `vehicle.gear`): Gear 1 if rear slip >= 8 deg or sideways >= 1.5 m/s; 2 if slip >= 4 deg; else 3 (SP-1 2026-11-19: the grade no longer picks a gear; a slope is the tyre controller's grade push). Down after 0.4 s, up one gear after 2.5 s. Writes g_gs[idx].gear; the player's own driving keeps the gear key.
- **Read keys (manual) or the plan (autopilot) into throttle, roll, lift** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Manual: boost/accelerate amount, brake, throttle lock, roll A/D, lift up/down, Alt drop, camera pitch aim over the learned rest. Paid boost = boost key with the game's meter (x1.75). Autopilot: throttle from adOut.speed, brake, apGear=4 with AI gear tables.
- **Top speed and thrust from the gear** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Manual gear tops 30/60/100/150 m/s (coasting at least kSpeedCap 60), thrust x0.6/1/1.6/2.5; AI 5/18/50/150 m/s, thrust 0.15/0.5/1.4/3.2; x1.75 paid boost; autopilot further capped by nav speed limit and adOut.speed+1. FC-1/FG-1: the gear tops are carcap::kFlightTop; on autopilot the FG-1 flight gear's top caps the speed (B.flightGear).
- **FG-1: autopilot flight gear from landing, obstacles, speed and turns** (decision, `flightgear.h:56` · log `OBS system=vehicle.gear event=flight_shift`): Gear 1 for landing, take-off, strafe, plan under 1 m/s, under 3 m over the floor, within 15 m of the stop or 20 m of a wall or vehicle; else the lowest gear whose top reaches the wanted speed, lower if the committed plan's sharpest turn needs more lateral than the gear holds, one up on a straight leg over 200 m. Down after 0.4 s, up one gear after 2.5 s. Player: the gear key's gear. On wheels the flight gear restarts; AG-1 is the ground rule. The plan's sharpest turn comes from flightgear::PeakTurn over the committed hover path.
- **Take the gear row; hull turn share and top for both writers** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385` · log `OBS system=vehicle.flightgear event=row / event=track`): flightRow = carcap::Flight(gear, autopilot); the hover publish writes g_sh.row and g_sh.hull = hulllogic::Flight(hullBase, physical, row.lateralScale). Planners read the hull, the physics step reads the row. Traces the row on a change and its handling each second.
- **Manual hover assist: clear / climb / turn left / right / stop?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): From the three corridor verdicts; held kAvoidHoldMs; off on autopilot (the plan decides) and when parked in the air. Climb raises pitch aim and lift; turns slow to kAvoidSlowTo; stop brakes to 0.
- **Autopilot attitude: yaw rate to bearing, pitch to climb, bank** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): yaw = kApYawGain x heading error; pitch along the wanted vertical speed (0 braking); roll toward a bank proportional to the yaw rate.
- **Speed cap: is the ground ahead ray-visible and cell-attached?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): All three ahead samples loaded: top; two: hold speed; one: 0.85 x speed; none or no ground: kSpeedCapBlind. Eased 0.5/1.5/3 s.
- **Autopilot: wheels wanted (script travel or plan mode Drive)?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): A scripted/manual kTravel command (1 float, 2 drive) wins, else adOut.mode. Drive wanted but empty vehicle or not yet down: groundHug (hover held low). Enter on the game's wheels only when occupied, ground known, < 2.5 m above, |vz| < 4. Leave when the plan wants hover (LeaveDrive).
- **Drive mode ON: hover stops writing, the game's step runs** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): drive=true, Shared::active=false, haveLast=false, boost visuals cleared.
- **Out: drive actuation (g.drive) or hover targets** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Stage exit. g.drive: s4 and return; otherwise s5.

Arrows: fly → contact; contact → handover; handover → driveEndAuto (no); driveEndAuto → autodrive (autopilot); driveEndAuto → input (manual hover); autodrive → autogear (on wheels, not flying (inside AutodriveDecide)); autogear → input (g.adOut, g.gear); autodrive → input (g.adOut); input → top; avoid → apAttitude (autopilot); avoid → capLoaded (manual); apAttitude → capLoaded; capLoaded → driveDecide (autopilot); capLoaded → p3 (manual hover); driveDecide → enterDrive (wheels wanted, occupied, down, |vz| < 4); driveDecide → p3; enterDrive → p3; top → flightGear (autopilot: wanted speed, plan, floor gap, obstacles); flightGear → flightApply (held flight gear (player: gear key)); flightApply → avoid (top capped by the gear)

## B4 Drive actuation on the game's wheels  `#flow-b-s4`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Stage 4 of 8 in B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save. Next: Hover targets and flight-model choice.

- **Steer to the plan's bearing; pedals from its speed** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Speed from position differences; steer = gain x bearing error (eased at a crawl), sign learned over 2 s; accel/brake on signed forward speed (brake only while moving forward; at rest no pedal), reverse = brake pedal + inverted steer; forward boost over kDriveTop 30 m/s.
- **On a Drive lane (rail lane) and plan mode Drive?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385` · log `traffic.rail`): g_driveRail is constant true. Yes: railOn, railVx/Vy along the nose turned at most 0.6 rad toward the aim, railYaw eased 0.3; Cruise boost up to the corner cap; jump key sets railAir. No: rail off.
- **Cruise paid boost: boost key raises the rail speed** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6622`): paidBoost = boost key (or W hold) with the game's boost meter on this vehicle. On Cruise (slot 0 on a rail) it raises the rail target to min(plan speed x boostlogic::kMultiplier, the corner cap) only while top speed alone limits the car (g_railFree); corners still win.
- **Fresh plan path? Follow it (AF-1) and feed the tracking cap (TK-1)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6991` · log `drive.tracking`): Path < 400 ms old: aim and yaw rate from pathfollow::Follow; lateral/heading/rear slip and Sample::grip (PlanGrip of the gear) go to tracking::Update, whose cap lands in g_trackCap, read by the plan's Speed stage next tick. After the native tyre step, slow grounded rail-tyre motion can receive a bounded extra impulse in the requested direction along the road plane. This applies during forward motion and reverse recovery. Traffic holds retain priority.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: data: g_planPath (plan path) in, g_trackCap / g_rearSlipDeg / g_yawMeasured out to the plan's Speed stage
- **Tyre mode (railTyres) on? Pedals instead of velocity writes** (decision, `railtyres.h:71` · log `traffic.tyres`): settings traffic railTyres / railTyresReverse. One signed pedal PI controller on wanted minus forward speed; the rail step then only measures. SP-1 (2026-11-19): the climb push from the road grade along the travel (g_roadGrade) is added at once: clamp(dir x grade x gradeFeed 2.0, 0, gradeFeedMax 0.8); downhill adds nothing. Wheel angle from the bicycle model for the rail's yaw rate either way. After the native tyre step, slow grounded rail-tyre motion can receive a bounded extra impulse in the requested direction along the road plane. This applies during forward motion and reverse recovery. Traffic holds retain priority.
- **TM-1: held throttle kept per direction across stops** (state, `railtyres.h:34` · log `traffic.tyres`): The integral (held) part of the pedal is remembered per direction (fwdHeld / revHeld) when the car stops or reverses and restored when it drives that way again, fading to a third over heldFade 6 s unused; iMax 0.6. Kept through gaps so a slope's push is not rebuilt from zero after each stop.
- **Rail wants to move but no step for 300 ms? Wake the body** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:1124` · log `traffic.rail`): Light throttle (or reverse pedal) plus the game's own activation (read vt+0xB8, write vt+0x140), at most every 250 ms, main thread.
- **drive.wheel: selected speed, actual speed, actuator values** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:7035` · log `OBS system=drive.wheel`): Rail-driven cars every 0.5 s: selected_speed, rail_target, fwd, reverse, accel/brake before the rail, aim error, yaw command; event=no_rail_step when the rail step has not run for 300 ms.
- **Passenger control (kManual)? Your keys replace the plan's** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Command steer/throttle/brake/vertical/boost from passenger.cpp (area D) override the pedals.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: call: passenger control sends kManual commands (PilotCommand) and PlayerHands
- **Autopilot steering capped to the tyres and rate-limited** (action, `wheelgrip.h:154` · log `vehicle.steer`): Cap from speed, wheelbase, steering lock and carcap TyreLat(PlanGrip(gear)) = 6 m/s^2 x the gear's grip (D2); not for passenger manual control.
- **Publish steer/accel/brake/boost/up to the drive mailbox** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:7131`): Read by the input hooks through vehicleflight::DriveFor. After the native tyre step, slow grounded rail-tyre motion can receive a bounded extra impulse in the requested direction along the road plane. This applies during forward motion and reverse recovery. Traffic holds retain priority. The Controller translates the selected motion into wheel or rail commands. It performs no separate wheel-clearance veto or clearance-based pedal rewrite. Explicit holds, normal speed control and braking remain available. Vehicle Coordinator handoff admits the command owner before planning. Revocation on takeover or world reset invalidates old grants. The mailbox transports an admitted command and checks freshness without reading Traffic policy. All pilots use PublishAdmitted for wheel commands. The wheel trace reports selected speed, actual speed and actuator values. Route policy and sensing have their own traces. 6E6B0E74: every pilot publishes through PublishAdmitted with its wheel admission (npcvehicles::AdmitWheelOutput for a Traffic handoff, otherwise a non-revocable grant); a refused or revoked admission clears the mailbox.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: data: DriveFor read by npcvehicles input path (wheels) and passenger.cpp; coordinator handoff admission
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: data: DriveFor read by bindings.cpp to pick ground/hover control context
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: wheel admission from the Vehicle Coordinator handoff (D.wheelAdmit)
- **Input hook: mailbox fresh for this vehicle and grant current?** (decision, `handoff.h:38`): vehicleflight::DriveFor reads each pilot's mailbox: same vehicle, an even version unchanged across the read (two attempts, writers serialized, reader lock-free), and a generation still current (0 = not revocable). Takeover and world reset bump the generation (D.taken, D.wheelReset). No Traffic policy is read here.
- **Out: Fly returns (the game's step drives the wheels)** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Stage exit. Samples and corridors are asked for next frame.

Arrows: steerPedal → railDec; railDec → cruiseBoost (rail on); railFollow → tyresDec (rail target, yaw); railDec → passengerManual (rail off); tyresDec → heldPush (pedals (tyres) or none (rail writes)); wake → passengerManual; passengerManual → publishDrive (manual: your keys); passengerManual → smoothSteer (autopilot); cruiseBoost → railFollow (rail target); heldPush → wake (pedals); smoothSteer → publishDrive (Selected wheel command); publishDrive → mailboxRead (native input hook (DriveFor)); mailboxRead → p4 (fresh and current: the game drives it; else no command); railFollow → wheelTrace (rail drive)

## B5 Hover targets and flight-model choice  `#flow-b-s5`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Stage 5 of 8 in B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save. Next: Physics step hook (physics thread): before and after the game's wheel step.

- **Lift-off near the ground: climb to 5 m first** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Ends at kLiftoffHeight - 0.5 or when already high.
- **Height hold: autopilot cruise height, lift-off, or manual level hold** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): refGround from ground, floor the rays miss and the front sample. Autopilot: nav clearance or groundHug just over the ground, corridor terrain, lane height, goal z, landing; manual: hold where a climb eases out when level and no lift key. Ceiling caps the hold.
- **Under 1 m, not climbing, no thrust for 1.5 s? Landed** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Yes: Stop("landed") (hover off, back to wheels).
- **Autopilot channels: reverse, combat split, held command, landing** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385` · log `plan.combat`): Plan reverse; CB-4 combat facing splits travel into thrust + strafe (planhover::SplitTravel); PilotCommand channels gated by the motion fence; supported touchdown Stop when landed safely; speed over top brakes.
- **Which flight model: physical VTOL, free roll, bank test, stock?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Physical = assist hooks ready AND (player manual with PhysicalVtol, or autopilot with PhysicalAi). FreeRoll only the player's manual hover. Changing model resets the physical state; physical plans with hulllogic::Physical limits.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: settings: features PhysicalVtol, PhysicalAi, FreeRoll, FlightAssist, HoverOrDrive
- **Write the targets into Shared for the physics step** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): thrustAcc, pitch aim, view/yaw want, roll in/aim, lift, strafe, vz, brake, hold/refGround/ground, cap, hull, assist, freeRoll, physical. Under the Shared lock; the physics thread reads them every step.
- **Trip status and HOVER notice** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:3893`): triplogic status for the HUD and ProgressFor; 1 s hover line (speed, height, gear, assist, pitch).
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: HUD: hoverui SetState / SetTripStatus / SetFoot / SetAutoKey and notify lines
- **Out: next physics step flies these targets** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Stage exit. Main thread done; s6 runs on the physics thread.
- **Shared per-slot physics mailbox** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:845`): Data store read by other steps. Main thread writes targets, physics thread writes v/attitude/steps; scene body ids from BindBody (a momentary 0 bodies keeps the binding).
- **Hull limits from the vehicle's size** (state, `hulllogic.h:29`): Data store read by other steps. Yaw/pitch/roll rates, acceleration, brake, lateral from the vehicle's size (For). FC-1: Flight(limits, physical, lateralScale) caps a physical chassis at lateral 9 m/s^2 with brake gain 2, then scales lateral by the flight gear row's turn share (1.33/1.11/1/0.67). YawAt(speed) = carcap::TurnAt(yaw, lateral, speed).
- **Per-gear flight row (FC-1)** (state, `carcap.h:59`): Data store read by other steps. Physical: rate gain 10/7/5/3 /s, angular limit 12/9/6/4 rad/s^2, drift gain 3/2/1.2/0.5 /s, side budget player 12/8/3/2 (AI 12/12/6/4) m/s^2, deadband gear 4 only. Standard: yaw response 8/5.6/4/2.4, tilt 10/7/5/3, side leak = drift gain. Both: turn strength 1.33/1.11/1/0.67, top 30/60/100/150 m/s. Gear 3 = the pre-change flight bit for bit (flightgear_test). TurnAt = min(yaw, lateral / max(4, v)).
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: FlightSpec: hoverYaw / hoverLat / hoverTop for the hover planner (C.flightSpec)

Arrows: liftoff → heightHold; heightHold → landed; landed → apChannels (still flying); apChannels → physSelect; physSelect → publishShared (model choice, targets); publishShared → tripHud; tripHud → p5; publishShared → shared (writes); publishShared → hull (reads)

## B6 Physics step hook (physics thread): before and after the game's wheel step  `#flow-b-s6`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Stage 6 of 8 in B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save. Next: Camera.

- **Hooked native vehicle step (every vehicle, every physics step)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:2110`): Installed on addr::kVehicleStep by vehicleflight::Install. Counts steps, notes the body, observes chassis mass (bodymass).
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: hook: StepDetour on addr::kVehicleStep, plus vtolhooks level/damp/clamp, boost update, camera euler redirects (installed by Install)
- **Which pilot slot owns this body?** (decision, `bodybinding.h:12`): Enabled slots: active, groundOn, fresh rail or guard. Exactly one match by full body id; two claims = nobody (counted as ambiguous).
- **Before the step: velocity jumped from what we wrote? Contact** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:1453`): Change > max(kCollisionDelta, share x speed) sets collided/impactAt; mostly up = collisionUp.
- **Physical requested and motion readable? Own the chassis** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:1324`): Stores physInstance/physBody/physChassis; any other slot holding this instance releases it.
- **BEFORE the wheels: tyre grip and turning weight x gear (GT-1)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:1762` · log `vehicle.grip owner= rule=`): Per vehicle data block: front friction x gearN%, rear x gearN% x rearN%, yaw inverse inertia / weightN%. Defaults gear1 160/130/250, gear2 125/120/175, gear3 game's own, gear4 100/115/150. GO-1 (2026-11-19): the block is the car's own (one per instance); wheelgrip::Want decides one owner if a block is ever shared (player slot 0 owns it; AI cars do not change it while that car is fresh; else the highest ask). A block address reused by another car is read again (block_reused). Failed writes never retried.
- **BEFORE the wheels: traction control (engine torque x factor)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:1624` · log `vehicle.grip`): Off unless [grip] slipN > 0 (all defaults 0) and the car is not under hover or rail velocity writes. GK-3: the engine check uses torque only (the game's rpm fields read ~0/~0/1000).
- **The game's own wheel step runs** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:971`): Seconds forwarded unchanged; custom control skips paused/invalid/long steps (physicsstep::ModelSeconds).
- **AFTER: read the wheels' slip** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:1718`): WheelInfo +0xB4 max |slip| into g_wheelSlip (traction input).
- **Is hover or the rail writing this car's velocity?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:2110`): Shared::active or (railOn and not railTyres): no traction, rear measurement or corner forces.
- **AFTER: measure rear slip angle and yaw flips (RB-1)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:1653` · log `vehicle.grip`): Bicycle-model rear slip angle and yaw rate into g_rearSlipDeg / g_yawMeasured (read by auto gear and tracking). Upright (up.z >= 0.5) only.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: data: rear slip angle and measured yaw rate read by auto gear and tracking
- **AFTER: four corner forces within the gear's budget (CF-1)** (action, `cornerforce.h:66` · log `vehicle.corners`): Turn rate held to the front wheels' angle and the rear slide, never over carcap TyreLat(Input::grip)/v; minimum-norm 3x8 allocation, each corner <= budget/4. Budget [grip] assistN: 6.0/4.0/0.5/2.0 m/s^2 for gears 1-4. Writes velocity and spin. Manual and autopilot alike.
- **No owning slot? Count hand-off steps and return** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:2110` · log `vehicle.model`): A drive-mode pilot's body still counts steps for the traffic hand-off (HandoffPhysics). Otherwise the car model is observed and StepModel runs.
- **StepModel: guard, rail, ground cap or hover?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:2228`): Order: rail clears the guard; release guard; rail (hover off); ground cap (hover off, groundOn, someone driving); else the hover (active).
- **Release guard: zero sideways speed, no launch up** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:2228`): After an autopilot Stop, until kGuardMs or the player drives it.
- **Rail step: velocity along the road plane, then spin** (action, `railforce.h:26` · log `traffic.railprobe`): Accel 6 / brake 9 m/s^2 on the rail's own command, sideways capped, hands off vertical in the air; tipped = no push. Tyre mode: the native tyres drive; when grounded with wheels touching, TyreAssist adds a bounded impulse along the road plane toward the requested direction (at most 0.65 m/s and 0.65 m/s^2 x dt over the measured speed; none if the road normal is steeper than up_z 0.75 or the car moves the other way faster than 0.15 m/s), forward and in reverse recovery (400EAEE6).
- **Manual wheels: ease speed over gear share of learned top (or boost)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:2228`): cap = groundFrac x groundTop (x1.75 paid boost); about 3 % per step.
- **Physical flight on and not faulted?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:2228`): Orientation published first (bodyattitude::Publish) for both models.
- **Physical VTOL: rates -> vtol::Solve -> native impulses** (action, `vtolphysics.h:80`): Rates from vtolai (autopilot), vtolassist (assist, roll < 60 deg) or freeroll aim. Impulses through write vt+0xC0/+0xC8 (checked addresses). Parked wipe 5 steps or a fault -> stock model for that vehicle. FC-1: vtol::Command takes rateGain, angularLimit, sideGain, sideBudget and the gear-4 deadband from the gear row (s.row); Solve bounds the rate gain with StableRateGain = min(gain, max(5, 0.8 / dt)); gear 3 = the old numbers.
- **Stock hover: write spin and velocity after the game's step** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:2228`): Absurd velocities replaced; push-up over 12 m/s = collision, capped 14. Yaw from camera gap (or yawWant) within hull.YawAt; pitch to aim; roll keys/lean/roll aim; freeroll/bank test about body axes. Thrust along nose, strafe, side leak, drag, lift = response + gEst integral, floor clearance, tilt push, speed cap. FC-1: the spin eases at the row's yaw / tilt response (flightgear::Ease) and the side speed leaks at the row's side gain past its deadband (flightgear::SideLeak); gear 3 = 4 /s, 5 /s, 1.2 /s.
- **Out: step result returned to the game** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:2110`): Stage exit. Shared::v/lastV/bodyPitch/bodyRoll/steps feed the next Fly.
- **Measured car: steering lock, wheelbase, input shaping** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:1544` · log `vehicle.model`): Data store read by other steps. Havok offsets read after the step; Shared carLock/carWheelbase feed the rail wheel angle, corner forces and ST-1.
- **settings.ini [grip] per gear (re-read every 2 s)** (state, `wheelgrip.h:103`): Data store read by other steps. gearN friction %, rearN %, weightN %, assistN tenths m/s^2, slipN tenths m/s.
- **Physical chassis: mute upright actions, skip damping and clamp** (action, `vtolhooks.h:5`): Hooks at vtolhooks::kLevel/kDamp/kClamp. Rail cars also skip the game's speed clamp; rail drops are probed.
- **Boost update: hover meter and booster visuals** (action, `boostlogic.h:7`): Hovering: native boost force off, FVO meter on the vehicle's own timings sets g_meterBoost; driving: native boost flag = request. Booster graph bools held while hovering.

Arrows: step → owner; owner → collCheck (owned); owner → grip (not owned (grip by scene body still applies)); collCheck → prepPhys; prepPhys → grip; grip → traction; traction → native; native → slip; slip → gripHands; gripHands → rear (game's wheels move it (manual, drive mode, tyre mode)); gripHands → unowned (hover or rail writes); rear → corners; corners → unowned; unowned → p6 (no owner: hand-off steps only); unowned → stepModel (owned); stepModel → guard (guard and not active); stepModel → rail (railOn and not active); stepModel → groundCap (groundOn, player driving); stepModel → physDec (hover active); physDec → stepPhysical (physical, no fault); physDec → stockHover (stock); stepPhysical → stockHover (fault/wipe: this vehicle falls back next step); guard → p6; rail → p6; groundCap → p6; stepPhysical → p6; stockHover → p6; step → assistHooks (game's level/damp/clamp calls for any vehicle); step → boostHook (game's boost update); grip → gripSettings (reads); unowned → carModel (ObserveCarModel)

## B7 Camera  `#flow-b-s7`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Stage 7 of 8 in B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save. Next: Save, load and restore.

- **Camera read only: heading, pitch aim, rest pitch** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:892`): Data store read by other steps. The camera is never written in hover: its yaw gap turns the nose, its pitch over the learned rest pitch is the nose pitch wanted. Learned only while driving.
- **Publish chassis pitch/roll for the camera (physical only)** (action, `vehiclecamera.cpp:97`): Player slot only; vehicle 0 when not physical (no bank).
- **Fresh (<= 500 ms), physical with you at the wheel, vehicle view?** (decision, `vehiclecamera.cpp:91`): Also read by hoverui's attitude instrument.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: data: hoverui attitude instrument reads vehiclecamera::ReadAttitude
- **Bank the camera rotation with the chassis roll (smoothed)** (action, `cameraroll.h:20`): Redirected euler calls (third person 0xFB6CA2, first person 0xFB68C4); filter rate 10/s, circular.
- **First-person look limits while driving** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:950`): On while a vehicle is driven, off otherwise and on load.
- **Out: camera done** (state, `vehiclecamera.cpp:64`): Stage exit. 

Arrows: camPublish → camGate (render callbacks); camGate → camApply (yes); camGate → p7 (no: native camera); camApply → p7; fpLimits → p7

## B8 Save, load and restore  `#flow-b-s8`  (Detailed flow · B Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save)

Stage 8 of 8 in B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save.

- **Save or load event (game hooks)** (state, `flightsave.cpp:42`): Stage exit. SaveGame/LoadGame hooks in main_vehicle.cpp (area A), installed only with the Traffic addon.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: SaveGame / LoadGame hooks call flightsave::OnSave / OnLoad
- **Save: hovering by hand? Write the record, else delete an old one** (action, `flightsave.cpp:42`): FlightStates\<key>.txt next to FVO settings: 'FVO saved hover v1', vehicle, z, gear. Key = save name lower-case without .sfs.
- **Load: read this save's record as a pending request** (action, `flightsave.cpp:65`): Forgets the old world's hover; unreadable or missing record = load as before.
- **Same vehicle at its wheel within 5 m height, within 15 s?** (decision, `flightsavelogic.h:57`): Wait / Restore / Expired / OtherVehicle / Moved; answered once.
- **Load reset: every pilot and body identity discarded** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:9623`): All State/Shared reset, drive mailboxes and camera cleared, physical released.
- **Out: restore answered (PlayerTick starts the hover)** (state, `flightsave.cpp:83`): Stage exit. 

Arrows: saveLoadEvent → onSave (save); saveLoadEvent → onLoad (load); saveLoadEvent → loadReset (load); onSave → p8; onLoad → restoreDecide (pending request); restoreDecide → p8 (answered); loadReset → p8

## C1 Tick start and drive::Input  `#flow-c-s1`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Every frame, for every pilot slot on autopilot (your Cruise is slot 0, NPC/traffic pilots 1..256), Fly calls AutodriveDecide, which builds one drive::Input (limits, preference, road users, floor) and picks the scene: an authored or job lane, a coordinator hold, or a goal scene with no lane. For a lane car it gathers World (wanted floor from the one map, field boxes, kept hits, coarse boxes, survey predictions, trust, SR-1, doubt probe, field volume, road users, contacts, radar boxes), applies the speed caps (crest, bend, slide, tracking) and hands a copied snapshot to the energy plan worker. The worker (off the game thread) scores the whole candidate library through the car's bicycle model, runs the RV-1 mode machine (Follow/Avoid/Recover/Stand) on what scoring found, masks candidates by mode, picks the lowest energy and lets the Arbiter hold the committed plan. Step 7 then takes the newest fresh result for this car as the only decision (path follower or hover follower, eased speed), or stands. EnergyStep runs last: one radar tick (rays + hull sweep, judged by SR-1 at sensing) and, for a car with no lane, the goal World, goal plan and goal step 7. The rail actuator in B follows the path and TK-1 measures tracking, whose cap the Speed stage reads next tick. Next: 2 Scene: lane, job line, hold or goal. Oddities found when mapping: ORDER: the radar ticks in EnergyStep at the END of AutodriveDecide, after the lane branch has already gathered World and submitted, so lane cars plan on the previous tick's radar boxes (goal cars get the fresh ones). The plan is asynchronous: stage 5 runs on a pool worker and step 7 reads whatever result is newest (<= 500 ms); stages 4 -> 5 -> 6 are not one in/one out within a frame. MODE AFTER SEARCH: the RV-1 design (decision-graph.html P_N 'fs': Follow needs no full search) is not what the code does: energyplan::Plan scores the whole library (up to 700) every plan, then recovery::Update decides the mode from those scores and masks; refineRounds is 0 so Avoid never refines either. TWO SURFACE RULES: SR-1 (surface.h, grip x gear climb limit, about 35 deg in gear 3) judges radar hits and kept lane ray hits, and since D2 (2026-11-19) energyplan::Score rejects too_steep_for_wheels at the same carcap MaxGrade(grip); the field-slice volume keeps its own ground rules (groundTolerance, terrain layer = clear, nz > 0.65 floor) and mayStop/soft flags (its pass height and low-edge verdict were removed by E), and planinput still makes !mayStop shapes soft (RT-1 said removed). SR-1 'up' is world z (nz), not the gravity vector its header describes. GOAL DRIVING GAPS: goal cars (no lane) get no SC-1/BC-1/slide caps, no TK-1 tracking (tracking only runs inside the rail block for in_railLane cars), no GR-1 grip weight and no plan.mode trace (PlanGoal does not log layer changes). DEAD / UNUSED: g_shadowLaneAt is never written, so EnergyStep's 'lane' test is always false (the owner string decides); planrays::Budget g_rayBudget is declared and never used; roadfield.h is included by nothing; navmesh tiles are baked (Want) but no driving code calls a navmesh path any more (the comment 'asked only when no progress' at the navmesh line has no code under it); navfield (flight field) is read only by environment.cpp CombatPosition (area D); worldclass.h survives only for tireHeight (now 2 x carcap climb) and routeitems' warning names; planspeed.h is used only by the traffic addon. COMMENT-SWALLOWED CODE: in the lane branch 'g.adOut.mode=...;  // NH-1: never a hover start on drive onlyg.adOut.phase=drive::Phase::Travel;' puts the phase assignment inside the comment. NOT ON THE TICK (mapped as data/links only): trustzone.h (traffic generator, D), laneplan/lanetransform/lanevolume/journeyplan/corridorprofile (addon lane data, E/D; corridorprofile gates routeguidance), routeprobes/autoroute (editor, E), mappoints (scanner marker, F), spawnfloor (spawn check, D), cellfit/loadedarea (cell listing and loaded-area wall pass, A; loadedarea also names hit body owners), navprobe/navbake (diagnostic and bake). trustzone.cpp and planview.cpp do not exist (planview is implemented in vehicleflight.cpp). Two hover-follower call sites (lane step 7 and goal step 7) and two FollowSpeed call sites duplicate the same step-7 logic. STAGE PORTS: stage 8 (tracking) has no port of its own: it ends in the g_trackCap store that stage 4 reads next tick; stage 9 is data only. Node count (118) is above the 90 guide because the RV-1 machine, the SR-1 rule and the World gather each have several real decisions.

- **Fly: one pilot's autopilot update this frame** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Runs for every active pilot slot (0 = your vehicle on Cruise/summon, 1..256 NPC and traffic pilots). Reads position, heading, body velocity and ground samples, then the autopilot decision.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: NPC jobs, traffic pilots and combat drive through the same Fly / AutodriveDecide / energy plan (PilotFly, PilotCommand set goals; slots 1..256)
- **Autopilot ended while the car is on its wheels?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385` · log `OBS system=vehicle.handover`): HO-1: g.drive && !g.autopilot && g.autoOnWheels (Cruise started on the wheels, CW-1). A drive key ends Cruise before this point.
- **Stop: hand the car back on its wheels, hover off** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:3763` · log `OBS system=vehicle.handover`): Nothing is planned or sent this frame. Without autoOnWheels, LeaveDrive releases the controls instead.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: HO-1 hand-back: Stop / LeaveDrive put the car back on its wheels with hover off
- **Autopilot on for this pilot?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Removal 2026-11-14: the energy plan decides heading, speed, wheels or hover for every car on autopilot; a command (combat, job hold) overrides only the channels it sets.
- **AutodriveDecide: build this frame's drive::Input** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Goal (x,y,z, stop, keepGoing, hold, cruise speed), position, velocity (wheels: heading x drive speed), measured ground. Cost timed per pilot.
- **Hard limits: may it drive, hover, or both?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Your travel profile Drive/Hover holds; Hover-or-drive feature off -> hover only; health < 30% -> drive only and hover impossible; ceiling room < 6 m -> drive only.
- **What the moment prefers: air or ground?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): First match: fighting, fleeing, catching you in the air -> air; traffic lane -> the lane's travel kind (1 drive, 2 hover); final approach < 30 m, settlement -> ground; long empty summon > 150 m -> air; else the profile. A preference, not a limit.
- **Paused, held on purpose, or no physics steps?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=autopilot.steps`): in.paused = play clock stopped, a seat brake command, or 1.5 s without counted physics steps while still (a moving car is simulated). NPC driverSeated re-read every 0.5 s.
- **Claim owner and rank (Cruise / Combat / Traffic / Job)** (action, `planowner.h:48` · log `OBS system=plan.owner`): One record per vehicle; a weaker owner never takes a vehicle a stronger one holds (refused, traced). Rebuilt each frame, never saved.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: owner/rank record exported (FVO_OwnerSnapshotV2) for the addon coordinator
- **Road users from the vehicle snapshot; own measured shape** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=vehicle.size source=measured|wheels|bounds`): Own half sizes, rest offset (fieldgate::kOriginAbove), measured steering lock / wheelbase (0 until read). MS-1 (2026-11-19): the own size is measured from the own collision box (radar::OwnSizeFrom, kept per slot in g_ownSize by RadarStep), at least the widest wheel hardpoint g_wheelHalf + kTyreHalf 0.25 across; bounds only when neither is known. Others: pose, velocity, radius, parked flag, right-of-way priority.
- **MS-1: own car size from its collision box, else wheels, else bounds** (action, `radar.h:174` · log `OBS system=vehicle.size`): RadarStep measures the own collision box along and across the heading once per car (g_ownSize, event=measured / measure_failed); AutodriveDecide takes max(measured, widest wheel hardpoint g_wheelHalf + kTyreHalf 0.25) as half width; bounds are the last fallback. One car (D2): Plan, radar and Plan's wheel-clearance vote use this one size.
- **Addon road floor answers first on its own lane** (action, `fieldgate.h:35` · log `OBS system=traffic.surface`): FvoRoadSurfaceV1: 1 authored road (floor height replaces the game ground), 2 lift/open air. The measured game ground is kept separately for GroundLift.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: FvoRoadSurfaceV1 authored road floor / lift answer
- **drive::Input ready** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Stage exit. 

Arrows: fly → handover; handover → backwheels (yes); handover → ap (no); ap → decide (yes); decide → permit; permit → prefer (permit); prefer → hold (preferAir); hold → owner (in.paused); owner → others; others → ownSize; addonFloor → p1; ownSize → addonFloor

## C2 Scene: lane, job line, hold or goal  `#flow-c-s2`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 2 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions. Next: 3 World gather for a lane car.

- **Authored lane frame for this pilot?** (decision, `routeguidance.h:91` · log `OBS system=lane.repair`): The addon's lane command (points, half width, speed, travel, held, stopDistance, policy). Every command enters through one repair: folds removed, corners sharper than kRoundTurn filleted (LF-1). Traffic supplies lane geometry, travel permissions, speed, hold reason and stop distance through Route guidance. World supplies obstacles and contacts. Speed and Plan process those inputs through the ordinary driving pipeline. 6E6B0E74: Route guidance is the one way Traffic's lane geometry, travel permissions, speed, hold reason and stop distance reach Speed and Plan.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: lane frames, policy, travel kind, held/stop line from the addon via routeguidance (FvoLaneGuidanceV1)
- **On wheels and a Drive lane within the 2 s grace?** (decision, `routeguidance.h:96` · log `OBS system=traffic.lane`): A late lane command keeps the last Drive lane up to 2 s instead of dropping the car to the coordinator hold.
- **NPC job or combat goal may become one line (GD-1)?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5429` · log `OBS system=plan.door`): Only slots >= 1 with no addon lane frame, on wheels (or a combat line), not scripted, farther than handBack from the goal. Refusals traced only for job/combat goals.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: job and combat goals (goal door, combat aim) become GD-1 lines
- **Build the job line over the nav map's ground** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5433` · log `OBS system=plan.door`): Rebuilt when the goal moves or every rebuildEvery s; combat lines keep the fight height in the air (CB-3). Becomes authoredLane for the rest of the tick.
- **Lane scene: rail car on wheels, or plan_flies (3D line)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=plan.hover`): railCar = on wheels and travel != 2. Off its wheels on a drive-only lane (NH-1) the lane stays a ground line and the car only comes down; Both/Hover lanes are 3D flight lines. Road plane normal from the lane grade (railforce).
- **Far traffic with no 3D: moved along its lane directly** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:478` · log `OBS system=traffic.far`): Slots >= 1 with no model: position written along the lane at min(lane speed, top); 0 within 14 m behind another car; stop line braking at 4 m/s^2.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: far traffic without a model moved along its lane (FarWrite)
- **Scene chosen** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Stage exit. 
- **Strict coordinator policy without a lane?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:165`): A traffic pilot whose policy says the coordinator holds it.
- **Coordinator hold: brake, phase Boxed** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=traffic.boundary`): driverOwner coordinator_hold; reason from routeguidance::Refusal.
- **No lane: goal scene, neutral controls until EnergyStep** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Job, summon, escort, combat, your trip: driverOwner energy_plan_goal. EnergyStep plans and drives it.

Arrows: lane → laneScene (yes); lane → grace (no); grace → laneScene (yes: last lane kept); grace → jobq (no); jobq → jobBuild (yes); jobBuild → laneScene (line built); jobBuild → strict (line_not_built); jobq → strict (refused); strict → coordHold (yes); strict → goalScene (no); laneScene → farMove (slot >= 1, no 3D model); laneScene → p2; farMove → p2

## C3 World gather for a lane car  `#flow-c-s3`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 3 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions. Next: 4 Speed caps, goal door, submit.

- **Gather World this update?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=plan.cost`): Your car every update; others every 0.25 s. Step 7 below runs every update either way.
- **Spot test step (health pulse request)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:607` · log `OBS system=spot.test`): Seated NPC on wheels, unseen: asks the energy plan for a short pulse (D12); the plan scores it like any candidate and may refuse it.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: spot test / pair health sample and self-reports read by the traffic generator
- **Wanted surface along the line (24 samples)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): The addon road near the expected level (within 4 m), else the car's own level carried along the lane's rise; a flight line wants ground only where measured near it.
- **Addon field boxes: Touch level or open air** (action, `fieldboxapi.h:3` · log `OBS system=plan.field`): Up to 12 oriented tile boxes; a Touch bottom picks the level among stacked decks, no Touch face = nothing wanted. lineEnd/arrive heading feed the goal door.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: FvoFieldBoxV1 field boxes and line end from the addon's route keys
- **C1: the wheels' own floor corrects samples ahead** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Touching ground on wheels: an offset over contactTrust is faded over contactCarry m ahead (the 11:38 run's 1.1 m phantom wall).
- **WM-1: each sample's height from the reachable map floor** (action, `routesurvey.cpp:273` · log `OBS system=plan.surface`): Contact floor noted into the map; reachable levels flooded from the car (lip 0.25 m + grade 1.0 x distance, every 0.5 s, 60 m); sample z = reachable floor within wantWindow 2 m. WM-2: only down-ray floor enters the map (hull contacts do not); flood lip and bend 0.25 (planrules mapBend).
- **Field containment and share (CH-1)** (action, `fieldbox.h:92`): Containment starts only once the whole car is inside the chain; faces never keep a car out.
- **Field slice is the fixed-obstacle source here?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): useFieldSlice = field boxes present, a surface wanted under the car, obstacles policy != 2. Otherwise the coarse sources below run, plus the extra volume (F1) where no road is under the line.
- **Kept ray hits + coarse map objects (whole boxes WB-1)** (action, `navmap.cpp:124` · log `OBS system=world.boxes`): Every 2nd sample across the span: kept hits in -1..+3.5 m; 4 m object cells become the game's whole box when tall and object-sized, else 2 m band cells; swept-clear ground overrides the coarse cell.
- **Survey's swept solids added as predictions** (action, `routesurvey.cpp:241` · log `OBS system=world.sweep`): Clear-path sweep solids along the line, far ahead; live rays outrank them.
- **Fix B: count agreeing ray hits per spot** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): One stray hit is doubt; several within confirmRadius are a real surface (Shape.support).
- **T1: a prediction a clean ray or measured hit disproves is dropped** (action, `worldtrust.h:65` · log `OBS system=world.trust`): Only Survey and Box shapes are dropped; trust order contact > users > confirmed rays > rays > sweep > box. With rays down nothing is dropped.
- **Drop Drivable and Below lane ray shapes** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4534` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): Run retained lane rays through SurfaceOf. Remove Drivable and Below shapes before planning. Below does not supply floor evidence. Actual blockers and unknowns keep their existing shape policy.
- **Look-ahead doubt probe: direct rays at doubtful spots** (action, `planprobe.h:36` · log `OBS system=plan.probe`): Low unsure hits and unmeasured floor between stopping distance and 40 m; 3 rays each (down, through, 0.2 m lower) within a per-car share of the frame budget; answers go into navmap and the survey floor for next frame. Floor confidence for a blocking point is its own step (ProbeBlockerFloor / ApplyFloorProofs, the floorConf node).
- **Vehicles and people within 90 m (riders skipped)** (action, `planworld.h:51` · log `OBS system=plan.people`): People only within 40 m; a person inside this or another vehicle's body is a rider (RD-1/RD-2). Moving users become movers in the scene.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: World's vehicles and people (published plans, steerRound, parked) from the vehicle registry
- **Contact events become shapes at the nose** (action, `contactevent.h:21`): Pushing without moving keeps a shape for 8 s so the next plans go round it (G6).
- **Field volume: beams across the field become shapes** (action, `fieldvolume.h:70` · log `OBS system=world.volume`): Columns x levels to Depth(speed, brake), phased and carried between refreshes; unmeasured space is no information. Ground rules (groundTolerance 0.15, terrain layers 13/17, nz > 0.65) still say where the floor is. E (2026-11-19): the volume reports measured hits only, each a shape at the height it was hit (one face per column within rules::face); its own pass height, straight-grade floor, Cell::Low and low counts are removed, so what is climbed or met is Plan's against World's road (RF-1). VT-1: a solid is 0.1 m half (rules::point), the measured hit, not the column's 0.5 m width.
- **Append radar shapes with a local floor recheck** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4864` · log `OBS system=world.radar event=shapes / event=below_point`): Before a lane snapshot is handed to Plan, World rechecks exported radar points against the fitted, reachable floor at each point's own XY. It withholds a point only when it is more than one wheel step under that floor, within the existing 4 m search, and there is no measured competing floor in the vehicle clearance band. The cache retains the original evidence, so it can count again from another driving level. Whole boxes are appended directly. The lane can read points retained from earlier updates.
- **Floor confidence: is this blocking point under a fitted road?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:4769` · log `OBS system=world.floor_confidence / OBS system=plan.floor_confidence`): Player lane: a held static ray point blocking the centre path below 2 m/s gets five down-rays 0.45 m round it (main thread). surface::FitProbedFloor clears it when the fitted reachable road lies wholly above it (error <= 0.10, grade <= carcap MaxGrade) and a connected sample is on the hit's own body or all five are connected (FC-2), with no competing lower floor. A cleared point is a verified floor point for 3 s; ApplyFloorProofs removes matching points from the lane snapshot, and Recover's Stand may return to Follow at once (verified_floor_retry; wheel command held to 1.25 m/s for 0.75 s).
- **RF-1: give each static shape the road surface under it** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4826` · log `OBS system=world.road_fit (fitted rough weak none over_climb); plan.blocker road= road_spread=`): For static shapes up to 1 m half size within 45 m (movers and wide boxes left), routesurvey::RoadFit fits the car's reachable floor 2 cells round the shape (Store::FitSurface: plane fit, points over 3 spreads set aside, refit; ok needs 5 points over both axes and a drivable grade). A spread over half the climb is rough ground and is not given. Writes Shape::road / roadSpread; Plan judges against it (within 2.5 spreads = road pattern, up to climb = low edge, above = solid).
- **Standing or full-speed plan blocked 2 s: trace and scene dump** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4993` · log `OBS system=plan.blocked`): Diagnostic only: first solid along the lane, plan.choices, SD-1 whole-scene dump.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: diagnostic OBS traces (plan.blocked, plan.scene, plan.choices) for the run logs
- **World shapes + wanted floor ready** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Stage exit. 
- **SD-1: dump the whole planner scene when blocked** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:6208` · log `OBS system=plan.scene`): With every blocked report, plan.scene writes the car, heading and size, then each shape within 40 m with its role (mover, soft, hard); read with notes/scene-dump-20261118/scene_view.py.

Arrows: gatherQ → spot (yes); spot → wantLine; wantLine → fieldBoxes; fieldBoxes → contactFloor; contactFloor → mapFloor; mapFloor → contain (wantZ, wantMeasured); contain → sliceQ; sliceQ → coarse (no); sliceQ → users (yes: field slice is the fixed source); coarse → sweepPred; sweepPred → support; support → trust; trust → sr1lane; sr1lane → probe (rays live); probe → users; users → contacts; volume → radarIn (volume shapes); radarIn → floorConf; blocked → p3; floorConf → roadFit (verified points removed); roadFit → blocked (shapes with road / roadSpread); blocked → sceneDump (blocked 2 s)

## C4 Speed caps, goal door, submit  `#flow-c-s4`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 4 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions. Next: 5 Plan worker: candidates, RV-1 mode, Arbiter.

- **Wanted speed = lane speed vs car top** (action, `planrules.h:191`): min(lane speed, top) with traffic at boostShare of top. PD-1c names the hold (heldWhy) and the lane limit.
- **Ground lane (not a flight line, not flying)?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Caps apply to wheel lanes only; flight lines and goal scenes get none.
- **SC-1 crest cap from the measured floor's bend** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5857` · log `OBS system=plan.speedcap`): v^2 x downward bend <= 0.6 g, reachable by braking at 3 m/s^2 to the crest; only measured samples.
- **BC-1 bend cap from the lane's curvature ahead** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5862` · log `OBS system=plan.speedcap`): planbranch::rules::BendCap: curvature from yaw change over 2 steps; v = sqrt(carcap PlanLat(grip) / curvature), PlanLat = 3.0 m/s^2 x the gear's grip (D2: was a separate 3.5), brought back to the car by braking at crestBrake. The lower of bend and crest.
- **SG-1: steep road down ahead? cap to what the slope allows** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5867` · log `OBS system=plan.speedcap grade_cap=`): Measured floor grade over ~4 m (samples i, i+2), downhill only (uphill is the tyre push's, SP-1). planspeed::GradeCap = SlopeLimit(top, grade) brought back by braking at crestBrake minus g x sin(slope) (never under 0.5 m/s^2). The lowest of crest, bend and grade wins; wantWhy 'steep road down ahead'.
- **Sliding over 1.5 m/s sideways or off its wheels?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5873` · log `OBS system=plan.speedcap`): Yes: hold max(3, 0.7 x speed) for 1.5 s.
- **want = min(want, crest/bend/grade, slide, tracking cap)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5881` · log `OBS system=plan.speedcap`): The tracking cap is the one TK-1 stored last tick; wantWhy names the binding cap (tracking, sliding, steep road down, bend, crest).
- **AG-1 auto gear from rear slip and skids; road grade stored** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5891` · log `OBS system=vehicle.gear`): Autopilot cars on wheels pick their gear from rear slip and sideways skid only (SP-1: the grade no longer picks a gear). The road grade over the next 2 samples is stored in g_roadGrade[idx] for the tyre controller's grade push (SP-1) and the vehicle.gear trace. The gear's grip feeds SR-1's climb limit and the plan's grip (carcap).
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: AG-1 gear choice; g_roadGrade to the tyre controller's grade push (railtyres, SP-1); gear grip read back by SR-1 and the plan through carcap (wheelgrip::PlanGrip)
- **Lane goal through the goal door: ending and facing** (action, `goaldoor.h:50` · log `OBS system=plan.door.lane`): Pass / stop / arrive; arrive within 20 m sets the final facing; combat lines face the aim point (CB-4).
- **Road lanes and coordinator lane permits** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5935` · log `OBS system=plan.permit`): The whole road at the car (up to 8 lanes, sanity-checked) so the plan may pass through other lanes; a lane the coordinator closes is not a lane this update (own lane never closed). Known edge bands and red dividers: the add-on reports their widths in FvoRoadLanesV1 bandLow/bandHigh (roadlanes.h; roadfield.h LanesAtFull with a size-safe LanesAt wrapper so an older caller is never overrun); planbranch::Lanes carries them. The 1.0 m lane plausibility check judges the untrimmed outer lane (RoadLanesPlausible) so a band-trimmed lane is not rejected.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: FvoRoadLanesV1 road lanes and coordinator lane permits (lanecoord) from the traffic addon
- **Your car with a measured steering model?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=plan.energy`): Slot 0 with steerLock and wheelbase: Submit (pool-scored, every gather). Else SubmitLane within the budget: 4 plans in flight, each car every 0.2 s.
- **FC-1: hover planner limits from the craft's flight gear row** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:2812` · log `OBS system=plan.flightspec`): Before each lane and goal submit: spec.hoverYaw = hull yaw, hoverLat = hull lateral (hulllogic::Flight with the row's turn share), hoverTop = min(frame top, row top). NoteFlightPlan stores the lane's wanted speed and the committed hover path's PeakTurn for FG-1. NoteFlightPlan sits just above it (vehicleflight.cpp).
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: reads the craft's flight row and hull (g_sh.row / g_sh.hull) written by Fly; feeds FG-1 (B.flightGear)
- **Copied snapshot handed to the plan worker** (action, `planenergy.h:518` · log `OBS system=plan.cost`): Frame, 101 line points, 24 floor samples, field boxes, shapes, bicycle state and spec. A busy worker means this update's data is not used. The lane scene keeps the original measured floor for collision and low-edge checks. Its grade profile may correct a suspect leading sample before extrapolating behind the car, when three contiguous forward measurements agree on a local slope and the extrapolated height is consistent with the measured car base.
- **Snapshot submitted** (state, `planenergy.h:511`): Stage exit. Asynchronous hand-off: the result is read by step 7 when it is published.
- **Sensor traces for the exact lane snapshot Plan used** (action, `planenergy.h:335` · log `OBS system=world.sensors / OBS system=plan.blocker road= road_spread=`): world.sensors counts inputs by sensor (radar rotating / forward rays, hull sweeps, lane rays, volume) and measured floor samples, by vehicle and snapshot_s; plan.blocker names the exact fastest-centre blocker (sensor, source, owner, body, layer, age, normal, tile) with road= and road_spread= from RF-1.

Arrows: want → wheelsQ; wheelsQ → crest (yes); wheelsQ → autogear (no: no caps); crest → bend; bend → gradeCap; slide → speedMin (slide cap or none); speedMin → autogear; autogear → door; door → lanes (facing, line end); submitQ → submit (yes: Submit / no: SubmitLane in budget); submit → p4; gradeCap → slide; submitQ → flightSpec (hover spec from the flight row); flightSpec → submit (spec.hoverYaw / hoverLat / hoverTop); submit → sensorTrace (player lane snapshot)

## C5 Plan worker: candidates, RV-1 mode, Arbiter  `#flow-c-s5`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 5 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions. Next: 6 Step 7 for a lane car: one authority.

- **PlanWork on the thread pool, one memory per slot** (action, `planenergy.h:368` · log `OBS system=plan.energy`): Memory and field reset when another car takes the slot or it switches between lane and goal scenes. Goal jobs go to PlanGoal. When a verified point left the snapshot and the full-speed centre candidate is feasible, Recover's Stand returns to Follow at once (verified_floor_retry) and the wheel command is held to 1.25 m/s for 0.75 s. Grade uses nine samples and a two-pass fit. A stationary candidate may hold its starting position, and a car already above its grade limit may move toward a gentler grade. A sustained climb above the existing grade limit remains infeasible. plan.choices reports raw and fitted starting grade, sample counts, and corrections. World records recent successful, unsaturated central hull-sweep spans that contain no retained blocking contact. The sweep can follow the published reverse rollout as well as the forward rollout. Remembered sweep evidence helps select a checkpoint and carries no clearance authority over the current scene.
- **Scene from the snapshot (one physical rule per shape)** (action, `planinput.h:40`): Movers (moving users with predicted boxes), soft (predictions and !mayStop: cost, never a block), solids over their real footprint; floor, field, route, stop station. PS-1: a measured ray point is kPointHalf 0.05 m half (ray and pose error), not the shape's box. RP-1: the lane floor is the line between neighbouring measured samples (no staircase); gaps over two samples are not bridged. Known no-go (route edge bands and medians) enters the scene as wall solids (planscene.h BuildKnownBandSolids, built before Index(), fed by planinput.h KnownLegal / Scene::legal): the cost-to-go, the corridor margin and the rollout refusal then treat them exactly like a sensed wall; their blockers are source=unattributed.
- **GR-2: repair a suspect starting sample in the grade profile** (action, `planinput.h:20`): Three contiguous forward measurements agreeing on a local slope, with the extrapolated height consistent with the car base, replace a suspect leading sample in scene.gradeFloor only; collision and low-edge checks keep the measured floor. Samples set aside are counted (floorSetAside).
- **RP-1: judge each solid against the road at its own position** (action, `planscene.h:424`): Solid::road is World's RF-1 fit, else the lane floor under the solid (found once in Index). It counts only within kMaxBandGrade 0.84 (tan 40 deg) x distance + 0.35 of the body's floor (another switchback level is not); unknown keeps the flat floor. Movers use MoverRef; hovering bodies stay flat (tilt off).
- **CC-1/CC-2: shift the followed line off measured edges** (action, `corridor.h:95` · log `OBS system=plan.corridor shift target left right edges remembered memory why`): Lane scenes on wheels: hard edges beside the lane 2 m behind to 15 m ahead (solids in the body band, judged against their road) shift the line by the least amount that keeps 0.5 m; too narrow for both margins -> midway; a solid within the centre zone is Avoid's, not an edge. Eased at 0.6 m/s with a 0.1 m hold (Ease, ShiftRoute). CC-2: each car remembers measured edge points (Memory, 0.5 m cells, 256 kept); Forget drops them only when passed by 5 m, over 40 m away, 30 s unseen or shown to be another level. Not seeing an edge never clears it.
- **Cost-to-go field stale (edge 14 m, 4 s, goal < 8 m)?** (decision, `planenergy.h:121` · log `OBS system=plan.energy`): Lane goal 30 m ahead along the route. Budgeted cars: one field build at a time.
- **Rebuild the cost-to-go field in the background** (action, `planlattice.h:61` · log `OBS system=plan.energy`): Backward Dijkstra over cells x heading x gear with this car's turning primitives, around every solid, so a reverse is chosen only when it shortens the real way.
- **Candidate library (full set, up to 700)** (action, `energyplan.h:642`): Forward arcs at 7 speeds, bends, reverse then forward, three-point turns, drive then stop (stop ahead), follow lane at 5 speeds x offsets 0/+-1 m, hover family (where hover allowed), kept (time-shifted committed plan).
- **Hover rollouts turn at min(hoverYaw, hoverLat / v)** (action, `bicycle.h:59`): FC-1: HoverStep and the energy plan's hover rollouts turn the nose at carcap::TurnAt(hoverYaw, hoverLat, v) (speed under 4 m/s counts as 4), the same formula the hull uses; the fixed 1.6 rad/s hover turn is gone.
- **Score movement and recovery, including clearance tiers** (action, `energyplan.h:335` · log `OBS system=plan.choices`): Managed lanes score ordinary and recovery motion in one candidate set. Crawl, steering, reverse, and an available breadcrumb retrace compete. Static and moving contact, predicted grade, and terminal braking room raise the clearance tier. A 100-point tier step dominates the bounded preference score. Equal tiers use the original motion costs and recovery preferences. A better tier releases commitment immediately. Explicit Traffic holds, route stop lines, and travel or capability restrictions keep their enforcement. Goal scenes and health pulses retain their existing rules. The short wheel-clearance check evaluates the first wheel command of each ordinary rollout on the same World snapshot. Its suggested speed and reason contribute to Plan's score, including a clearance tier on managed lanes. A zero suggestion is eligible for selection. The Arbiter owns the choice and the Controller publishes its result. A rollout whose body enters a known band or median is refused by the same collision rule as any wall, with reject reason enters_a_known_band (a sensed wall is enters_a_solid); a car already touching a band alone may take a path that does not carry its centre deeper (kNoGoTolerance 0.03 m, kNoGoRise 0.05 m); trace plan.nogo.
- **GR-2: start grade fit; may this candidate hold or ease off a steep start?** (decision, `energyplan.h:256` · log `OBS system=plan.choices raw_start_grade start_grade grade_points grade_set_aside max_grade floor_set_aside`): FitGrade: nine samples over the car's length, two-pass fit with outliers set aside. A candidate over carcap MaxGrade is rejected unless it holds its start (under 0.1 m/s, within 0.1 m) or the car already starts over the limit and the candidate moves to a grade at least 0.02 gentler; a sustained climb over the limit stays infeasible. On managed lanes the excess grade raises the clearance tier.
- **Wheel-clearance vote on each rollout's first wheel command (WG-2)** (action, `wheelguard.h:32` · log `OBS system=plan.choices wheel_vote_speed wheel_vote_reason clearance_tier`): 865DFD5C: inside Score, wheelguard::Check sweeps each ordinary rollout's first wheel command to its stopping distance on the same snapshot; its suggested speed and reason add collision cost (12 x deficit / demand) and, on managed lanes, a clearance tier (10 under 0.1 m/s). WG-2: the 0.15 m margin and the 1.25 m/s crawl on an unverified scene only for a constrained move (clearance under 0.65 m, not following, or an offset). No veto or pedal rewrite after the Controller.
- **RV-1: meant to stand (want < 0.5, stop < 10 m, queued)?** (decision, `recovery.h:97` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): Rule 1: never recovers while it is supposed to wait; resets tries and the progress clock. Queued = the full-speed centre plan rejected by a road user.
- **Flight stretch (travel = 2)?** (decision, `recovery.h:97` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): Rule 2: flight stretches plan freely; no ground routine in the air.
- **Update motion context** (decision, `recovery.h:97` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): On managed lanes Follow, Avoid and Recover supply scoring context and do not mask the candidate set. Goal scenes and active holds retain the existing filters. SP-1c: on a measured climb (road grade >= 0.15) with the centre clear and the car moving forward >= 0.1 m/s, the no-progress clock holds (recovery.h:155).
- **Start Recover: anything safe, tries left?** (decision, `recovery.h:138` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): Nothing feasible and no reverse -> Stand nothing_safe; 3 tries -> Recover Hover if hover allowed else Stand; else BackUp (or TurnToLane when reverse is blocked).
- **BackUp 3 m at 1.5 m/s -> TurnToLane -> Retry** (state, `recovery.h:57` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): BackUp ends at 3 m, 4 s or reverse blocked; TurnToLane (steer = -2 x heading error at 1.5 m/s) ends under 0.35 rad or 4 s; Retry hands back to Follow or Avoid. Backing must be won back before it counts as progress.
- **Recovery contributes candidates and preference** (state, `energyplan.h:147` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): The measured breadcrumb return path is an ordinary candidate. A clearer forward or turning move can win over a preferred retrace.
- **Follow supplies lane-centre preference** (state, `recovery.h:13` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): On managed lanes all moving candidates remain eligible. A goal or held scene retains its prior mode rules.
- **Avoid considers offset and steering alternatives** (state, `recovery.h:14` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): Avoid can refine the best rollouts when refinement is enabled. It uses the same tier comparison for managed lanes.
- **Stand context or enforced hold** (state, `recovery.h:17` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): A managed lane without a Traffic hold uses the ranked motion set. When no permitted command exists, the previous brake fallback remains.
- **Mask candidates by mode, pick the lowest energy** (action, `energyplan.h:3` · log `OBS system=plan.mode`): Masked ones get rejects follow_mode_lane_centre_only / stand_mode / recover_hover_only / recover_step_only. The whole library was scored before the mode was known.
- **Any finite candidate?** (decision, `energyplan.h:3`): 
- **Stand plan: every candidate enters a solid (or mode stands)** (state, `energyplan.h:3` · log `OBS system=plan.choices`): Speed 0 is always physically possible; memory cleared.
- **Arbiter: keep the committed plan or switch?** (decision, `energyplan.h:3` · log `OBS system=plan.switch`): Switch at once if the kept plan enters a solid, is within 0.5 m of a solid and the best has more room, got worse by 3 (5 for a direction change) x 0.3 past its smallest gap, or the challenger yields (slower in 1-2 s). Else a challenger cheaper by 0.3 for 0.4 s, not within 0.6 s of the last switch.
- **Publish Result: driven path, hover path, speed 0.5 s ahead** (action, `planenergy.h:26` · log `OBS system=plan.energy`): Path up to the first gear change (points 0.3 m apart); hover path = whole rollout; first command through follow-lane pure pursuit (LF-1); the turn limit is carcap PlanLat(grip) at the gear's grip (D2; the separate latAccel weight is gone). In eligible forward, on-wheels Follow, Plan publishes a stable lane-centre steering reference for the wheel follower. The reference uses the same scene to check sampled static clearance before switching. Radar and other trajectory consumers continue to use the collision-scored rollout. The wheel follower uses the rollout when the lane reference is ineligible.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: planview: PlanLedgerLine / PlanBranchesView read the newest result for F7
- **Lane-centre steering reference for forward wheel Follow** (action, `planenergy.h:67` · log `OBS system=plan.follow reference=lane_centre`): In eligible forward on-wheels Follow, the result carries a stable lane-centre path for the wheel follower, checked for sampled static clearance in the same scene; radar and other consumers keep the collision-scored rollout; otherwise the follower uses the rollout.
- **Newest result stored for the slot** (state, `planenergy.h:580`): Stage exit. 
- **RT-1: hits world truth says never stop the car are soft** (decision, `planinput.h:40`): A prediction or a shape with mayStop false (an explained road or terrain hit small enough to drive over) is a soft cost, never a block; walls, structures, unknown, loose objects, vehicles and people keep mayStop and stay solids.

Arrows: worker → sceneBuild (lane snapshot); sceneBuild → roadRef; fieldStale → lattice (yes (second worker)); fieldStale → cands (no: newest field); lattice → cands (field (when built)); cands → score; score → mStand (energies, rejects, centre/any/reverse feasible, progress); mStand → follow (yes); mStand → mFlight (no); mFlight → avoid (yes); mFlight → mState (no); mState → follow (stays Follow / Avoid clear 1.5 s / Stand progress); mState → avoid (centre blocked, infeasible, no lane / Stand 5 s); mState → startRec (no progress 6 s / nothing feasible 2 s); mState → recSteps (in Recover); mState → stand (stays Stand); startRec → recover (tries < 3, or 3 tries with hover); startRec → stand (nothing safe / 3 tries, no hover); recSteps → recover (held step); recSteps → follow (Retry, centre clear); recSteps → avoid (Retry, centre not clear); recSteps → stand (hover not allowed / hover no progress); follow → mask (centre + kept only); avoid → mask (all); recover → mask (step only / hover only); stand → mask (none); mask → anyQ; anyQ → standPlan (no); anyQ → arb (yes); arb → publish (kept or switched); standPlan → publish; publish → p5; roadRef → corridor (lane scene on wheels); corridor → fieldStale (shifted line); sceneBuild → gradeRepair (lane floor); gradeRepair → roadRef (scene.gradeFloor); cands → hoverTurn (hover family); hoverTurn → score (hover rollouts); score → gradeFit (each rollout: grade under the car); gradeFit → mStand (grade rejects or tier); score → wheelVote (first wheel command); wheelVote → mStand (suggested speed, reason, tier); publish → laneCentre (forward wheel Follow); laneCentre → p5 (result.laneCentre); sceneBuild → radarTruth (each shape)

## C6 Step 7 for a lane car: one authority  `#flow-c-s6`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 6 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions. Next: 7 EnergyStep: radar tick, SR-1, goal driving.

- **Result for this car, lane scene, <= 500 ms, ok?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=plan.authority`): Read every update, replan or not.
- **Stand: energy plan not ready (say why)** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=plan.authority`): no_result_yet / result_for_another_car / goal_scene_result / result_stale / no_finite_candidate.
- **Does the plan hover now (or still coming down)?** (decision, `planenergy.h:49`): firstMode == 1 or the next rollout state hovers.
- **Hover follower flies the rollout (or lands)** (action, `hoverfollow.h:87` · log `OBS system=plan.authority`): Mode Hover (Drive when landing), speed, brake, height, bearing or held nose for a strafe (split).
- **Wheels: note the path, ease the speed (FollowSpeed)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:438` · log `OBS system=plan.switch`): Target = plan speed 0.5 s ahead, raised at 6 m/s^2 and lowered at the brake limit; brake only 1.5 m/s over it or a stop (O3). In eligible forward, on-wheels Follow, Plan publishes a stable lane-centre steering reference for the wheel follower. The reference uses the same scene to check sampled static clearance before switching. Radar and other trajectory consumers continue to use the collision-scored rollout. The wheel follower uses the rollout when the lane reference is ineligible.
- **Can the car hold the plan's speed? Boost if thrust-limited** (decision, `boostneed.h:44` · log `OBS system=drive.boost`): BO-1 (wheels) and BO-2 (hover): Speed's possible speed, not a second decider. Start when the car is 2.5 m/s under the plan's speed with the pedal (or hover throttle) already full and gaining under 1.5 m/s^2 for 0.8 s and the native meter has at least 0.35 charge; stop when the shortfall is under 1 m/s or charge under 0.05 (no restart until 0.35); minimum 0.8 s on and 0.6 s off. Never boosts into a Traffic hold, a stop line within 60 m, a brake request, a plan speed under 3 m/s, reverse, a wheel-clearance zero, sliding over 12 degrees, hard steering or off the wheels; hover adds precise-flight reasons and a cap below the ask (speed_cap_below_ask). The actuator only executes drive::Output::boost (input +0x1C on wheels, thrust x 1.75 for hover); the native meter is read, never pinned.
- **The plan's mode, speed, reverse, brake are this car's command** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=plan.authority`): g.adOut written; every 2 s traced with candidate and age.
- **Rail aim along the lane (railturn::Pursue)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:462` · log `OBS system=traffic.rail`): Not for a flying car. Corner cap and free-boost flag stored for the actuator.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: RailAim bearing, corner cap and free-boost flag for the rail drive / CF-1 corner forces
- **Ask the car navmesh to bake cells under the lane** (action, `navmesh.cpp:648` · log `OBS system=navmesh`): Every 0.5 s: the car's cell and every 4th lane point. Bake only; nothing reads a navmesh path now.
- **Record who drives this car (census every 30 s)** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225` · log `OBS system=plan.ownermap`): plan_rail / plan_flies / energy_plan_goal / coordinator_hold.
- **Lane / hold / goal-neutral command set** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:5225`): Stage exit. 

Arrows: fresh → notReady (no); fresh → hoverQ (yes); hoverQ → hoverFollow (yes); hoverQ → pathNote (no); hoverFollow → authority; pathNote → authority; notReady → authority (speed 0, brake); authority → rail (not flying); authority → navWant (flying); rail → navWant; navWant → ownermap; ownermap → p6; pathNote → boost (eased speed (lane step 7)); boost → authority (drive::Output::boost to the actuator)

## C7 EnergyStep: radar tick, SR-1, goal driving  `#flow-c-s7`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 7 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions. Next: 8 Drive-side tracking feedback (TK-1).

- **EnergyStep: radar tick, then a goal car's plan** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5142`): Runs for every car on autopilot, after the lane branch.
