- **RadarStep: rotating + forward-fan rays, budgeted** (action, `radar.h:99` · log `OBS system=world.radar`): 2 rotating rays (14 m) + 2 fan rays at 0.6 m and 1.3 m over the base, reach speed x 2.5 s in 8..45 m; 32 rays per frame for all cars; free stretch is air evidence.
- **RadarSweep: own hull swept along the plan path + side shift** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4629` · log `OBS system=world.radar`): Along the published path (< 600 ms) else the nose (reverse when backing), up to 4 pieces following the map floor; one side offset per pass cycling to 2 m; player every frame, others every 2nd; 48 pieces per frame. World records recent successful, unsaturated central hull-sweep spans that contain no retained blocking contact. The sweep can follow the published reverse rollout as well as the forward rollout. Remembered sweep evidence helps select a checkpoint and carries no clearance authority over the current scene.
- **SR-1: more than climb (0.35 m) over the floor?** (decision, `surface.h:120` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): Floor is the reachable map floor or the supplied swept floor. Plan then judges what reaches it against the road at the hit's own position (RP-1/RF-1). For map verdict 3 without a fallback, SurfaceOf can recover an upper reachable floor climb..4 m above the hit if no reachable floor is in the lower vehicle-height band. Climb is carcap's 0.35 m (D2: replaced surface's own 0.25 m step).
- **SB-1: More than climb (0.35 m) below the known floor?** (decision, `surface.h:120` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): SB-1 runs before the normal check, so the underside of the support deck can be excluded.
- **SB-1: Discard Below without learning floor** (action, `surface.h:120` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): Hull sweeps skip the contact, radar rays skip obstacle insertion, and retained lane rays are removed. The surface counter reports below=N.
- **Trusted normal? (rays yes, hull contacts no)** (decision, `surface.h:120`): Hull contact normals are unreliable (ramp decks read -0.39).
- **Faces up within the climb limit?** (decision, `surface.h:43` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): carcap MaxGrade(grip) = min(1.15 x gear grip x 0.85, engine 0.7); facing down = solid; within the limit = drivable (below-the-floor hits never reach here since SB-1).
- **Solid: keep its real shape** (state, `surface.h:15` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): Wall, rock face, box side, deck underside.
- **Within climb (0.35 m) of a known floor?** (decision, `surface.h:120`): No trusted normal: only height can say drivable.
- **Drivable: dropped (hull floor noted as ground)** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:4562` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): Gives the plan nothing.
- **Unknown: kept as solid (doubt is never drivable)** (state, `surface.h:20` · log `OBS system=world.surface`): 
- **RadarHit: game collision test + body box, or hit points** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4479` · log `OBS system=world.radar`): Known static body refreshed; new body: calcAabb box turned by its rotation; mesh/compound/large or no box -> its hit points; collides unknown = assume yes.
- **Verify one box against the game; expire by range/age** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4697` · log `OBS system=world.radar`): Gone or moved bodies leave; static 4 s past listing, dynamic 1.5 s, beyond 70 m.
- **Goal car and the plan budget allows a submit?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5142`): Owner energy_plan_goal; budget 4 in flight, 0.2 s per car.
- **GoalWorld: floor levels, kept hits/boxes, users toward goal** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4864`): Up to the sub-goal reach (30 m + 4): floor grid every 2 m x 12 m wide, kept hits and small boxes every 4 m, vehicles and people, radar boxes. Travel: drive-only / hover-only / ground preferred priced as drive-only.
- **SubmitGoal: goal scene through a 30 m sub-goal** (action, `planenergy.h:294`): Field rebuilt in the job when the goal moved 3 m, car near edge, or 2 s old; then the same energyplan::Plan (mode Avoid: no lane).
- **Goal result for this car, <= 500 ms, ok?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:5142` · log `OBS system=plan.authority`): Else stand: energy plan not ready.
- **Goal step 7: hover follower, or path follower + eased speed** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:5142` · log `OBS system=plan.authority`): Wheels: pathfollow::Follow bearing + FollowSpeed; no SC-1 caps and no TK-1 for goal driving. Speed raises what the car can hold with the game's forward boost for goal cars too (BoostSpeed, same function as the lane step).
- **Trip status: reached, near, blocked (HUD and jobs)** (action, `triplogic.h:5`): Arrival judged from distance, stop radius, height error and speed.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: trip phase and distance for the taxi marker HUD
- **adOut: mode, bearing, speed, brake, reverse** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:2772` · log `OBS system=autopilot`): Stage exit. Handed to the actuators in Fly (B).
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: adOut (mode, bearing, speed, brake, reverse, height) to the drive pedals, rail velocity writes, tyre mode and the hover actuator
- **Air evidence turns a mostly-empty box into points** (action, `vehicleflight.cpp:4610` · log `OBS system=world.radar`): A line through a box core touching no body counts; enough passes make the body its hit points (the fallen tree's half-open road).

Arrows: energyStep → radar (Radar feature on, main thread); radar → srStep (each ray hit (normal trusted)); radar → sweep (after the rays); radar → airy (free stretch); sweep → srStep (each hull contact (no trusted normal)); sweep → airy (piece touched nothing); srStep → solid (yes); srNorm → srAngle (yes); srNorm → srFloor (no); srAngle → drivable (yes, at floor or no floor); srAngle → solid (facing down / too steep); srFloor → drivable (yes); srFloor → unknown (no); solid → lookup; unknown → lookup; drivable → verify (nothing to the plan); lookup → verify; verify → goalQ; goalQ → goalWorld (yes); goalQ → goalFresh (goal car, budget full); goalQ → trip (lane car: done); goalWorld → submitGoal (shapes + floor points); submitGoal → goalFresh (published later (async)); goalFresh → goalDrive (yes); goalFresh → trip (no: stand); goalDrive → trip; trip → p7; srStep → srBelow (no); srBelow → below (yes); srBelow → srNorm (no); below → verify (nothing to the plan)

## C8 Drive-side tracking feedback (TK-1)  `#flow-c-s8`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 8 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions. Next: Shared World stores (data, read by the stages).

- **Rail drive with a fresh plan path (< 400 ms, same direction)?** (decision, `vehicleflight.cpp:6385`): Only rail cars (in_railLane) get path following and tracking; slot <= kSlotPilotCount.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: reads the physics step's measured yaw rate (g_yawMeasured) and rear slip angle (g_rearSlipDeg); the rail actuator calls the path follower
- **Path follower: curvature feed-forward + heading/lateral feedback** (action, `pathfollow.h:21` · log `OBS system=plan.follow`): One law forward and reverse; the nose bearing and yaw rate the rail takes. In eligible forward, on-wheels Follow, Plan publishes a stable lane-centre steering reference for the wheel follower. The reference uses the same scene to check sampled static clearance before switching. Radar and other trajectory consumers continue to use the collision-scored rollout. The wheel follower uses the rollout when the lane reference is ineligible. For forward lane-centre Follow on native tyres, the wheel follower uses fresh yaw from the same vehicle. The correction projects heading during steering unwind, based on the previous command and existing slew limit. Measured yaw is compared with the lane turn rate so steady curve tracking keeps its feed-forward turn.
- **TK-1: weaving, back stepping out, off plan, too much turn?** (action, `tracking.h:7` · log `OBS system=drive.tracking`): Window 2 s: 3 yaw flips over 0.15 rad/s -> 0.7 x onset speed; rear slip over 15 deg (caller) scales down; off plan 0.4 m / 0.1 rad for 0.5 s; lateral accel over carcap PlanLat(grip) (3.0 x the gear's grip, Sample::grip). Min cap 3 m/s, held 1 s, rises 1 m/s per s.
- **Tracking cap per car (read by Speed next tick)** (state, `vehicleflight.cpp:1649`): Data store read by other steps. +inf when tracking is fine.
- **Lane yaw damping: project heading over the steering unwind** (action, `pathfollow.h:78` · log `OBS system=plan.follow yaw_damped= measured_right_yaw= applied_yaw_target=`): Forward lane-centre Follow on native tyres: the heading error is advanced by the time to unwind the last wheel command (at most 0.45 s, from the sent steer and the slew limit), using measured yaw relative to the lane's turn so steady cornering keeps its feed-forward.

Arrows: trackQ → pathFollow (yes); pathFollow → tracking (lateral, heading error + yaw rate, rear slip); tracking → trackCap (cap, why); pathFollow → yawDamp (lane-centre reference on native tyres)

## C9 Shared World stores (data, read by the stages)  `#flow-c-s9`  (Detailed flow · C Autopilot decisions)

Stage 9 of 9 in C · Autopilot decisions.

- **Collision-box seed: loaded references' boxes into the nav map** (state, `navseed.cpp:187`): Data store read by other steps. Full pass about every 2 s within kRadius; boxes over kMaxHalf left to the probes.
- **Shared nav map: 4 m cells, kept hits, boxes, ground levels** (state, `navmap.cpp:124`): Data store read by other steps. Fed by every vehicle's probes and rays (NoteRay, NoteGround) and the collision-box seed.
- **Route survey: measured floor levels + swept solids (WM-1 map)** (state, `routesurvey.cpp:207` · log `OBS system=world.survey`): Data store read by other steps. Down rays every metre along the world's routes in loaded cells; plane per level; reachability flood per slot.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: route survey casts down rays along the world's authored routes
- **Per-car radar list: boxes and hit points** (state, `radar.h:90`): Data store read by other steps. g_radar per slot; kMaxBoxes; read by RadarAppend and RadarSweep. The diagnostic build retains sensor labels through World-to-Plan conversion, including radar rotating rays, forward rays and hull sweeps. world.sensors counts inputs by source and measured floor samples for the exact player lane snapshot used by Plan. World retains excluded points in radar; each lane snapshot can reach its own conclusion.
- **Cell bake: 8 m terrain grid per loaded cell** (state, `cellbake.cpp:185`): Data store read by other steps. Used for pilots' ground (BakedFor) and compared against live terrain probes.
- **Car navmesh tiles (Recast/Detour), cached per world** (state, `navmesh.cpp:491` · log `OBS system=navmesh`): Data store read by other steps. Baked from the game's own navmesh input (navprobe) with car settings (navbake.h); no driving reader today.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: frame task ticks cellbake, route survey, navprobe, navmesh, navseed, navfield (main_vehicle.cpp)
- **Engine rays and hull sweeps (CastNow / SweepPathNow)** (state, `vehicle_probe.cpp:1011` · log `OBS system=sensing.sweep`): Data store read by other steps. Main thread only; grown Havok hit arrays read and freed (PF-1, hitarray.h); body owner via loadedarea::HitBodyOwner.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: engine ray / hull sweep / body lookup interface (decomp addresses), PF-1 hit arrays, loaded-area wall bodies
- **Contact bus: one event per contact** (state, `contactevent.h:17`): Data store read by other steps. World shapes and stuck evidence read it.
- **Goal door: one goal per pilot (source, ending, facing)** (state, `goaldoor.h:47` · log `OBS system=plan.door`): Data store read by other steps. Jobs (PilotFly), commands (PilotCommand), lanes and Cruise all admit here.
- **One car record: climb, grade, turn and tyre limits (D2)** (state, `carcap.h:22`): Data store read by other steps. climb 0.35 m (half a tyre); MaxGrade(grip) = min(1.15 x grip x 0.85, 0.7); PlanLat(grip) = 3.0 x grip (clamped 0.5-3); TyreLat(grip) = 6 x grip (at least 0.25). Read by surface.h, bicycle.h, planrules.h (climbHeight, BendCap), tracking.h, cornerforce.h, energyplan.h, wheelgrip.h (ST-1) and worldclass.h (tireHeight = 2 x climb). Replaces the per-sensor 0.25 / 0.35 / 0.4 / 0.5 steps, 0.7 / 1.0 grades and 3.0 / 3.5 / 6 lateral limits. FC-1: the same file holds the per-gear flight row (carcap::Flight, kFlightTop, TurnAt) read by the hover planner via FlightSpec.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: data: carcap TyreLat(grip) read by the steering cap (wheelgrip ST-1) and corner forces (cornerforce CF-1)

Arrows: navseed → navmap (boxes written)

## D1 Frame entry, shots and vehicle health  `#flow-d-s1`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Area D decides which NPCs drive which vehicles and what they do aboard. Every full vehicle update, vehiclehits follows shots into vehicle hulls and keeps health and wrecks. npcvehicles then runs one Job per NPC pilot slot (256): boarding, following or a task (trip 9 for traffic), picking targets, choosing manoeuvres through the combat AI, firing, and getting out. Later in the same update the core traffic service (api_traffic.cpp, trafficpool::Tick) does the engine work for the FVO_Traffic addon: create, seat, persist, hide, delete, keep loaded. The addon itself (traffic/generator.cpp + traffic_main.cpp) owns every population decision: a pair is born only behind the loading screen or the X panel, Health judges, Handoff releases it to driving, and Retirement is the only remover, out of sight or under cover. Passengers (the player riding), player boarding/seat swaps and pull-outs are decided in the seat and force-out ticks of the same file. Next: Job table: who gets a vehicle job this frame. Oddities found when mapping: Oddities: (1) Two combat deciders chosen by an ini switch: CombatAITick (combat_ai/brain/bfm/tactics/steer, the full stack) vs ManoeuvreTick (manoeuvre.h staged set); plus the older AttackRun/AimPoint/OrbitGoal path still fires when neither is active, and coverfight.h is a third fighter for the player's empty vehicle running inside vehicleflight. manoeuvre.h says rams were removed 2026-10-25, but combat_tactics.h still scores rams and RamTick applies ram damage. (2) Seat repair is decided in several places: npcvehicles (boardSnaps x5 SeatNewborn, SeatBrake, PinToVehicle, HoldInSeat/HoldUnlinked), the addon's Created state (6 instant seats, driver moved every 2 s), and core WatchDrivers/RescueDriver; they share the trafficpair.h rules but each has its own counters. (3) Dead or vestigial paths: Job.scheduledTraffic/ClaimScheduled (the timetable owner was removed 2026-10-24/25 yet Update still returns early for it); everyday.cpp is a stub (stripped 2026-11-05) though core still calls hooks.everyday from context.cpp; ringroad.cpp only disables removed beacons; traffic.cpp Tick only logs a probe. (4) Files in this list that belong to other areas by use: jobline.h (used only by vehicleflight.cpp, area C), vehiclelinks.h (vehicleworld.h, C), lanevehicles.h (laneplan.h, E/C), farmover.h (shipmodel.cpp, ships, F), birthholds.h (vehiclediag.cpp, A/B); they are listed but not given their own nodes. (5) Stage ports: stage 1's entry fans out to several separately scheduled parts of the update (NPC jobs, traffic pool, registry, keep, scanner, coverfight), and stage 5/6 have several exits back to the job loop, so not every stage has a single way out. Two OBS systems (traffic.birth) are written both by core (api_traffic ListenBirth) and the addon (grant lines), so the same name means two speakers.

- **Vehicle update enters area D** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): Stage exit. Order in the full update: npcvehicles::BeginFrame, vehiclehits::Tick, (map/nav), npcvehicles::Tick + passenger::Tick, vehicleflight::Tick, then realvehicles, ringroad, trafficpool::Tick, scantarget, traffic::Tick, vehiclekeep::Tick. On loading-screen arrival frames only world::Publish, navseed and trafficpool::Tick run.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: main_vehicle Update / Frame call D's ticks; Install hooks (npcvehicles::Install, vehicleaim::Install, vehiclehits::InstallEnemyBar)
- **Follow shots and native impacts into vehicle hulls** (action, `vehiclehits.cpp:561` · log `health`): Vehicles take no game damage, so launched projectiles (NoteLaunch from the launch hook) and native impacts (ImpactDetour) are tested against each listed vehicle's oriented hull (interactionlogic::HullHit); the shooter's own vehicle never counts. Also drives the enemy health bar (EnemyBarTick).
- **Is the struck vehicle friendly to the shooter?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:8830`): Ally pairs (combatrelations::Classify: protected pair, faction reaction 2-3 both ways) take no damage; only attributed damage between allies is friendly fire (BlockFriendlyDamage).
- **Add vehicle damage and the driver's share** (action, `vehiclehits.cpp:460` · log `health`): Damage per hit = native weapon total x player difficulty x [hits] weaponScale (weapondamage.h: a native zero stays zero; [hits] damage=20 only when unreadable). Health 300 by default ([hits] health / limit). DriverShare passes part to the driver; healthlogic heals after a quiet interval that starts after combat.
- **Aggression and crime from a hit or ram** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:8853`): The occupants (and nearby owner / faction allies, combatrelations::AttackRole) turn on the shooter; a player shot on a non-hostile raises a crime once. Ram consequences come from combatrelations::RamConsequences.
- **Vehicle wrecked (health 0)** (state, `vehiclehits.cpp:786`): A wreck has a dead weapon and a banned autopilot; its job ejects the driver at rest (D.wrecked). HealthShare feeds the lifebar and enemy bar.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: health share for the lifebar and enemy bar
- **Hits done; NPC jobs next, traffic service later** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): Stage exit. The update continues to npcvehicles::Tick (stage 2) and, later in the same update, to the traffic service (stage 7).
- **Player's empty vehicle fights under Cover / Guard** (action, `coverfight.h:30`): Runs inside vehicleflight (not npcvehicles): never while the player drives it or a crew driver is aboard, only under Cover (U) or a guard point. Picks enemies within 120 m, holds a shield spot 12 m toward the threat, fires bursts when the nose is within 25-40 degrees and range < 100 m.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: runs inside vehicleflight for the player's own vehicle

Arrows: in → hits (full update); in → coverfight (vehicleflight::Tick, own empty vehicle under Cover/Guard); hits → friendly (shot inside another vehicle's hull); hits → s1out (no hit); friendly → s1out (friendly: no damage); friendly → damage (not friendly); damage → aggression (attacker known); damage → wreck (health reaches 0); aggression → s1out; wreck → s1out

## D2 Job table: who gets a vehicle job this frame  `#flow-d-s2`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Stage 2 of 8 in D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding. Next: Per job: validity and boarding (Update).

- **NPC vehicle tick** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:6891`): Main-thread tick for every NPC pilot slot, the player's seat rules and the follower HUD rows (hoverui::SetRows, SetLifebar, SetVehicleBar).
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: hoverui rows, lifebar and vehicle enemy bar; FollowerNotice notices
- **Is the player missing or dead?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:6891`): Dead player: every job stops firing and its pilot stops once; NPCs stay aboard. No player: the full-sim list is cleared. Either way the tick ends.
- **Escort context, pick-ups, followers ride** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:1625`): Escort mode (rescue / scout) chosen from the player's situation; PickUps and FollowersRide start boarding jobs for followers near the player's vehicle; ApplyStance for the own rover.
- **Walk, exit-all or profile key pressed?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:5017`): Walk key: the NPC faced within 60 m / 20 degrees gets a boarding job (Force). Exit key: ExitAllJobs. Aggro key: all following jobs flip escort <-> pursuit (ApplyProfile).
- **Keep traffic drivers fully simulated / far-cell pass** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:6847` · log `driver.pass`): Seated traffic drivers are kept on the full-simulation list; DriverPassTick requests or drops a driver model through the native wrapper (lifecycle 'driver-model').
- **Queued native exits and exit requests** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:3354`): DismountDetour refuses an AI dismount while our autopilot flies a cold-start driver and queues native exit intent (g_nativeExits) for StartExit with an 8 s timeout; ExitTick waits for a safe exit site.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: DismountDetour / SeatPlayerDetour / PlayerEntryDetour / SeatKeywordDetour engine hooks
- **Player seat rules: passenger request, stand-up, swap** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:6282` · log `seat.ladder`): boardinggate::PassengerAttempt steps a native passenger get-in (10 s limit); a pulled-out NPC still seated after 4 s gets the instant exit. SeatKeywordDetour gives the player the passenger seat of a vehicle someone else drives and NPCs their ladder marker.
- **Player forced out: owner or hijacker takes over?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:6520`): Never for a riding passenger. Forcer Owner: remounts its vehicle. Forcer Hijacker: gets a new job in Pursuit and comes after the player.
- **Start a boarding job in a free slot** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:4933` · log `npc.assignment`): StartJob / StartWalkBoard / StartTask fill an Idle slot and Set(Boarding); Mount uses the game's follower get-in FUN_141951FB0 (mount package type 0x29) or the quest package.
- **Squads and ram contacts** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:2122`): RefreshSquads partitions fighting jobs into ally groups (combatrelations::Partition), shares focus; RamTick claims impact pairs (1.5 s cooldown) and applies capped ram damage (tactics::RamDamage / CapRam).
- **Every non-idle job goes to Update** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:6891`): Stage exit. for each slot: Update(job) when not Idle; a following job not firing then PushDrive.
- **Player passenger ride and control** (action, `passenger.cpp:398`): The player rides a vehicle an NPC job flies; the NPC's autopilot drives (2026-10-28: no taxi steering from the seat). Exit and take-the-wheel stay on the passenger keys; Steer is the taxi goal when the controller is Taxi.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: passenger keys (exit, take the wheel)
- **Scanner marker and T-key gestures** (action, `scantarget.cpp:505` · log `input.key`): Scanner open: ray distance and T sets the map marker, chaining the own vehicle (summon / follow / Auto to marker). targetinput: tap selects, double tap clears, hold arms a ram; targetsession keeps the marker identity.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: scanner key, marker text, T gestures
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: own vehicle summon / follow / Auto to marker
- **Traffic pre-armed pilot job (task 9)** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:8092` · log `npc.assignment`): Called by the addon through ApiStartArmed: refuses dead/busy/autopilot-disabled pairs; sets task 9, coldStart, armed, the FvoTripClaim, then pilotquest::AssignTraffic (pending through lifecycle 'pilot-assign').
- **Send the NPC to the vehicle by quest package** (action, `pilotquest.cpp:143`): FVO_NPCPilots.esm quest aliases are filled with ForceRefTo so the NPC runs 'activate the vehicle alias' above its schedule. AssignTraffic is the traffic variant; Release frees the alias.
- **Seated traffic trip job (task 9)** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:7805` · log `npc.assignment`): Called through ApiStartTrip after ScriptedRefusal says nothing blocks it; the driver is already seated, so the job starts aboard with the addon's trip step.
- **Job table g_jobs (one per pilot slot)** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:131`): Data store read by other steps. kJobs = fvolimits::kVehicles (256), pilot = index + 1 = vehicleflight pilot and pilotquest alias slot. Phase Idle/Boarding/Following/Settling/Leaving, profile Escort/Pursuit, task 0-9, coldStart/armed flags for traffic.

Arrows: tick → playerDead; playerDead → context (no); context → startJob (follower near your vehicle); context → keys; keys → startJob (walk key on a faced NPC); keys → fullSim (no key / after keys); startJob → jobs (Idle slot -> Boarding); startJob → pilotQuest (walk boarding by quest package); armed → jobs (task 9 Boarding, armed); armed → pilotQuest (AssignTraffic); scripted → jobs (task 9 aboard); fullSim → exits; exits → seatTick; seatTick → forceOut; forceOut → startJob (hijacker: pursuit job); forceOut → squad (none / owner remounts); squad → s2out; s2out → passenger (after all jobs: passenger::Tick); s2out → scanTarget (later in the update)

## D3 Per job: validity and boarding (Update)  `#flow-d-s3`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Stage 3 of 8 in D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding. Next: Following: task, targets and orders.

- **Update one job** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560` · log `npc.job`): Returns at once for scheduledTraffic (timetable owner) or while an exit task owns the NPC.
- **Driver or vehicle gone, or driver dead?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Life is the driver's licence: firing stops, PilotStop ('driver died' / 'vehicle lost'), Set(Idle).
- **Traffic pair missing a model?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Cold-start only. Out of loaded cells: flies on as data, driver pinned to the vehicle (trafficpair::FlyWithoutDriverModel). In range with a model missing: brake hold, pin drift > 2 m, land when LandForMissingModel says; no airborne exit.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: brake hold commands and PilotStop landing while a model is missing
- **Both models here but driver not sitting?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560` · log `driver.seat`): trafficpair::SeatBrake: hold the vehicle still for kSeatBrakeMs so the seat repair can reach; bounded, then gives up.
- **Aboard and not moved 2 m for 10 s?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): IDLE ABOARD line every 10 s (paused time skipped, held-far pilots excluded). A task 9 trip stuck 90 s (kTripStuckMs) is ended; the driver stays aboard and the pool restarts it.
- **Which phase is the job in?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560` · log `npc.job`): Boarding, Following, Settling or Leaving (switch on j.phase).
- **Pilot package assignment still pending?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Retries pilotquest::AssignTraffic; still pending in lifecycle waits; failed ends the job (EndJobOf).
- **Boarding as a combat move?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:6205`): The combat AI's own activate request walks the fighter in; within 4 m it is calmed and instant-seated, its enemy restored once aboard. Failed, never started, too long or activated-but-not-aboard: falls back to the get-in package.
- **Is the vehicle at rest to board?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): environment::EntrySite verdict must be Safe for an unarmed job; otherwise wait, and after 45 s (kBoardTimeoutMs) refuse and go Idle.
- **Arrived at the vehicle: one plain get-in** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:458`): Within 3.5 m (or 8 m and still 1.2 s): EnsureSeat then the game's instant seat (kAnimatedEntry off) or the Activate package; unreachable after 15 s and 8 s still: notice and Idle.
- **Snap a newborn traffic driver into its seat** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:8222` · log `seat.ladder`): A creation-time seat link leaves sit state 0; once both models exist the snap sets sit 3 (only a sitting driver is carried). Once a second, 5 tries; learns the ladder marker per base.
- **Aboard: passenger, or pilot accepts the first goal?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Vehicle without 3D: wait 10 s then Leave. Passenger: moved to g_riders, slot freed. Driver: escort profile by default; first goal = trip step for task 9 else vcontract::GoalFor; PilotFly ok -> Following, refused -> Leave.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: vcontract::GoalFor first goal and PilotFly acceptance
- **Riders list (NPC passengers)** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:1174`): Data store read by other steps. NPC passengers need no pilot slot; RidersTick keeps them and they show as 'riding' rows.
- **Not walking: calm, re-ask, instant seat or time out?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:558`): After 1 s: package not 0x29 -> calm a fighter and Mount again once. Standing up: Mount after kStandMs. Not closer after kWalkMs: the game's direct instant seat. 45 s (120 s quest walk): Idle.
- **Following job continues** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Stage exit. case Phase::Following.

Arrows: update → lost (not timetable-owned, no exit task); lost → no3D (no); no3D → seatBrake (models present or flying on as data); seatBrake → watchdog; watchdog → phase (otherwise); phase → questPending (Boarding); phase → s3out (Following); questPending → combatBoard (assigned / none pending); combatBoard → aboard (combat move seated it); combatBoard → entrySafe (not a combat boarding); entrySafe → arrive (safe (or armed)); arrive → coldSnap (aboard cold-start driver not sitting); arrive → aboard (aboard); arrive → boardFallback (not aboard yet); coldSnap → aboard (sitting); aboard → riders (passenger: slot freed); aboard → s3out (PilotFly accepted -> Following)

## D4 Following: task, targets and orders  `#flow-d-s4`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Stage 4 of 8 in D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding. Next: Combat manoeuvre, flight hand-off and fire.

- **Aboard a wrecked vehicle?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Controller Wreck: fire off, PilotStop, eject request with waitLanded (12 s): ride the wreck down, out at rest, fight on foot.
- **Does the job have a task?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): An aggressor on this vehicle cancels the task back to escort. Tasks: 1 transit, 2 patrol, 3 home, 4 park, 5/7 supply, 6 wait for player, 8 convoy, 9 addon trip.
- **Run the task** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:2672` · log `npc.behaviour`): Traffic (coldStart task 9): HoldInSeat / HoldUnlinked keep the driver at its seat within kHoldReach; never repaired in a fight, wreck or pull-out. Any task over kTaskMaxMs (x3 for trips) parks (BeginPark).
- **Ask the addon's trip step for this frame's goal** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:2672`): j.trip.step(user,x,y,z,&FvoTripGoal): 0 = goal (stop, passThrough, speed, RouteNav lane frame); destination code parks there; other codes park at home; trafficcycle::Repeat may restart it.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: vehicleflight::RouteNav lane frame from FvoTripGoal
- **Player override rules (recall / cover / guard)** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:1470`): overrides::For(mode) limits pickFoe, chase and fire; firing stops at once when fire is not allowed.
- **Navigation / Combat / Weapons profiles** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:1458`): vcontract classes: Navigation (escort default) says where, Combat (attack/defend) how, Weapons when. Player-side vehicles fight by the player's stance.
- **Who is the target?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:1502`): Only aboard, outdoors, with a goal. Order: player combat focus, aggressor, boarding fight (60 s), squad focus, sticky current, native enemy, then a scan: vehicles (tier 0) before on-foot (tier 1), +25 m per ally already on it. Range 150 m defend (leash 250 m to the player) / 700 m chase; re-pick 500 ms. Native targeting is authoritative.
- **Fight starts or ends: set the combat profile** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): A foe and no combat profile: profileclass::DefaultCombat(navigation). No foe for 20 s (kFightOverMs): the auto profile is removed.
- **Attacking, retaliating or defending?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:1711`): Attack profile with no override -> PursueGoal; aggressor -> retaliate (OrbitGoal); otherwise DefendGoal around the player.
- **Which order owns the goal?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:2972`): Priority: rescue (hover to the player) > recall N > guard point > Hold > crew marker > scout (reports enemies at the marker); otherwise the combat/escort goal.
- **Goal set; manoeuvre and fire next** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Stage exit. pg (vcontract::Goal) is handed to the manoeuvre and flight choice.

Arrows: wrecked → taskDec (no); taskDec → taskTick (task set); taskDec → override (no task (follower / crew / combat)); taskTick → tripGoal (task 9); override → profiles (rules); profiles → pickFoe (aboard, outdoors, have goal, rules.pickFoe); pickFoe → combatProf (foe or none); combatProf → stance; stance → orders (goal from pursue / defend / orbit); orders → s4out

## D5 Combat manoeuvre, flight hand-off and fire  `#flow-d-s5`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Stage 5 of 8 in D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding. Next: Getting out and ending the job.

- **Full combat AI on ([combat] ai)?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:1800`): Only with a foe, aboard, chase allowed, no override, not rescuing/marker/scouting. ai=1 -> CombatAITick (also shields for a defensive escort); ai=0 -> ManoeuvreTick (a failed manoeuvre means the ram order flies it).
- **Combat AI tick: carrot, sticks, aim, fire** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:2333` · log `plan.combat`): Fills combatai::Situation, runs combatai::Tick, sets the goal carrot (hold or cruise speed). CB-2: when a combat line drives the plan only aim pitch goes as a stick (PilotCombatAim); stunned after a ram it drifts; otherwise steering commands.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: PilotCombatAim: combat line drives the plan (CB-2); carrot as the goal
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicleflight::PilotCommand stick commands (yaw, throttle, strafe, vertical, pitch, gear, brake)
- **Perception, behaviour tree, BFM, tactics, steering** (action, `combat_ai.h:116`): Memory/impulses/morale (combat_brain.h, Halo-style tree, personality from faction, difficulty), BFM Pick with hysteresis (combat_bfm.h), Mad Max tactics (combat_tactics.h), Reynolds steering combined by priority with SafeAhead 2.5 s terrain check (combat_steer.h).
- **Staged manoeuvre choice (fallback)** (action, `manoeuvre.h:154`): Close in, attack run, lag pursuit, circle, break, scissors, extend, overwatch, break off; [combat] stage limits them; rams and sideswipes removed 2026-10-25.
- **No foe: search the last known position?** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:2521`): A target seen or heard under 60 s ago: fly the brain's search pattern, weapon off.
- **Record which controller owns the vehicle** (action, `integrationlogic.h:10`): Order/rescue/fight/search/task decide j.controller (Taxi only if a passenger controls). Leaving AI or manoeuvre clears their pilot commands.
- **How does the vehicle move this frame?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Not aboard -> PilotStop + Idle. Searching / other nav profile / fight / follow -> PilotFly. Player indoors -> park at the door. Recall within reach or gave up beyond 600 m leash -> Settling. Retreated (pursuer near hit limit) -> autopilot off 60 s, reinforcements, Settling. Player still 5 s within 20 m and grounded -> park. Not flying -> relaunch after 4 s when far (25 m) or orbiting.
- **Fire this frame?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): canFire = in weapon range (BallisticsOf) and profile allows, environment::ClearShot from the muzzle, nose within FireCone (25-40 deg) and pitch, and the AI's own fire/ally check; weaponBurst paces bursts.
- **Submit the vehicle weapon fire flag** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:1239`): Sets or clears the fire input for the pilot slot; StoreAim keeps the led aim point for the launch hook.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicle weapon fire flag on the input submit path
- **Turn the projectile at launch to the led aim** (action, `vehicleaim.cpp:53`): Projectile::Launch hook (FUN_141B3BD50): for an NPC job's vehicle the launch angles are set from npcvehicles::AimFor; player rover shots are only logged. Reports the handle to vehiclehits.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: Projectile::Launch hook FUN_141B3BD50
- **Hand the goal to the pilot / planner** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:1281`): Stage exit. vehicleflight::PilotFly / PilotStop / PilotCommand with this job's pilot slot; the planner (area C) and flight (area B) move the vehicle. Overwatch holds the spot with yaw/pitch commands.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: vehicleflight::PilotFly / PilotStop with the job's vcontract::Goal; job goals become jobline lines
- **Vehicle Coordinator handoff: admit this pilot as wheel owner?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:8077`): Before planning, Fly asks for the pilot's admission: only a live cold-start Traffic job (task 9) on this vehicle is admitted, with the generation from trafficpool::CaptureWheelHandoff (0 = refused, the player took it). The admission travels with the command to PublishAdmitted.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: WheelAdmission {allowed, generation} for PublishAdmitted (B.publishDrive, B.mailboxRead)
- **Frame for this job done** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): Stage exit. Back to the next slot in npcvehicles::Tick.

Arrows: aiSwitch → combatAi (ai on and engage or shield); aiSwitch → manoeuvre (ai off); aiSwitch → search (no foe, ai on); aiSwitch → controller (no combat this frame); combatAi → brainParts (Situation); brainParts → controller (Output: carrot, commands, aim, fire); manoeuvre → controller (plan or ram fallback); search → controller; controller → flyDecide; flyDecide → pilotFly (fly / relaunch / hold); flyDecide → fireDec (flying with a foe); flyDecide → s5out (parked / waiting at the door); fireDec → submitFire (canFire (burst on) or stop firing); fireDec → pilotFly (blocked: keep flying); submitFire → aimLaunch (projectile launched: led aim point); submitFire → pilotFly; pilotFly → s5out; pilotFly → wheelAdmit (Traffic job drives on wheels)

## D6 Getting out and ending the job  `#flow-d-s6`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Stage 6 of 8 in D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding. Next: Core traffic service for the addon (api_traffic.cpp).

- **Settling 2.5 s then get out** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:3560`): case Phase::Settling: after kSettleMs Leave(j, 'parked').
- **Leave: is the exit site safe?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3110`): environment exit site not Safe: wait up to 8 s; then StartExit and Set(Leaving).
- **Exit request: animated, eject or instant** (action, `npcvehicles.cpp:3509`): vcontract::ExitRequest (mode, waitLanded, timeout); FinishExit / PlaceAfterExit put the NPC beside the vehicle; ClearOfVehicle afterwards.
- **Out of the vehicle before the watch ends?** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:3080`): Out -> ClearOfVehicle + Idle; still in after kExitWatchMs -> InstantExit then Idle.
- **Job ends (Idle)** (state, `npcvehicles.cpp:986` · log `npc.assignment`): Set logs every phase transition; the slot is free for a new job and the pilot quest alias is released.

Arrows: settling → leave (after 2.5 s); leave → exitReq (site safe or 8 s waited); exitReq → leaving (Set(Leaving)); leaving → idle (out, or instant exit after the watch)

## D7 Core traffic service for the addon (api_traffic.cpp)  `#flow-d-s7`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Stage 7 of 8 in D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding. Next: FVO_Traffic addon: population decisions (generator).

- **Traffic service tick** (action, `api_traffic.cpp:1612`): KO-1: the player in the driver's seat of a vehicle traffic made has taken it. If the addon is attached: vehiclekeep::UpdateTrafficLook, hooks.tick (the addon), WatchDrivers; always ServiceVisibility. RushTick runs housekeeping on paused frames.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: lifecycle service queue (Post/Poll) for every engine mutation; presentation / loading-screen state (trafficarrival)
- **Has the player taken this vehicle?** (decision, `api_traffic.cpp:1308` · log `traffic.taken`): Own rover or player in the driver seat (not a passenger ride): the identity is protected, never hidden, moved or deleted; the addon gives the pair to the world. 6E6B0E74: a new take increments handoff::wheelGeneration, revoking every earlier wheel grant (its mailbox command is then ignored).
- **FVO_Traffic.dll attached with API v5?** (decision, `api_traffic.cpp:1720`): Version must equal kFvoTrafficApiVersion (a v4 call with no hooks gets a read-only legacy table for ES 0.3). Without the addon nothing is born and Start/Swap only logs.
- **Core traffic API table and V1/V2 exports** (state, `traffic/traffic_api.h:59`): Data store read by other steps. Plain C table: create, seat, persist, move, show, delete, ground, trip start, mayUse, playerTook, presentation. Version 13 and the read-only version 4 stay for the published Traffic Addons 0.3 and 0.3.1.
- **Keep vehicles in sight loaded (Full LOD)** (action, `vehiclekeep.cpp:403` · log `keep.world`): A vehicle near or past the cell edge inside the camera cone gets the game's Full LOD + persistent setter; released after 10 s out of view or back inside. Also camera evidence / look forecast for births and retirement.
- **Addon tick hook called** (state, `api_traffic.cpp:1612`): Stage exit. g_hooks.tick = the addon's TrafficTick; the addon calls back into the API table above.
- **Watch linked drivers and publish evidence** (state, `api_traffic.cpp:1515` · log `traffic.move`): Data store read by other steps. Publishes pair health and reports orphans to the addon's retirement listener. Generator owns repair and retirement requests. QueueTrafficMove refuses a native one-ref actor move if the actor has linked to a vehicle since nomination.
- **Hide or show traffic refs, paced** (action, `api_traffic.cpp:956` · log `traffic.visibility`): Hide queue with a token pace; a retirement hide is held while the camera evidence or authority says no.
- **World reset revokes every wheel grant** (action, `api_traffic.cpp:1666`): The traffic service Reset clears the taken set, bumps the traffic epoch and handoff::wheelGeneration, so no old grant survives a load.
- **Vehicle registry and road inventory** (action, `realvehicles.cpp:688` · log `traffic.road_inventory`): Records every real vehicle in loaded cells with place, owner and user (vehicles.tsv), clears stacked duplicates; RefreshRoadInventory/AppendRoadVehicles give the addon the road's existing vehicles; placements and summon points.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: placements and summon points (RecordPlacement, SetSummonPoint)
- **Clean up removed ring beacons** (action, `ringroad.cpp:63`): After a load settles, beacons listed in ringroad.ini are disabled; the ring shape stays as route data (ringlogic.h).
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: ring shape data (ringlogic) kept as a route
- **Read NPC path destinations (read-only)** (action, `traffic.cpp:235`): Reads an actor's current path goal (trip if > 150 m, vehicleSuitable if exterior with no door). Tick only logs a probe every 5 s; context.cpp and StartTransit read it.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: path destinations read by context.cpp civil scan
- **Birth window open for this request?** (decision, `api_traffic.cpp:645` · log `traffic.birth`): Loading window only with the loading screen (presentation 3), Panel only under the X panel (2), Diagnostic only in non-release play; checked again on the service queue (lifecycle).
- **Is the birth pose proven (sited, road clear)?** (decision, `api_traffic.cpp:685` · log `traffic.site`): The addon's siting pose is the proof for the next create of that base; a road start also needs a fresh roadclearance proof (RoadRefusal, realvehicles::CheckRoadSite).
- **Create the vehicle, frozen at birth** (action, `api_traffic.cpp:714` · log `create.vehicle`): Engine create-reference through referencebirth on the frame thread; the vehicle is held (birthholds / holdBirth) until its driver sits; vehiclekeep::WantFromBirth.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: referencebirth create-reference listener; birthholds hold identity in vehiclediag
- **Create the driver and attempt seating in the creation callback** (action, `api_traffic.cpp:783` · log `create.driver`): Refused when traffic authority was revoked or the chassis left. The callback uses SeatNewborn to attempt the existing interaction and snap. Health confirms the seat from live linked sit state. The callback can defer when the actor has no AI process.
- **Make the pair traffic (persist, remember ids)** (action, `api_traffic.cpp:878` · log `traffic.persist`): QueuePersist on the service queue; WatchPersistence reports a lost persistent flag.
- **Period: create a test driver near the faced vehicle's seat** (action, `api_traffic.cpp:1626` · log `seat.fast`): Diagnostic input only. One exact ticket rechecks same cell, ownership and occupancy. The existing creation callback attempts the native seat interaction before the actor joins a cell. Start with a chassis-relative origin and use a measured seat offset on later model trials.
- **Measure linked seating and the factory spot check** (decision, `npcvehicles.cpp:5163` · log `seat.fast`): Poll creation, actor 3D, full process and exact seat each frame. Loaded snaps use the existing native service. Keep the same seat through the hold and the factory's concealed wheel-motion proof. Numpad 3 can then use the existing physics-step mover on this pair.
- **May this ref be deleted?** (decision, `api_traffic.cpp:1448` · log `traffic.delete`): DeleteRefusal (player took, not ours, wrong flags) or revoked authority refuses; else persist first, then NativeDelete.

Arrows: poolTick → taken (KO-1 check); taken → attached; attached → api (attached: kApi table handed to the addon); attached → keep (attached: UpdateTrafficLook); attached → visibility (not attached: visibility only); keep → s7out (hooks.tick); s7out → watchDrivers (after the addon returns); watchDrivers → visibility; poolTick → registry (same update: vehicle registry part); registry → ringroad; ringroad → destinations (traffic destinations part); birthGate → siteProof (open); siteProof → createVehicle (proof matches); createVehicle → createDriver (chassis exists); createDriver → persist (made); fastSeatBirth → fastSeatCheck (exact creation receipt); taken → wheelReset (world reset / load)

## D8 FVO_Traffic addon: population decisions (generator)  `#flow-d-s8`  (Detailed flow · D NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding)

Stage 8 of 8 in D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding.

- **Addon traffic tick** (action, `traffic/traffic_main.cpp:2628`): SyncProfile (the save's traffic profile), PreparePool, generator::Tick, CoordinateOutOfTraffic, WantSpotCells.
- **Traffic profile: save snapshot + working copy** (state, `trafficprofilestore.h:12`): Data store read by other steps. Each save holds an immutable 24-bit snapshot identity; edits go to a separate working copy (trafficpermanent overlays shared entries). SyncProfile blocks the addon until the profile is current.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: traffic profile snapshot / working copy the editor and Permanent Save use
- **Demand: route positions and models for this world** (action, `traffic/traffic_main.cpp:2398` · log `traffic.demand`): From the traffic files: each allowed route's schedule level gives a target per km and spacing; parking places add spots; generator::SetSpots receives the pool.
- **Traffic files: routes, exits, places, rules** (state, `trafficdata.h:28`): Data store read by other steps. Every Data\SFSE\Plugins\FVO_Traffic\*.ini plus ringroad.ini; trafficinitial gives initial stations, trafficconstraints restrictions, trafficlinks junctions. Read by Load in traffic_main.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: FVO_Traffic\*.ini and ringroad.ini written by the route editor
- **Traffic on, presentation known, wanted world?** (decision, `traffic/generator.cpp:3448`): Any no: the generator does nothing this frame.
- **Has the player's world changed?** (decision, `traffic/generator.cpp:2512` · log `traffic.census`): Saves this world's pool, loads the next; Reconcile edits without moving healthy pairs; RecoverRegistry restores remembered pairs (base re-checked: ids are reused across saves).
- **Cover pass under the loading screen** (action, `traffic/generator.cpp:2930` · log `traffic.retirement`): Deletes old spares and last session's recorded retirements (only ids still of the stored base), judges every pair with cover=true and replaces bad ones unseen.
- **Spawn window open?** (decision, `traffic/spawnflow.h:66` · log `traffic.window`): Open only behind the loading screen or a covering X panel (spawnrule::Open), not while the clock is pending; the test switch opens it in play. Arbiter: 1 create per tick, 12 waiting covered.
- **Step each pair (one candidate admitted at a time)** (action, `traffic/generator.cpp:2162`): The player took a staged vehicle: Life World, slot emptied. Otherwise every pair is judged before it acts.
- **Surplus, Health verdict or retirement due?** (decision, `traffic/generator.cpp:938` · log `traffic.health`): RetireSurplus || HealthJudge || RetireTick. Health (once a second, healthscore account + spawnhealth records) is the only judge and never removes; a fall through the world queues fall retirement.
- **May the pair be removed now?** (decision, `traffic/generator.cpp:876` · log `traffic.retirement`): spawnflow::MayRemove: world-owned (player took, Life World) never; under cover yes; otherwise only when both halves are unseen (no model, far and out of sight, or camera prediction). Waiting requests are queued and saved (retiringrecord).
- **Retirement: hide then delete** (action, `traffic/generator.cpp:696` · log `traffic.retirement`): ApplyRetirement marks ids retiring; RetirementShowV2 hides them; deleteRef is retried up to kDeleteGiveUp; a vehicle the player took is dropped from the queue.
- **Pair state?** (decision, `traffic/generator.cpp:2162`): Unknown (restored: reuse if still the same base, frozen if saved flying), Empty (filled only by InitialScene), Created, Ready, Started, Failed (retry only the missing driver on the same chassis).
- **Created: wait for 3D and body, seat the driver** (action, `traffic/generator.cpp:2162` · log `traffic.seat`): Far (> 350 m) waits; no 3D after 120 s fails; no physics body waits; instant seat once a second, 6 tries; a driver far from its vehicle is moved to it every 2 s. Far pairs fly on a pre-armed pilot (startArmed).
- **Ready: does the route side give a trip?** (decision, `traffic/traffic_main.cpp:1843` · log `traffic.trip`): Ask first (tripRefusal), claim after; Room per route (roomrule) and FairRoute decide; no trip -> retry in 10 s; claimed -> startTrip into core (npcvehicles task 9) and Life Driving.
- **Started: driving under its job** (state, `traffic/generator.cpp:2162`): Trip ended at its spot: after 10 s idle a new trip (Ready), 5 restarts, earned back by a minute of driving. A staged chassis with no body 30 s is a fault for Health.
- **Route side: next trip goal along the route** (action, `traffic/traffic_main.cpp:1513` · log `route.goal`): The FvoTripClaim step core calls each frame: legs Climb/Join/Follow/Leave/Done (trafficroute.h), junctions (trafficlinks.h), place paths and authored journeys; Cycle restarts a trip.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: route lines, exits, junctions, places, journeys from the authored route data
- **Lane guidance, permits and right of way** (action, `traffic/traffic_main.cpp:1122` · log `traffic.lane_permit`): coordinator::Holds is the one writer of held/stopDistance; lanecoord arbiter grants lane changes (LanePermit); junction right-of-way by approach order; spacing to the leader. Traffic supplies lane geometry, travel permissions, speed, hold reason and stop distance through Route guidance. World supplies obstacles and contacts. Speed and Plan process those inputs through the ordinary driving pipeline.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: lane provider, lane permits, travel rules, field gate, road lanes registered with the core planner
- **Right-of-way ladder** (state, `trafficrank.h:6`): Data store read by other steps. Player > combat/pursuit > escorts > scripted > traffic > civil > parked; collision safety ignores rank.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: right-of-way ladder read by vehicleflight and planowner
- **Handoff: release the frozen pair to driving** (action, `traffic/generator.cpp:2964` · log `traffic.handoff`): Released only when seated (sit 3), aboard, with a body and the core's handoff facts (package ready, physics bound and stepping); deferred 10 s then a Health fact. A queued build-drop correction checks the original hold generation at native commit. During the diagnostic move, LoadedArea sync yields and older refiles fail the pose-generation check.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: handoff facts from vehicleflight::HandoffPhysics; release to the driving pipeline
- **Fill empty positions: Arbiter, Siting, Birth** (action, `traffic/generator.cpp:3132` · log `traffic.fill`): Under the window only: fleet room (every slot), a complete world list or the core's road proof, model fits the lane envelope (InitialModelFits), field box and spacing siting, the core's road clearance, then a birth request (vehicle, then driver). Free slots are ordered by visibility ring, then distance. The factory and stock paths were removed on 2026-11-21.
- **Publish population inspection and health** (state, `traffic/generator.cpp:3448`): Stage exit. populationinspect::Publish rows (state + waiting reason) for the Traffic Manager; HealthPublish / HealthSave.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: population inspect and health records for the Traffic Manager views
- **Census: one Life per pair + Health records** (state, `traffic/spawnflow.h:83` · log `traffic.census`): Data store read by other steps. Life None/Born/Proving/Driving/Recovering/Broken/World/Retiring/Gone with allowed transitions; published read-only to core (FVO_TrafficCensusPublishV1) and to the inspector (populationinspect).
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: Census copy into the core's owner record (FVO_TrafficCensusPublishV1); out-of-traffic list

Arrows: addonTick → profile (SyncProfile); addonTick → sendPool (PreparePool); sendPool → trafficFiles (reads routes / places); sendPool → genGate (generator::Tick); genGate → genOut (off / no world); genGate → worldSwitch (on); worldSwitch → coverPass (under cover after a load); worldSwitch → window; coverPass → retire (bad pairs, old spares, recorded retirements); window → step (window state passed to every step); step → judge (not taken by the player); step → census (taken: Life World, slot emptied); judge → retire (surplus / broken / fall); judge → pairState (healthy or proving); judge → census (verdict -> Life); pairState → seatPair (Created / Failed retry); pairState → tripClaim (Ready); pairState → started (Started); pairState → handover (Empty / Unknown resolved); seatPair → tripClaim (seated -> Ready); tripClaim → started (claimed); tripClaim → handover (no room: retry in 10 s); started → handover (idle 10 s -> Ready (restarts)); retire → deleteTick (removal granted); retire → handover (waiting unseen / world owns it); deleteTick → handover; handover → initialScene (ServiceHandovers done); initialScene → genOut (window closed / budget spent); started → routeStep (core job calls the trip step each frame); routeStep → lanes (lane frame for the planner); lanes → rank (reads ranks); lanes → genOut

## E1 Host frame: hooks, movie advance, safety gates  `#flow-e-s1`  (Detailed flow · E Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements)

The Traffic Manager (called F7 in code and notes, opened with F8 in photo mode, or the right stick) edits the traffic route files of the current game save's working traffic profile. It runs inside the game's Scaleform movie Advance (photo-mode movie for editing; the local-map movie draws a read-only overview), guarded by SEH so that a fault turns the overlay off and never leaves the player stuck in photo mode. Opening it loads FVO_Traffic\Editor_<world>.ini (only if the editor wrote it), every other *.ini as grey originals, ringroad.ini, VehicleGroups.ini and the shared Permanent routes, then places anchored-city routes by the addon's world shifts. Every change goes to one Draft (64-step undo) of the selected route. Save checks it, moves anchored routes back into city coordinates and writes the whole editor file atomically (tmp, .bak, replace), then tells FVO Traffic to re-read. Cancel restores the saved copy. The pure route-data headers here (route policy, schedule, line end, road tiles and field boxes, curves, splines, lanes, junction network, fair share) are also what the traffic addon and the autopilot read. Placement tables and the summon-point helpers are data for realvehicles.cpp. Next: Open and load route files. Oddities found when mapping: 1) Key naming: the Traffic Manager key is F8 (PhotoKeyTick, VK_F8) but code, comments, OBS names (f7.*) and notes call it F7; one refusal trace even says f7_enter_failed. The routeedit.photo/fault/install/place traces are written 'OBS routeedit.x' without 'system=', unlike the f7.* and routeedit.fit traces, so a grep for 'OBS system=' misses them. 2) Several listed files are not editor code: context.cpp/.h is the NPC boarding context engine (area D; the editor only uses its FVO_WorldName export), anchor.* is surroundings/ship anchoring (A), placement_spots.h / own_spots.h / placementlogic.h / placementedit.h / parkedlayout.h / sitelogic.h are read by realvehicles.cpp and environment.cpp, pilotlayout.h by npcvehicles/pilotquest/trafficprofile, photoeditorinput.h by inputgate.cpp. placementedit's summon-point and aimed-spot helpers are still read although placement mode was removed 2026-10-28. 3) Commits that bypass Cancel: Delete route (every branch), Test, Parking (SetParking) and Permanent Save all call Save immediately, so they also write any other pending edits in the file. 4) The editor and the addon each parse the whole traffic folder with the same trafficdata steps (Read, AddRingroad, PlaceWorld, PreferEditor, ApplyLaneDefaults): two readers of the same files that must stay in step; the editor re-reads every 3 s while open and the map overview re-reads too. 5) A playthrough (profile dir) change resets g_ed to Editor{} in WorkBody, silently dropping an unsaved draft; this matches trafficprofile::BeginLoad ('reloading the same save discards unsaved route changes') but is not reported to the user. 6) Stages are not single-exit everywhere: stage 1 has two outs (photo editor / map overview) plus the separate main-thread tick path (E.maintick is a second entry, not reached from Advance in code; the edge marks the same-frame relationship). The s7 data stage has no port. 7) MapOverview calls Open(), so merely viewing the local map loads the editor state. 8) docklayout.inl 'D11 is NOT approved': rounded / tube / trapezoid tiles are kept but not materialised by Update Route. 9) routeedit.cpp is 7083 lines with most logic in very long single lines; mapped at decision level only (WorkBody widget code not enumerated). 10) entry is E.install (the boot event that patches the hooks); the per-frame entry is E.advance, with E.maintick as the parallel main-thread entry.

- **Patch photo-mode, star-map message and map-update vtable slots** (action, `routeedit.cpp:7251` · log `OBS routeedit.install`): Each slot is checked against the expected 1.16.244 function first. Photo hook refused: F8 editor off, map overview kept; star-map or SurfaceMapState slot differs: whole editor off.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: Install called from main_vehicle.cpp boot with the other UI hooks; vtable patches checked against exe bytes
- **Movie Advance from hoverui's MovieImpl detour** (state, `routeedit.cpp:7214`): Stage exit. Per-frame entry after Install. Entry each frame. Only the photo movie or the star-map movie is served, under g_workLock; skipped when the overlay already failed on this movie. Crash breadcrumbs mark begin and end.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: hoverui.cpp MovieImpl detour calls Advance / MovieClosing / MovieGone (Scaleform host shared with the scanner HUD)
- **Is this the photo-mode movie (editor) or the local map (overview)?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:7214`): g_photoHost picks one of two Ui sets (g_hostUi[0/1]). Editing happens only in photo mode; the map only shows routes.
- **Run ApplyGround + WorkBody under SEH** (action, `routeedit.cpp:7201`): Any exception sets g_ui.failed (overlay off until the movie reloads); in photo mode it restores native input and asks photo mode to close.
- **Camera captured this frame (or last good within 300 ms)?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:262` · log `OBS routeedit.photo`): Photo camera misses are counted by reason; a gap up to 300 ms reuses the last good camera so the editor does not flicker. No camera: hide everything and release input.
- **Traffic addon attached and save's traffic profile available?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:5884`): Without FVO_Traffic.dll no F8, hint or first-time message. Empty trafficprofile::Routes() (no save identity yet) hides it. A changed profile dir resets the editor state (unsaved draft dropped).
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficpool::AddonAttached; trafficprofile::Routes (per-save snapshot + working copy, BeginLoad / BeforeSave)
- **Host ready: camera, profile, overlay built** (state, `routeedit.cpp:5884`): Stage exit. Overlay layer built once per movie (Build) and kept on top; values are forgotten only in MovieGone.
- **Draw read-only route overview on the local map** (action, `routeedit.cpp:5859`): Calls Open() if the world or profile changed (keeps g_ed.on as it was), reloads other files every 3 s and draws lines; no editing on the map since the photo-mode editor (2026-11-04).
- **Report the fault, clear gestures, keep the draft** (action, `routeedit.cpp:7185` · log `OBS routeedit.fault`): Logs module+offset stack; drops hub, drags, naming and context previews so the next F8 does not repeat the fault. The open draft survives.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: crash::Crumb breadcrumbs and module+offset fault report
- **Hide overlay, release photo editor input** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1997`): The common exit when nothing may be shown.
- **Main-thread editor ticks (from the vehicle frame task)** (state, `routeedit.cpp:7074`): Stage exit. Called each frame by main_vehicle.cpp, paused or not; they serve engine work the movie thread must not do.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: main_vehicle.cpp frame task calls PhotoKeyTick, GroundTick, ItemsMainTick
- **F8 pressed and allowed (no map, gameplay, addon, hook)?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:7074` · log `OBS routeedit.photo`): Asks the game to open photo mode and marks the visit as wanting the editor; also sends the game's own photo-mode close when the editor asks to leave. Refusals are logged by reason.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: inputgate::GameplayAllowed; game photo-mode open / close calls
- **Answer queued ground rays (ray, direct ray, nav map, baked cell)** (action, `routeedit.cpp:7112`): New points sit kGroundLift 2 m over what is under the cursor. The paused map never services queued rays, so after 300 ms a direct cast is used; after kGroundWaitMs navmap ground, then cellbake terrain, then the game's terrain.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: vehicle_probe::CastNow, environment::RayHit, navmap::At, cellbake::Ground for placed point heights
- **Scan references in route fields (2 cells a frame)** (action, `routeitems.inl:102` · log `OBS system=f7.items`): THREAD-1: read-only modscene reference scan on the main frame task, feeding the route item warnings.

Arrows: install → advance (hooks installed; hoverui calls Advance each movie frame); advance → host (photo or star-map movie, overlay not failed); host → worksafe (either host); worksafe → camera; worksafe → fault (exception); fault → hide (overlay off until movie reloads; photo mode asked to close); camera → hide (no camera); camera → gates (camera ok); gates → hide (no addon or no profile); gates → s1out (ok (profile change resets editor)); s1out → overview (map host); advance → maintick (same frame, main thread (separate call path)); maintick → photokey; maintick → groundtick; maintick → itemstick

## E2 Open and load route files  `#flow-e-s2`  (Detailed flow · E Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements)

Stage 2 of 7 in E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements. Next: Dock, selection and tool dispatch.

- **New photo visit, F8 or right stick: open or close the editor?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:5884` · log `OBS routeedit.photo`): Each new photo visit starts closed unless F8 asked for it; F8 while open closes and also leaves photo mode. Ignored while the traffic restart panel is up.
- **Open: world name, profile dir, file name** (action, `routeedit.cpp:828`): Needs FVO_WorldName (context.cpp export) and trafficprofile::Routes(). Status line counts editor and grey routes; logs planet local time.
- **Not loaded, or world / profile changed?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:828`): Yes: g_ed = Editor{} and the editor file is read again; no: keep the loaded state and only reload other files.
- **Editor_<world>.ini exists and carries the editor signature?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:828`): Signature 'written by the map route editor'. A file without it is never parsed or overwritten (saving refused) so hand-written files are safe.
- **Parse editor file into routes, spots, zones, links** (action, `routefiles.h:245`): Reads what Format wrote or an older save; anything not the editor's own shape goes to problems (logged). g_ed.saved = the parsed copy, which Cancel restores.
- **Foreign or unreadable file: start empty, saving off** (action, `routeedit.cpp:619`): Status warns; Save later refuses to overwrite.
- **Load vehicle groups (VehicleGroups.ini + owned permanent files)** (action, `routeedit.cpp:933`): routeGroups and routeGroupsSaved; groups are written only when they changed.
- **Load every other *.ini + ringroad.ini as grey originals** (action, `routeedit.cpp:754`): trafficdata::Read on each file in name order, AddRingroad, PlaceWorld for anchored cities, PreferEditor and ApplyLaneDefaults (same steps the addon uses). Routes the editor file holds are left out; permanent_* files are remembered for Permanent Save. Repeated every 3 s.
- **Move anchored-city routes and spots into game coordinates** (action, `routeedit.cpp:690`): Shift from FVO_SetWorldShift; a city not laid down here leaves its routes unplaced (not drawn or editable). The saved copy is shifted too so Cancel matches.
- **Editor open with a selected route** (state, `routeedit.cpp:828`): Stage exit. First non-removed editor route selected on a fresh load.
- **Close editor (draft kept, context previews dropped)** (action, `routeedit.cpp:5884`): g_ed.on=false; a half-placed context item is reverted to its base; the draft stays for the next visit.

Arrows: toggle → open (F8 wanted / F8 or RS while closed); toggle → close (new plain visit or F8 / RS while open); open → reset (world and profile known); reset → readfile (yes: fresh Editor{}); reset → loadothers (no: keep loaded state); readfile → parse (signed); readfile → readonly (unsigned or missing); parse → loadgroups (saved copy = parsed file); readonly → loadgroups; loadgroups → loadothers; loadothers → placeshift (others + anchored shifts); placeshift → s2out

## E3 Dock, selection and tool dispatch  `#flow-e-s3`  (Detailed flow · E Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements)

Stage 3 of 7 in E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements. Next: Edit the draft.

- **Read focus, mouse deltas, pad cursor, camera-navigation hold** (action, `routeedit.cpp:5884`): Right mouse or left stick = camera navigation, never a click; a button held across the gesture is swallowed. Shift / LB = 5x camera speed. The editor draws its own pointer from raw photo-mode mouse counts.
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: inputgate::PhotoEditor / TakePhotoMouse, bindings::PadState, bindlogic pad bits; photoeditorinput.h block policy lives in inputgate.cpp
- **Dock level (Routes > Route > Tile / Point, Types, Arrive, City, Places, Problems)** (state, `f7dock.h:16` · log `OBS system=f7.dock`): The approved HTML dock (2026-11-06) replaces the side panel; breadcrumbs go up, cards and pickers go down. Layout in docklayout.inl, shared pure parts in f7dock.h, f7layers.h, panelpaging.h, strokefont.h, editorcontext.h.
- **Click point, line, double-click or Ctrl+click: what is selected?** (decision, `f7dock.h:59` · log `OBS system=f7.select`): Point = that point; line = tile under the click (within reach px); double-click = every point or tile; Ctrl+click toggles one. Points and tiles never mix. Selected points also fill g_ed.picks for group moves.
- **Node view: pick nodes, Enter makes a route** (action, `nodeview.inl:4` · log `OBS system=f7.node`): The network drawn over the dimmed map; wires of landings edited here too, each change undoable.
- **Arrange mode: drag dock areas, saved layout** (action, `routeedit.cpp:576` · log `OBS system=f7.arrange`): UI layout only; no route data.
- **Which tool or button (Press id / shortcut letter)?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:4297` · log `OBS system=f7.key`): Letters the map does not use (never number keys or , . / '); Ctrl+S save, Ctrl+Z/Y undo, Insert drop, Del delete, PgUp/PgDn height. Basic/primitive/restored tool filters can refuse an id.
- **An edit or a commit was asked for** (state, `routeedit.cpp:4297`): Stage exit. Edits go to stage 4, Save/Cancel/Test/Delete/X to stage 6.

Arrows: input → dock (focused, not navigating); dock → select (map click); dock → nodeview (Nodes view on); dock → arrange (Arrange on); dock → press (button or shortcut); select → press (selection set; tool acts on all selected); press → s3out

## E4 Edit the draft  `#flow-e-s4`  (Detailed flow · E Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements)

Stage 4 of 7 in E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements. Next: Check and preview (reader only).

- **A draft open? Else start one (saved route or original)** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:666`): Every change starts a draft of the selected route; a grey original becomes an editable copy; nothing selected: status 'Pick a route first'.
- **Copy an original into the editor file under the same name** (action, `routeedit.cpp:802`): Saved copy replaces the original (addon PreferEditor). Anchored cities keep their key and are saved in city coordinates; other worlds become the editor key. Del route on it later brings the original back.
- **New route: ed_<slug> id, Both travel, Quiet level, Drop mode** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1017`): Refused while a draft is open. Id unique across every file's routes. Draft shift is zero (player's world).
- **Draft of one route with 64-step undo / redo** (state, `routeeditor.h:441`): Add/Move/Remove/Reverse/Round keep sections, rounding, spline handles, lane plan and road tiles in step with the points. Edit = one undo step; Amend = same step (held key). Nothing touches disk.
- **Drop a point (after selected / into a stretch / at end)** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1178` · log `OBS routeedit.place`): Placed at once at the baseline height, then moved to 2 m above the ray answer when GroundTick returns. PlacedHeight never lowers a point. The tile's primitive is carried to the new point.
- **Move / raise / delete many picked items as one step** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1541`): Mass edits across routes and spots snapshot the whole file (MassSnap) for undo; GroupFits refuses moves that break a route.
- **Whole-route edits: raise, turn, scale, mirror, copy, merge, shape** (action, `routeedit.cpp:3625`): Each goes through RouteFits / ApplySize before it lands in the draft.
- **Junctions and lane changes between routes** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1835` · log `OBS system=f7.link`): A link joins any route (ring by degrees, path by point) to an editor route's start or end with a share of laps (default 0.2). Lane change: two same-direction lanes of one path, 12-500 m apart. Links follow inserted/removed points. The f7.link trace is written by the context (dock) path in contextedit.inl.
- **Field, flow, position and rules edits on selected tiles / points** (action, `contextedit.inl:16` · log `OBS system=f7.commit`): Three-layer F7 editing (Field, Flow, Position, Rules). Typed numbers via - / + or entry. Commit checks only moved or added points against terrain.
- **Point under known terrain or invalid?** (decision, `routeclearance.h:9`): Only a proven terrain violation or invalid coordinate blocks; mapped structures (hangar, tunnel) only warn. Context edits check only points they add or move (2026-11-11).
- **Lane volume picks and lane edits** (action, `laneedit.inl:2`): lanevisual.inl picks lanes on screen with the existing Pen projection; laneedit.inl applies lane-plan changes to the draft.
- **Spline handles and profile keys** (action, `splineedit.inl:2`): Authoring only: handles and profiles in splinekit::Plan; a geometry error later blocks Save.
- **Survey road, snap to deck, fit lane width** (action, `routeedit.cpp:2430` · log `OBS system=routeedit.fit`): roadfit::Survey over every station using the nav map's answers (no new engine calls); SnapPoints = median deck height per point; FitWidth = 20th percentile free span, central railing at 40% sets the divider.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: nav map ground and solid answers feed roadfit::Survey
- **Convert old lanes to one road with red dividers** (action, `roadmigrate.h:15` · log `OBS system=route.migrate`): Measures current lanes per tile and makes dividers midway; old lane records stay in the route, unused while road tiles are on (non-destructive). Crossing or stacked lanes are not guessed. Called and traced from contextedit.inl.
- **Update Route: fill missing tile field boxes with defaults** (action, `docklayout.inl:505` · log `OBS system=f7.dock`): Authored boxes kept; rounded / tube / trapezoid tiles wait for the user's D11 answer; unsupported stored records left unchanged. Result is a draft until Save.
- **Landings, spawns, zones, access and auto wires** (action, `placewires.inl:18` · log `OBS system=f7.autoroute`): A new landing gets one In wire from the nearest upstream lane and one Out wire downstream, routed round known obstacles from nav memory; unknown stretches marked red. Spots in an anchored city are stored in city coordinates (CityAt within 800 m).
- **Time rules: busy words, windows, traffic level bands** (action, `routeedit.cpp:884` · log `OBS system=routeedit.traffic_level`): Planet local time from planet+0xEF8 (preview hour does not change the clock). Level bands are vehicles per km, clamped to fvolimits::kVehicles.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: FVO_LocalTime export: planet local hours for the addon's schedules
- **Undo / redo (draft first, then mass snapshots)** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1547`): Mass undo is cleared on Save and Cancel.
- **Draft or file changed in memory (not on disk)** (state, `routeedit.cpp:5884`): Stage exit. Status says 'Save keeps it'.

Arrows: ensuredraft → editoriginal (grey original selected); ensuredraft → draft (saved route selected or draft open); ensuredraft → s4out (nothing selected: refused); editoriginal → draft (copy under same name); newroute → draft (empty ed_ route, Drop mode); draft → droppoint (Drop / Insert); draft → groupmove (picks, height keys, hub, drag); draft → shape (Route / Shape tools); draft → connect (Connect); draft → contextedit (dock field / flow / rules values); draft → lanes (lane tools); draft → spline (spline kit tools); draft → roadfit (fit tools); draft → migrate (convert lanes); draft → updateroute (Update Route); draft → places (Landing / Spawn / Zone / wires); draft → schedule (time rules); draft → undo (Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y); droppoint → clearance (new position); groupmove → clearance (moved positions); contextedit → clearance (only added or moved points); clearance → s4out (ok or structure warning: applied / blocked: refused); shape → s4out (RouteFits ok); connect → s4out (link added to file); lanes → s4out; spline → s4out; roadfit → s4out (survey applied); migrate → s4out (dividers made, old records kept); updateroute → s4out (defaults added / route unchanged); places → s4out (spot / wires drafted); schedule → s4out; undo → s4out

## E5 Check and preview (reader only)  `#flow-e-s5`  (Detailed flow · E Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements)

Stage 5 of 7 in E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements. Next: Commit: save, permanent save, cancel, test, delete, restart traffic.

- **Draw routes, flow, balls, marker solids, dock and text** (action, `routeedit.cpp:2133` · log `OBS system=f7.draw`): Uses routemap projection and clipping, routecache line cache, strokefont text; layers are recorded and only re-sent when changed (DRAW-1).
- **Route item warnings: what each thing in the field does to a car** (action, `routeitems.h:51` · log `OBS system=f7.items`): F7 reader only: words come from the driving rules (worldclass, planbranch, planworld) so no number is a second copy. Tile checks and problem groups shown in the Problems level.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: warning words from worldclass / planbranch / planworld rules; modscene reference scan
  - hands off to F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics: Health records (traffichealthapi) for model verdicts on Types cards
- **Drive preview: where a car will drive on this route** (action, `routeitems.inl:259` · log `OBS system=f7.preview`): Started by Test (HV-4) on the route as shown before Save; draws only.
- **Frame drawn** (state, `routeedit.cpp:5884`): Stage exit. Back to the next Advance.

Arrows: draw → items (Problems / items view); draw → s5out; items → s5out; preview → s5out

## E6 Commit: save, permanent save, cancel, test, delete, restart traffic  `#flow-e-s6`  (Detailed flow · E Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements)
