Stage 6 of 7 in E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements. Next: Route data model and placement data (read by C, D, F).

- **Commit kind: Save, Permanent Save, Cancel, Test, Delete, X?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:4297`): Also Ctrl+S and pad Start save; dock save column has Test, Update Route, Cancel, Save, Permanent Save, Permanent Delete.
- **Permanent Save of the selected route?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:941`): Needs a draft of a route at a valid index and shared storage (trafficprofile::PermanentDir).
- **Test: mark route test=1 and save** (action, `routeedit.cpp:4297`): The next free vehicle with a driver near the route flies it once (addon). Dock Test also opens the drive preview.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: test=1 route: addon sends the next free driven vehicle round it once
- **Delete: own route, edited original, or another file's route?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:1031`): Second press within 3 s confirms. Another file's route: a removal marker (removed=true) is saved. Edited original: entry erased so the original returns. Own route: erased with its links. Every branch saves at once.
- **Draft valid (points, settings, schedule, spline geometry)?** (decision, `routeeditor.h:31`): Blocks: too few points (2, 3 for a loop), duplicate neighbours, invalid point, travel or corridor 1-200 out of range, bad sections, schedule overlap. Warnings (under 15 m apart, >45 degree climb, >120 degree turn) never block.
- **Playthrough changed, or target file not editor-written?** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:941`): Profile dir differs from the one opened: refuse ('reopen the Traffic Manager'). Existing file without the signature: refuse.
- **Convert to file coordinates (minus each city's shift)** (action, `routeedit.cpp:698`): Routes, spots, access paths and zone floors/tops go back into their anchored city's own coordinates.
- **Write route into shared permanent_<world> entry, refresh overlay** (action, `permanentroutes.h:10` · log `OBS system=f7.permanent`): Shared across playthroughs; a disabled old file gets a live sibling instead. The route is then removed from the save-local editor file, so later normal edits become an explicit local override.
- **Write Editor_<world>.ini: tmp, flush, .bak, replace** (action, `routeedit.cpp:444`): Whole file rewritten each save; traffic flag set only when no other file supplies traffic. On success g_ed.saved = file, draft closed, mass undo cleared.
- **Status 'Not saved: ...'** (action, `routeedit.cpp:619`): The draft stays open; nothing is written.
- **Cancel: drop draft, restore saved file and groups** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1006`): g_ed.file = g_ed.saved; picks and mass undo cleared; others reloaded.
- **Permanent Delete: remove the shared copy** (action, `routeedit.cpp:1005`): Refused if the playthrough changed since opening.
- **Tell FVO Traffic to re-read its files** (action, `routeedit.cpp:459`): Called by every successful WriteAtomic; the addon no longer polls the folder. Vehicles finish their lap and land before changing route.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficpool::ReloadFiles: FVO Traffic re-reads all *.ini (PreferEditor: editor routes replace originals by name)
- **Route groups changed? Write VehicleGroups.ini** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:941`): Same atomic write; failure reports 'Routes saved, but not the route groups'.
- **X: player in combat? Else restart traffic on active lanes** (decision, `routeedit.cpp:2207` · log `OBS system=traffic.reset`): Refused with a draft open or in combat. Places = per active route its scheduled count spread along lanes, routes interleaved; secretunpause runs the world behind the panel; trafficpool::Rush.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: trafficpool::Rush / RushStatus cold start; secretunpause runs world behind the panel
- **Loading panel until the restart is done (no other input)** (state, `routeedit.cpp:6037` · log `OBS system=traffic.reset`): Blocks F8 / close and all tools; notes lane-too-narrow blocks.
- **Committed (or refused with a status line)** (state, `routeedit.cpp:619`): Stage exit. LoadOthers runs again so the grey list matches disk.

Arrows: commit → validate (Save); commit → permanent (Permanent Save (draft ensured)); commit → cancel (Cancel); commit → test (Test); commit → delete (Delete route); commit → permdelete (Permanent Delete); commit → startswap (X); permanent → validate (same checks first); validate → notsaved (issue); validate → foreign (valid (or no draft)); foreign → notsaved (profile changed or unsigned file); foreign → unshift (ok); unshift → permwrite (permanent); unshift → writeatomic (normal save); permwrite → writeatomic (route removed from local file); permwrite → notsaved (shared write or refresh failed); writeatomic → reload (replaced); writeatomic → notsaved (tmp / backup / replace failed); reload → groups; groups → s6out (unchanged, or written); notsaved → s6out (draft kept); cancel → s6out; test → validate (test=1 then Save); delete → validate (confirmed: saves at once); delete → cancel (only an unsaved new draft); delete → s6out (first press: ask to confirm); permdelete → reload (shared copy erased); startswap → s6out (draft open or in combat: refused); startswap → loading (lane places sent to Rush); loading → s6out (RushStatus done)

## E7 Route data model and placement data (read by C, D, F)  `#flow-e-s7`  (Detailed flow · E Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements)

Stage 7 of 7 in E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements.

- **Save's working traffic profile folder (FVO_Traffic routes)** (state, `routeedit.cpp:430`): Data store read by other steps. Per game save: an immutable snapshot id stored in a GLOB, cloned into a Working copy the editor writes; the game save clones it again (BeforeSave). Reloading a save discards unsaved route changes.
- **Editor_<world>.ini (editor-owned traffic file)** (state, `routefiles.h:187`): Data store read by other steps. One per world, [world.ed_*] section, signed; routes, landings, spawns, zones, links, lane defaults, restrictions, schedule lines. An ordinary traffic file the addon reads like any other.
- **Other traffic files + ringroad.ini (never written by the editor)** (state, `routeedit.cpp:754`): Data store read by other steps. Hand-written and addon files; shown grey and read-only.
- **VehicleGroups.ini (same on every planet)** (state, `routefiles.h:162`): Data store read by other steps. Route vehicle groups.
- **Shared Permanent routes store (TrafficProfiles/Permanent/routes)** (state, `permanentroutes.h:5`): Data store read by other steps. Owned entries carry the trafficpermanent signature plus their worlds, anchors and groups; synced into each working copy.

## F1 Sample input and situation  `#flow-f-s1`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Once per vehicle update (main_vehicle.cpp Update; housekeeping frames run bindings::Tick(false, worldSafe) only), bindings::Tick reads bindings.ini, follows the game's own ControlMap for 'default' lines, polls the XInput pad, picks the one situation the player is in (editor, scanner, passenger, VTOL, hover, ground, foot) and decides whether FVO keys are live at all (world present, no menu input context on the stack, not suppressed after a reload, not capturing a rebind). Live actions are resolved per alternative into raw / tap / hold / double gestures, batched as Triggered/Started events for the gameplay modules, and the keys and pad buttons the mod owns are switched off for the game through a hook on the game's input-event resolver. The same area owns the rebinding paths (Gameplay Options capture and the native Controls page hooks) with a conflict check, the settings.ini feature switches, the HUD (Scaleform work inside the HUD movie's Advance: status card, inline prompts, route panel, declutter, layout editor, notices), the per-frame vehicle list (world::Publish), the ray/site checks (environment), the ship hull clone, the vehicle chain probe and repairs (vehiclediag), and the separate InteractiveCover test DLL. Next: Gate: are FVO keys live, who owns each press. Oddities found when mapping: 1) Two key systems: bindings.ini (bindings.cpp, gated by the input gate) and the older controlmap Claim/Note table with raw GetAsyncKeyState test keys (flight bank test, navprobe, npcveh debug keys, gated only by [keys] debug=1); also the far mover probe (Ctrl+Shift+F11) and InteractiveCover's ' key read keys directly, bypassing the menu-context gate. 2) Dead or stale code: profiles.cpp ChooseKeys/Pressed/g_npcKey/g_ownKey (bindings own F9 / ]), shipvehicle ChooseKey/g_toggle/g_keyDown (ShipPreview binding is used), keygesture::Input 1 s timer and altcontrol drop (gestures now decided by the binding layer at 0.5 s; targetinput still uses its own 300 ms / 1000 ms timers, so scanner hold is 1 s while bound holds are 0.5 s), features.cpp kPlanDefault unused, readout::Tick / TrackPlayerMarker / OnRecentre never called (only SetPlayerMarker), bindings::Assign's 'moved' string always empty, bindlogic::Held template repeats the InCtx / gesture checks after the vk branch and is not called. 3) controlspage.h comment says Assign 'moves the input off any other of our actions' but Assign now refuses conflicts (notice 'Binding unchanged'). 4) The bindings.ini template header says 'Flying Vehicle Overhaul 0.73.0' and hoverui.h says F7 toggles the HUD editor, while kDefs bind hud_edit to F7 (0x76) but routeedit owns F7 on the map; profiles.h still documents '[' as the NPC key. 5) vehiclediag is named a diagnostic but owns live repairs (shadow pending list, birth holds, static-to-dynamic setup, launch guard, after-load attach, put-back of dropped cars): its owner looks like A (lifecycle). 6) drivecontrols.h has no key layer (types for the energy plan, area C). 7) Stages s6 (HUD, render thread) and s4 (rebinding, settings movie thread) are entered from the HUD/settings movie Advance, not from the vehicle update, so they have their own way in; the InteractiveCover DLL is a separate plugin with its own task. 8) hoverui.cpp is counted here as the HUD owner; panelpaging.h and strokefont.h are used only by routeedit (E) and resources.cpp is a diagnostic readout (A), so they are not listed here.

- **Vehicle frame task: features::Tick then bindings::Tick** (state, `main_vehicle.cpp:315`): Stage exit. Full updates call features::Tick(); bindings::Tick(); before world::Publish and the flight tick, and bindings::EndUpdate() at the end. Housekeeping frames (paused/loading) call bindings::Tick(false, worldSafe): samples kept, no events published, no game reads without a world.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: main_vehicle.cpp Update / housekeeping schedule calls features::Tick, bindings::Tick, world::Publish, environment::Tick, profiles::Tick, vehiclediag::Tick, shipvehicle::Tick, secretunpause::Tick
- **bindings.ini missing, changed on disk, or layout < 4?** (decision, `bindings.cpp:160`): Checked every 1 s by file time. Missing file: a template with every default is written. [meta] layout < 4: backup .before-layout4, lines still at 'default' or at their layout-2/3 text take the new default once; edited lines are kept. Unknown words are skipped and logged; a reload shows a notice and suppresses input until bound keys are up.
- **bindings.ini + kDefs: 53 actions, alternatives, context, gesture** (state, `bindingdefs.h:29`): Data store read by other steps. One row per Action (Scanner..Cruise): ini key, default spec (e.g. 'default-key:tap@foot|scanner, Pad:Y:tap@scanner'), default VK, optional game context/event to follow, and Layer (Gameplay, Target, Placement, Editor). Syntax parsed by bindlogic::Parse; [display] pad_names auto/xbox/playstation.
- **Follow the game's own key / pad for 'default' (every 5 s)** (action, `bindings.cpp:216`): For actions with a gameContext (Vehicle:VehicleBoost/Accel/Brake/Turn*, VehicleExit, MainGameplay:Activate) the keyboard VK and pad bit come from controlmap::KeyFor / PadFor, so rebinding in the game's own settings is followed. Logs once which game actions share the mod's pad buttons.
- **Game ControlMap: 78 contexts, keyboard/mouse/pad events** (state, `controlmap.cpp:228`): Data store read by other steps. Read from DAT_1461DE668 (40-byte entries), re-read every 5 s, indexed by (context, event) in controlindex::Index. KeyFree/KeyFreeIn/WhoUses answer whether the game binds a key; Dump writes Logs\SeamlessPlanets\controlmap.txt.
- **Poll XInput pad (8 ms), sticks and triggers** (action, `bindings.cpp:241`): xinput1_4/1_3/9_1_0 loaded on first use; empty slots rescanned every 2 s. Buttons plus triggers (>=100/255) and stick directions (>=16000) become bindlogic bits; analog axes kept for Amount (deadzones 0.24 stick, 0.12 trigger).
- **Name pad buttons Xbox or PlayStation** (action, `bindings.cpp:291`): Every 5 s while a pad is connected, HID game controllers are listed; Sony vendor 0x054C gives PlayStation names unless [display] pad_names overrides.
- **Fresh press from the other device? switch prompt device** (decision, `bindlogic.h:586`): Prompts name the pad or keyboard only after a fresh button press from that device (the input-gate hook's ordered press stamp wins; mouse moves and held buttons never switch). A change bumps the bindings generation so HUD labels redraw.
- **Situation: editor, scanner, passenger, VTOL, hover, ground or foot?** (decision, `bindings.cpp:321`): Order: HUD editor, scanner open, riding as passenger (with passenger control: ground or hover by the vehicle's mode), driven vehicle with physical camera = VTOL, vehicleflight::Active = hover, else ground; no vehicle = foot. With no world it is forced to foot. Each alternative's @context mask is tested against this one bit.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: reads vehicleflight::Active, PhysicalCameraActive, DriveFor and passenger::Controlling to pick the context
- **Sample ready (context, keys, pad, axes)** (state, `commandinput.h:10`): Stage exit. The per-frame sample handed to the gate: 256 key states, pad bits, axes, context, driving / placement flags.

Arrows: frame → loadIni (every update); loadIni → bindData (parse lines / write template / upgrade layout); loadIni → gameDefaults (loaded, unchanged); gameDefaults → controlMap (KeyFor / PadFor every 5 s); gameDefaults → pollPad; pollPad → padStyle (pad connected); pollPad → device (fresh key / pad press); padStyle → device; device → situation (prompt device decided); situation → s1out (context bit)

## F2 Gate: are FVO keys live, who owns each press  `#flow-f-s2`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Stage 2 of 8 in F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics. Next: Actions handed to consumers.

- **World present (not loading, not main menu)?** (decision, `bindings.cpp:387`): 2026-11-15 hotfix: with world=false the player and vehicle are not read, FVO input is off and no key is taken from the game.
- **Any menu input context on the game's stack (or unfocused)?** (decision, `inputownership.h:47`): inputgate reads the ControlMap context stack (+0x2A8 count, +0x2B0 entries). 0x4E StopContextProcessing or 0x11 Console blocks gameplay; any listed menu id (ship builder, maps, settings, terminals, photo mode, cursor 0x0D...) gives every key back to the game. 0x01, 0x06, 0x23, 0x3A are not menus. Unreadable or game not in front = not allowed.
- **Suppressed after a reload / capture until bound keys are up?** (decision, `bindings.cpp:368`): After Load, ResetRuntime or a capture, input stays off until no FVO-bound key and no pad button is down, so a release of an earlier press cannot fire an action. Unrelated stuck keys do not block.
- **Action available here (driving, placement layer)?** (decision, `commandinput.h:19`): While driving, Summon, Cover, CrewDrive, RideAlong and Placement-layer actions are off. Layer Placement only while recording placements, Target only while not. Editor-layer HUD keys only count in the editor context.
- **Resolve raw / tap / hold 0.5 s / double 0.3 s per alternative** (action, `commandinput.h:38`): Per alternative a GestureState; chord modifiers (Pad:LB+X) keep plain bindings of other buttons quiet; a double-bound input delays its tap to tell a double. A context or allowed change cancels gestures and requires a fresh press.
- **Batch Triggered / Started until the gameplay batch consumes** (action, `inputevents.h:10`): Samples accumulate pending events; Publish only on gameplay frames; EndUpdate clears. A context change or not-allowed discards stale events instead of replaying them.
- **Mark keys / pad bits the mod owns now** (action, `bindings.cpp:387`): For every live action alternative in context: its VK and pad bit are blocked from the game unless they follow the game's own binding for that action (Boost on RB counts as native; DriverExit is not native while hovering). Riding as passenger also blocks W A S D, Space and Shift.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: passenger::RidingVehicle blocks WASD/Space/Shift for the game
- **Game input queue: owned press gets block reason 4** (action, `inputgate.cpp:58`): E8 redirect of the resolver call at 0x022DAC60 (-> FUN_1422F1840). After resolving, a press the mod owns gets +0x34 = 4 so no game handler matches. inputownership::Own keeps ownership through the release even if menus or modes change; a native block reason is never replaced. Also stamps fresh device presses and accumulates look-mouse deltas.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: inputgate::Install E8 redirect via hook::RedirectCall at startup (main_vehicle.cpp)
- **Route editor, photo editor or loading panel holding input?** (decision, `inputgate.cpp:223`): Each call holds 300 ms. Map editor: keyboard except Tab/Esc/WASD/arrows, left mouse, D-pad and face/shoulder pad buttons blocked; HoldMapAll also Tab/Esc; WheelForEditor counts wheel notches. Photo editor (photoeditorinput::Block): only camera movement reaches photo mode, F8 and LB/LS/RS always blocked.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: routeedit calls HoldMapForEditor / HoldMapAll / WheelForEditor / TakeWheel; photo editor calls PhotoEditor / TakePhotoMouse
- **Gate state traced on change (live or why not)** (state, `bindings.cpp:387` · log `OBS system=input.gate`): Reason: game_not_in_front, a_game_menu_or_input_context_holds_gameplay, waiting_for_fvo_keys_and_buttons_up_after_a_reload, capturing_a_new_binding or fvo_keys_live. inputgate also logs each new context stack (first 400).
- **Live actions published for this update** (state, `bindings.h:25`): Stage exit. Allowed() = gate on, not suppressed, not capturing. Everything below reads through Triggered / Started / Held / Amount.

Arrows: worldUp → modKeys (world present); worldUp → gateTrace (no world: not allowed, nothing taken); modKeys → suppress (no menu context, game in front); modKeys → gateTrace (menu context: keys given back); suppress → available (not suppressed, not capturing); suppress → gateTrace (suppressed or capturing); available → gesture (per action available here); gesture → events (triggered / started per action); events → ownKeys; ownKeys → gateHook (SetBlocked pad bits + SetBlockedKeys); gateHook → mapHold (each queued game input event); mapHold → gateHook (held: block per editor rule); mapHold → gateTrace (not held: ownership rule applied); gateTrace → s2out

## F3 Actions handed to consumers  `#flow-f-s3`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Stage 3 of 8 in F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics. Next: Rebinding (Gameplay Options and native Controls page).

- **Modules ask Triggered / Held / Amount / Label** (action, `bindings.cpp:513`): Held returns any gesture-on alternative; Amount gives 0..1 analog for sticks/triggers; Label / HudSnapshot / PromptInput name the input for the device in use ('Scanner > Y' on foot when only the scanner binds it). GameHeld/GameLabel read the game's own actions.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicleflight reads Held / Amount / Triggered for Accelerate, Brake, Turn, Lift, Boost, Gear, Hover, ThrottleLock, FlightAssist, AutoToggle, Cruise
- **NpcProfile (F9) or OwnProfile (]) triggered?** (decision, `profiles.cpp:405`): Runs every full update after the surface checks. No player = nothing.
- **Driving own vehicle? cycle autopilot end mode, else remote profile** (decision, `profiles.cpp:355`): No own vehicle: notice 'You own no vehicle'. Driving it: Stop -> Orbit -> Patrol -> Cruise (vcontract kCruise) -> Stop, each with a notice. On foot: default -> Scout -> Return home -> Hold assigned at the vehicle's position.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: vehicleflight::SetAutoMode Stop/Orbit/Patrol and vcontract Cruise profile for the autopilot
- **Next NPC profile for every piloted follower vehicle** (action, `profiles.cpp:337`): Cycle default, patrol, hauler, courier, hold, return home; assigned via vcontract to every world vehicle with an npcvehicles pilot. Log only, no notice.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: assigns profiles to vehicles with npcvehicles::PilotFor
- **Registered profiles: 8 navigation, 2 combat, 4 weapons** (state, `profiles.cpp:397`): Data store read by other steps. escort, hold, return home, patrol, hauler, courier, scout, cruise (each with a Goal function); attack, defend; free fire, opportunistic, hold fire, never. Reset on save load clears vcontract.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: Goal functions (escort, hauler, courier, scout, cruise, patrol, hold, return home) feed the vehicle contract / plan
- **Weapons gate and follower status text rules** (state, `profileclass.h:20`): Data store read by other steps. Player seat never fires by itself; Recall forces hold fire, Cover turns free fire into opportunistic; never stays never. DefaultCombat: patrol/hold attack, others defend. State() builds 'NAV · COMBAT · HOLD FIRE' row text.
- **ShipPreview (F10) triggered while in a vehicle?** (decision, `shipvehicle.cpp:48`): Toggles the visual-only hull preview; shown only while vehicleflight::Active (hovering). Leaving or changing vehicle turns it off. Also checks every 500 ms whether the player sits in a ship pilot seat.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: preview only while vehicleflight::Active (hover)
- **Gear tap -> next gear 1..4** (action, `altcontrol.h:29`): vehicleflight feeds bindings::Triggered(Gear) into altcontrol::Action; the old Alt hold (300 ms drop) state machine is still present but the drop is always false now.
- **N / U: tap Summon or Cover, hold Recall or Follower drives** (action, `keygesture.h:7`): vehicleflight builds keygesture::Action from Triggered(Summon/RecallEscorts) and Triggered(Cover/CrewDrive). keygesture::Input's own 1 s hold timer is no longer used: the binding layer decides tap vs hold (0.5 s).
- **Scanner T: tap select, double clear, hold guard/ram** (action, `targetinput.h:13`): Tap reported only after the 300 ms double window; hold at 1000 ms; capture marks the press whose aimed selection the later tap uses. Used by scantarget.cpp.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: scantarget.cpp T gestures, SetRoute, SetPlayerMarker and placement-layer actions
- **settings.ini [keys] debug=1? claim test keys** (decision, `controlmap.cpp:334`): Re-read every 5 s. Test/debug keys (NPC board ; / [, exit Insert, navprobe / . ', flight bank test ,) exist only then; they are claimed with Claim/ClaimFirst only if the game binds nothing there and no FVO owner has the key.
- **Key claim table (owner, action, VK)** (state, `controlmap.cpp:292`): Data store read by other steps. A second, older key registry beside bindings.ini: Claim / ClaimFirst / Note record raw GetAsyncKeyState keys of flight, navprobe and npcveh; LogTable prints it (context.cpp).
- **Actions consumed for this update** (state, `bindings.cpp:512`): Stage exit. End of the gameplay event batch; events not consumed are dropped.

Arrows: query → profileKeys (profiles::Tick); profileKeys → cycleOwn (OwnProfile); profileKeys → cycleNpc (NpcProfile); cycleOwn → profileReg (vcontract::Assign); cycleNpc → profileReg (vcontract::Assign); profileReg → profileClass (combat / weapons class rules); profileKeys → shipToggle (next consumer); shipToggle → gearAlt (next consumer (vehicleflight)); gearAlt → summonGest; summonGest → scanGest; scanGest → debugKeys; debugKeys → claimTable (debug=1: Claim / ClaimFirst); debugKeys → s3out (debug=0: no test keys); claimTable → s3out

## F4 Rebinding (Gameplay Options and native Controls page)  `#flow-f-s4`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Stage 4 of 8 in F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics. Next: Settings files and feature switches.

- **Gameplay Options: FVO checkboxes and control rows** (action, `hoverui.cpp:1361`): Runs on the settings movie thread while SettingsPanel is visible: our feature checkboxes (features::InMenu) and one control row per action/device appended when generations change; selecting a control starts BeginCapture. Hiding the panel cancels the capture.
- **Capture: key, pad, chord, Esc cancel, Backspace default, Delete clear?** (decision, `bindlogic.h:100`): Armed only after every input is up. First pad button + a second = chord (modifier first). 20 s with nothing = give up. Losing focus cancels. Result goes to Assign / ClearSlot / RestoreDevice.
- **Native Controls page remap: our rows answered by FVO** (action, `controlspage.cpp:68`): Hooks FUN_1422EEA90 (validate) and FUN_1422EF4C0 (apply) for FVO_* event names only, plus translator vtable slot 2 for '$FlyingVehicleOverhaul...' labels. Rows use context id 0x49 (Vehicle). Validate answers free only if CanAssign; apply calls Assign.
- **Overlaps another action in a shared context and gesture?** (decision, `commandinput.h:123`): Same device input, overlapping @context masks, same gesture (or either raw) = conflict. Target and Placement layers never overlap; Boost vs ThrottleLock is allowed (hold vs double).
- **Notice: 'Binding unchanged: conflicts with X'** (action, `bindings.cpp:684`): Nothing is moved off the other action; the slot keeps its old input.
- **Restore defaults for the device in use** (action, `bindings.cpp:614`): commandinput::RestoreDevice keeps the other device's alternatives and puts back this device's defaults for every action.
- **Write the changed bindings.ini lines and reload** (action, `bindings.cpp:644`): Only lines whose spec changed are written (WritePrivateProfileStringW), then Load(false): input suppressed until keys are up, generation bumped for HUD and menus.
- **Bindings changed (generation bumped)** (state, `bindings.cpp:545`): Stage exit. HUD, Controls page and settings rows rebuild when the generation changes.

Arrows: settingsPage → capture (control row selected: BeginCapture); settingsPage → controlsPage (Controls page shown with our rows); capture → conflict (key / pad / chord); capture → save (Delete clear / Backspace default); capture → s4out (Esc, timeout or focus lost: cancelled); controlsPage → conflict (validate / apply for FVO_* event); conflict → refuse (overlap); conflict → save (free); refuse → s4out; settingsPage → resetAll (Restore defaults); resetAll → save; save → s4out

## F5 Settings files and feature switches  `#flow-f-s5`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Stage 5 of 8 in F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics. Next: HUD and notices.

- **FVO folder: Logs\FlyingVehicleOverhaul (migrates SeamlessVehicle)** (state, `fvopaths.h:14`): Data store read by other steps. First call moves Logs\SeamlessVehicle to Logs\FlyingVehicleOverhaul and renames seamless.ini to settings.ini; if the move fails the old folder stays in use. Holds settings.ini, bindings.ini, hoverui.ini.
- **Installer choices (Data\SFSE\Plugins\...features) changed?** (decision, `featureinstaller.h:25`): Checked once per session. mode.ini [installer] enabled=1: per-key fragment files are imported into settings.ini only when the chosen value changed (ledger); a page not shown installs nothing. Failure keeps existing settings.
- **settings.ini feature switches (reloaded every 5 s)** (state, `features.cpp:111`): Data store read by other steps. Defaults: NPC vehicles on, traffic on, route markers on, wall pass on, radar on, flight assist on; free roll, physical VTOL, physical AI, ground planner, verbose log off. Set() saves at once. Read from any thread via cached atomics.
- **Feature always on, retired, or shown in the menu?** (decision, `features.h:39`): AlwaysOn: combat AI, hover-or-drive, damage, health bars, status card, witnesses, ground planner (an old 0 is ignored). Placement and PlacementMode can never be on (keys kept). WallPass, Radar, PhysicalAi, DebugLog are settings.ini only.
- **Settings published to all systems** (state, `features.h:65`): Stage exit. Generation changes whenever any switch does; the settings page redraws.

Arrows: paths → featureInstaller (first Reload); featureInstaller → features (changed choices imported / unchanged); features → featureGate (every 5 s when file time or size changed); featureGate → s5out

## F6 HUD and notices  `#flow-f-s6`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Stage 6 of 8 in F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics. Next: Vehicle list, environment checks, ship hull.

- **HUD movie Advance hook runs FVO HUD work** (action, `hoverui.cpp:946`): Vtable patches: HUDMenu update, MovieImpl Advance and destructor (without it no native buttons: a stale menu would crash the next load), MonocleMenu message. All Scaleform work runs inside the movie's own Advance. An exception disables the HUD until the next load.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: hoverui::Install vtable patches at startup; controlspage::Install from InstallSettings
- **What modules pushed: vehicle, gear, trip, lifebar, rows, route** (state, `hoverui.h:21`): Data store read by other steps. Main thread setters (SetState, SetFoot, SetOccupied, SetTripStatus, SetLifebar, SetPlanLine, SetVehicleBar, SetRows, SetRoute, SetScan, SetTargetHints) store values the HUD thread reads.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicleflight pushes SetState, SetTripStatus, SetLifebar, SetPlanLine, SetRows, SetFoot, SetAutoKey each frame
- **HudEdit pressed (not on the local map or photo mode)?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:2899`): Called by vehicleflight each frame. On the F7 map or in photo mode the key is drained for routeedit. Edit on: Page Up/Down pick, arrows move (repeat 40 ms after 350 ms), +/- size 5%, Delete reset, Home/End spacing; off saves hoverui.ini with a notice.
  - hands off to B · Player vehicle control: drive, hover, VTOL, grip, camera, save: vehicleflight calls hoverui::EditTick(focused) every frame
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: yields HudEdit to routeedit while the F7 map or photo mode is open
- **ShowHints (J) toggles every hint and panel** (action, `hoverui.cpp:2899`): Flips show_all, saves hoverui.ini, notice 'Contextual hints shown' / 'Hints: only what you need now'.
- **Notice in the HUD notification area (or log only)** (action, `notify.cpp:59`): Sends the same event Papyrus Debug.Notification sends (main thread). notify::Log keeps diagnostics out of the screen (verbose log only).
- **HUD layout: hoverui.ini over built-in defaults** (state, `huddefaults.h:8`): Data store read by other steps. Built-in layout from the user's 2026-10-28 save is read first, then hoverui.ini overrides; hudchildren (card/route/action child offsets, separate scanner copies) and hudprompts (per-action prompt placement) lines; rides and show_all counters live there too.
- **Show basic controls? show_all, editing, first 3 rides, 10 s after boarding** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:223`): show_all defaults to true (every hint shown). After the 4th ride a notice says hints now hide after 10 s and names the ShowHints key.
- **Build inline prompts for what works now** (action, `inlinehints.h:37`): From bindings::HudSnapshot (inputs resolved for the device and context): Summon/Follow on foot when you own a vehicle, Hover/Gear/Auto driving, Passenger seat facing an occupied vehicle; with hints on also scanner Destination/Guard/Focus/Clear route, Recall, Descend, profiles, Cruise. Inputs shared by two meanings group under one key.
- **Status card on? fill heading, health, trip, escort lines** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:2029`): 8 card lines (foot and car layouts); the plan ledger line fills the last empty line in test builds. Empty card when features StatusCard is off (always on now) unless editing. Also the vehicle bar under the enemy bar.
- **Scanner (MonocleMenu) open in the last 300 ms?** (decision, `hoverui.cpp:2404`): Shows the call/follow buttons, status box (SUMMONING / FOLLOWING), Target text and the scanner copy of the route panel; captures the selected map marker's name and distance (scannercontext::RangeMeters) for 200 ms.
- **Push overlapping HUD text apart, keep it on screen** (action, `hudspread.h:31`): Overlapping boxes pushed along the smaller axis (gap 4, 16 iterations, max 160 px), split between movable boxes, screen edge has the last word; eased each frame. Off while editing.
- **Place the player's custom map marker at a point** (action, `notify.cpp:176`): The game's own FUN_141A43220 in the current planet world; used by scantarget for destinations. False when no planet world.
- **HUD frame drawn** (state, `hoverui.cpp:946`): Stage exit. End of the HUD movie's Advance work.

Arrows: hudAdvance → hudState (read pushed state); hudState → layout (placements); layout → basics; basics → hints (visible: basics + context prompts); basics → card (hidden: only the prompts that work now); hints → card; card → scanner; scanner → declutter (open: scanner panel set / closed: HUD set); declutter → s6out; hudEdit → showHints (ShowHints triggered); hudEdit → layout (edit on/off, moves, sizes saved to hoverui.ini); showHints → notify (hints notice); notify → s6out; marker → s6out

## F7 Vehicle list, environment checks, ship hull  `#flow-f-s7`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Stage 7 of 8 in F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics. Next: Diagnostics, health views, test DLL.

- **Publish the vehicle list (budgeted 3D / body re-reads)** (action, `vehicleworld.cpp:251` · log `OBS system=world.streaming`): Every keyword-gated vehicle in the attached cells plus Keep-in-sight held ones within 1000 m (kHeldReach), up to fvolimits::kVehicles. New or changed-3D vehicles are read every frame; idle ones only while their measured cost fits [world] sampleBudgetMs (default 0.5 ms), oldest first.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: world::SetServices providers from realvehicles / npcvehicles; MarkOutOfTraffic from the traffic coordinator
- **Player, NPC or parked? out of traffic?** (decision, `vehicleworld.cpp:251` · log `OBS system=world.who`): Driver lookups are rate-limited per frame; the player's own form = Player, a driver or in-use = Npc, else Parked (new vehicles read parked until their turn). MarkOutOfTraffic from the coordinator lasts kOutOfTrafficMs.
- **Fresh pilot body velocity? else smoothed position difference** (decision, `vehicleworld.cpp:576` · log `OBS system=world.velocity`): VL-1: a pilot's own body velocity is used while fresh (kFlownFreshMs); position jumps with implausible implied speed are ignored (vehiclevel).
- **Vehicle snapshot: pos, vel, rot, half extents, who, bodies** (state, `vehicleworld.h:50`): Data store read by other steps. Read by any thread as a synchronized copy (Vehicles excludes held-far ones, VehiclesAndHeld includes them). FirstInPath answers cylinder sweeps against it.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: world::Vehicles / FirstInPath read by the plan and radar; NoteFlownVelocity written by pilots
- **Run the ray queue: 16 ray slots, 256 cached queries** (action, `environment.cpp:124` · log `OBS system=sensing.queue`): Rays asked by sensing, spawn and combat are started in prefetch ray slots, answered next frames, cached (default ttl 1 s, ground 30 s); unasked queries dropped after 2 s. Pending() lets the ground sensing keep below a share of the queue.
- **Ray answer cached and fresh?** (decision, `environment.cpp:39` · log `OBS system=sensing.ray`): Yes: answer at once. No: queue (or replace the most idle query when 256 are held) and the caller asks again next frame.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: GroundAt / SurfaceAhead / RayHit / LineBlocked answer the planner's sensing; MappedSite reads navmap and cellbake
- **Site check: dry level support, headroom, clearance?** (decision, `environment.cpp:150` · log `OBS system=spawn.support`): Verdict Pending / Safe / Unsafe with a reason: water or normal z < 0.7 = 'No dry, level support', slope/edge, vehicle clearance, ceiling, another vehicle on the site. SpawnSite / AirSite / MappedSite add the nav map and baked terrain (under the ground, inside a building).
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: SpawnSite / AirSite / CheckSite / BoardingSite / ClearShot / CombatPosition for traffic spawn, boarding and combat
- **Vehicle at rest on the ground for entry / exit?** (decision, `environment.cpp:349`): Entry Safe once standing (sitelogic::Standing) or at <= 0.75 m/s on uneven ground; otherwise 'Vehicle must stop and descend before boarding'. BoardingSite also needs a usable boarding side.
- **Car control channels and limits (types only, no keys)** (state, `drivecontrols.h:38`): Data store read by other steps. Mode Drive/Hover, Permit, Phase, Shape, Limits (drive 20 / hover 26 m/s, max slope 0.62, step 1.1 m). The energy plan writes the Output; no key layer lives here.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: drive:: types the energy plan writes
- **Clone home ship 3D and show it on the driven vehicle** (action, `shipmodel.cpp:819`): Runs in the game tick. NiCloningProcess clone stripped of collision, attached to the vehicle (or player) root; the vehicle's own visible parts hidden; real ship and chassis untouched. Load reset forgets everything.
- **No clone yet and 5 s since last try? capture the hull** (decision, `shipmodel.cpp:819`): Only with SP_FEATURE_VEHICLE; must happen while the landed exterior's modules are loaded (interior refuses). Missing 3D logs 'nothing to copy yet' and marks Unavailable when wanted.
- **World list, ray answers and hull published** (state, `vehicleworld.cpp:502`): Stage exit. Consumers in B, C and D read the snapshot and ask environment sites.

Arrows: publish → who (per listed vehicle); who → velocity; velocity → snapshot (published copy); snapshot → envTick (environment::Tick later in the update); envTick → rayCache (each ask); rayCache → site (site checks use answered rays); site → entry (boarding / entry); entry → driveTypes; driveTypes → shipModel (game tick); shipModel → autoCapture (no clone); autoCapture → s7out (captured / not loaded); shipModel → s7out (clone placed)

## F8 Diagnostics, health views, test DLL  `#flow-f-s8`  (Detailed flow · F Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics)

Stage 8 of 8 in F · Input, HUD, settings, world list, environment, ships, diagnostics.

- **Vehicle chain probe every 5 s (component, instance, steps)** (action, `vehiclediag.cpp:1037`): Per nearby vehicle: component update count, Havok instance, physics step count (NoteStep from the step hook), player flag, freeze on exit, moved metres; the first link reading 0 is the broken one. Also retries the native full attach for vehicles with 3D but no component.
- **Shadow the game's 8-entry vehicle pending list** (action, `vehiclediag.cpp:795` · log `OBS system=chassis.pending`): When the game's list is full and wiped, FVO's unbounded list keeps every loading vehicle so each gets its attach (root cause of no physics). Off if signatures differ.
  - hands off to A · Boot, hooks, lifecycle, engine interface and diagnostics: vehiclediag hooks (pending list, build, motion type, component update) and load hooks NoteBeforeLoad / AfterLoadTick / PrepareForDataLoad
- **Trace every native physics-vehicle build; put back a dropped car** (action, `vehiclediag.cpp:795` · log `OBS system=chassis.build`): Build begin/end with static check result; a vehicle that went down more than ~2 m around its build is put back; static chassis set dynamic for the native setup; launch guard clamps parked launches; wreck glide.
- **Birth hold: flight controller armed and physics built? release** (decision, `vehiclediag.cpp:910` · log `OBS system=chassis.birth-handoff`): StageBirth holds a new vehicle before its chassis attaches; ReleaseBirth once its pilot is armed and the game built its physics vehicle; ReleaseBirthToPlayer when the player takes it; DropBirthHold when hidden or deleted.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: StageBirth / ReleaseBirth for traffic births; Built / BuiltAt read by spawn health
- **After a load: native attach for vehicles kept without a component** (action, `vehiclediag.cpp:954`): NoteBeforeLoad records each vehicle's reference, cell and form flags; from the load's return to the first live frame, a vehicle kept in memory without a component gets FUN_141314780.
- **Spot test rules: tiny hidden pulses check a new car answers** (decision, `spottest.h:146` · log `OBS system=spot.motion`): Pure. Once per spawned/arrived car while hidden, after 20 settled steps: 4-step pulses (~1.3 cm) per direction; 0.004 m = answered; two failures 3 s apart = fail; off lane > 2 m or up z < 0.5 = misplaced. The factory diagnostic records stronger evidence alongside this verdict: a matching Plan pulse, a fresh step and motion along the requested direction on the same chassis build.
  - hands off to C · Autopilot decisions: spottest::Step run inside vehicleflight's plan frame (planframe spottest::Ask)
- **Name the addon's lane-hold reason bits** (state, `heldwhy.h:7`): Data store read by other steps. Bit 30 set = reasons given (junction right of way, stop line, following, pickup, connection blocked, zone...). An older addon's 1 reads 'route hold (reason not given)'.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: decodes the FVO_Traffic addon's lane held bits
- **Ctrl+Shift+F11 in a test build? run the far mover probe** (decision, `shipmodel.cpp:647` · log `OBS system=far.mover`): Not FVO_RELEASE only. Clones the player's rover and flies the copy on an arc ([diagnostics] farMoverDistance), measuring update cost and pose error; refused when paused, not exterior or unstable.
- **Start/Swap: lift the map pause? refused in combat** (decision, `secretunpause.cpp:100`): Lifts SetMenuPausesGame for open pausing menus so traffic seats behind the cover panel; ends on combat, a closed map or the 20 s cap (pause always returns). Notices for combat refusal and 'Combat over' prompt. Describe() is a read-only pause readout.
  - hands off to E · Route editing, roads/lanes data, F7 dock, placements: route editor Start/Swap panel uses secretunpause Begin / End
- **Camera visibility forecast from look mouse deltas** (state, `camera_visibility.h:52`): Data store read by other steps. Pure rules: mouse turn rate from inputgate::LookMouse, a projected view cone (fringe 12 deg, 350 m) used by Keep in sight and the traffic addon to tell if a spot will be seen.
  - hands off to D · NPC vehicles, traffic population, combat, passengers, boarding: vehiclekeep and the traffic addon generator use the camera forecast
- **Diagnostics done for this frame** (state, `vehiclediag.h:14`): Stage exit. Area F's work for the update is complete.
- **InteractiveCover DLL: own SFSE frame task** (state, `main_cover.cpp:64`): Stage exit. Separate plugin. Starts on PostDataLoad (hooks SlideStart, adds a permanent task running covertest::Tick); PreLoadGame resets. Test stage only.
- **' pressed? run the next of 4 cover tests and restore** (decision, `cover_test.cpp:218`): Raw GetAsyncKeyState(VK_OEM_7): crouch 3 s, walk 1 s, sprint + slide 2.5 s (logs SlideStart), console ActionEnterCover; each restores what it changed and shows a notice.

Arrows: vdiag → pending (hooked list); pending → build (attach -> build); build → birth (new vehicle staged); birth → afterLoad (during a save load); afterLoad → spot (cars released: spot test when hidden); spot → heldwhy (health views name holds); heldwhy → farProbe (test builds); farProbe → unpause (secretunpause::Tick when world safe); unpause → camvis; camvis → end; cover → coverTest (every frame)
